### [1] [NAVI-Orbital: First In-Orbit Demonstration of a Zero-Shot Vision-Language Model for Autonomous Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18271)
*Juan Manuel Delfa Victoria,Taran Cyriac John,Andrew W. Herson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NAVI-Orbital系统，该系统于2026年4月16日在低地球轨道卫星上首次实现了视觉-语言模型的全自主在轨多模态推理，通过语义压缩将地球观测数据转化为文本描述，从而逆转传统"先采集再下传"的带宽模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测数据生成速度超过下行链路带宽和人工处理能力，导致星载采集与地面可用情报之间存在日益扩大的鸿沟，亟需在轨智能处理方案。

Method: 在LEO卫星上部署NAVI-Orbital软件系统，使用本地视觉-语言模型(Gemma 3)对捕获场景进行分类、生成文本描述及特征关系，支持自然语言对话交互；系统通过纯英语提示重新任务规划，由基于图的状态机(LangGraph)协调检测和对话专用代理。

Result: 地面基准测试在7,960张图像的AID基准上达到88.16%准确率；FlatSat验证成功；在轨实时捕获的未经处理的YAM-9图像上通过硬件加速GPU推理成功运行，无需针对飞行仪器进行微调。

Conclusion: 证明了在卫星级边缘计算机上运行基础模型的可行性，通过星载语义压缩地球观测数据，可逆转传统"采集-下传-全部"的带宽利用模式。

Abstract: As Earth Observation data generation outpaces downlink bandwidth and human-in-the-loop processing, a widening gap has emerged between onboard collection and actionable ground intelligence. This paper presents NAVI-Orbital, a software system deployed on a Low Earth Orbit (LEO) spacecraft. On April 16, 2026, NAVI-Orbital achieved what is, to the authors' knowledge, the first in-orbit demonstration of a vision-language model performing autonomous multi-modal inference entirely onboard. NAVI-Orbital uses a local vision-language model (Gemma 3) to classify each captured scene, produce a text description of its content and the relationships between its features, and respond to operator follow-up via natural-language dialogue. The system is re-tasked through plain-English prompts in place of conventional command sequences, and is orchestrated by a graph-based state machine (LangGraph) coordinating dedicated agents for detection and dialogue. Results across ground benchmarking (88.16% accuracy on the 7,960-image curated AID benchmark), Flatsat validation, and live in-orbit captures of newly acquired, previously unseen Earth imagery (including uncorrected YAM-9 imagery, processed onboard with hardware-accelerated GPU inference and no fine-tuning for the flight instrument) demonstrate the feasibility of running foundation models on satellite-class edge computers to invert the conventional acquire-then-downlink-everything bandwidth profile through semantic compression of Earth observations in-orbit.

</details>


### [2] [CaVe-VLM-CoT: An Interpretable Vision-Language Model Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18385)
*Sneha Rao,Shaina Raza,Dhanesh Ramachandram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CaVe-VLM-CoT 是一个基于模块化反射式代理RAG框架，通过五阶段闭环流程（提取器、检索器、求解器、引用注入器、验证器）来强制进行证据基础推理，有效减少视觉语言模型的幻觉问题，在ScienceQA和MMMU上分别达到87.1%和55.2%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型容易产生流畅但视觉上不真实的输出（幻觉），现有的思维链和检索增强方法只能部分解决这一问题，因为它们既没有强制步骤级别的引用基础，也没有将验证失败路由回检索进行纠正。

Method: 提出了CaVe-VLM-CoT框架，采用五阶段闭环流程：Extractor（提取关键信息）、Retriever（检索相关证据）、Solver（求解答案）、Citation Injector（注入引用）、Verifier（验证断言），并实现结构化反馈机制；同时提出了一套包含23个组件级指标的评估体系，以CaVeScore为核心复合指标。

Result: 在ScienceQA上达到87.1%准确率和56.6% CaVeScore；在MMMU（30个学科）上达到55.2%准确率和35.7% CaVeScore。

Conclusion: 该框架无需任何架构或提示修改即可有效提升VLM的推理准确性和证据基础，验证了将验证失败路由回检索进行纠正的闭环策略的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) remain prone to hallucinations, producing fluent but visually unfaithful outputs. Existing chain-of-thought and retrieval-augmented methods only partially address this, as they neither enforce step-level citation grounding nor route verification failures back to retrieval for correction. We present CaVe-VLM-CoT, a modular reflection-based agentic-RAG framework that enforces evidence-grounded reasoning through a five-stage closed-loop pipeline: Extractor, Retriever, Solver, Citation Injector, and Verifier, in which detected ungrounded claims trigger structured feedback to the Extractor for targeted re-retrieval. Since no existing framework jointly measures retrieval quality, step-wise citation faithfulness, and cross-modal grounding, we propose a suite of 23 component-wise metrics across all stages, anchored by CaVeScore, a composite metric weighting accuracy, citation precision and recall, attribution, and evidence grounding. Without any architectural or prompt modifications, CaVe-VLM-CoT achieves 87.1\% accuracy and 56.6\% CaVeScore on ScienceQA , and 55.2\% accuracy and 35.7\% CaVeScore on MMMU (30 subjects).

</details>


### [3] [Searching for Synergy in Shared Workspace Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18413)
*Nachiket Kotalwar,Rohini Das,Carolyn Rose*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 共享工作空间中的人机团队协作研究表明，添加模拟人类协作者可能因缺乏协调结构而降低性能；通过结合共享组记忆和模拟人类在环门控的脚手架设计，能显著提升团队绩效，尤其在三人员工团队中效果最明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自动化AI智能体能力日益增强，但许多科学和专业任务仍需要人类判断和上下文专业知识。如何有效协调人类与AI智能体在共享工作空间中的责任分配是一个关键问题。

Method: 使用Collaborative Gym环境与DiscoveryBench任务，通过1,482个会话实验，研究添加模拟人类协作者对团队性能的影响；并评估结合共享组记忆和模拟人类在环（HITL）门控的脚手架设计，其中选定动作需经指定模拟参与者批准。

Result: 添加相关协作者可能因缺乏协调结构而降低性能；提出的脚手架设计（共享组记忆+HITL门控）能获得更高的平均性能，在三人员工团队中效果最明显，提供了更清晰的责任信号和更强的专业知识路由能力。

Conclusion: 人机团队如何协调和整合专业知识，与其可用的能力同样重要。

Abstract: Automated AI agents are increasingly capable, yet many scientific and professional tasks require human judgment and contextual expertise. We study shared-workspace human-AI teams, where AI agents and human collaborators must coordinate responsibilities before submitting a final answer. Using the Collaborative Gym environment with DiscoveryBench tasks, we examine when adding simulated human collaborators improves performance and when process loss turns additional collaborators into coordination overhead. Across 1,482 sessions, adding relevant collaborators can lower performance when teams lack structure to coordinate their contributions. We then evaluate scaffolding that combines shared group memory with simulated human-in-the-loop (HITL) gates, where selected actions require approval from a designated simulated participant. This scaffolding yields higher mean performance, most clearly in three-person teams, with clearer responsibility signals and stronger routing of expertise to team actions. Overall, how human-AI teams coordinate and integrate expertise matters as much as the capability available to them.

</details>


### [4] [CEO-Bench: Can Agents Play the Long Game?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18543)
*Haozhe Chen,Karthik Narasimhan,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CEO-Bench是一个模拟创业公司500天运营的评测基准，用于评估语言模型代理在长期规划、噪声信息获取、环境适应和多任务协调方面的综合能力。最强代理能编写复杂代码进行客户群模拟和隐藏偏好挖掘，但即便最先进的模型（Claude Opus 4.8和GPT-5.5）也仅能维持$1M以上初始资金，且无法持续盈利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型代理评测集中于孤立的短期任务（如软件工程、客服），而现实世界挑战需要代理具备在不确定中导航长周期、在噪声环境中获取信息、适应变化世界以及协调多个子目标等综合能力。作者旨在填补这一评测空白。

Method: 构建CEO-Bench基准，通过可编程Python接口模拟虚构公司500天的运营。代理需管理定价、营销、预算等多个方面，面临与分析噪声商业数据库、将信号转化为策略、用编程协调多决策等挑战相同的环境。环境设计包含四个关键维度：长时域不确定性、噪声信息获取、动态世界适应性和多部件协调。

Result: 最强代理能编写复杂代码进行客户群模拟预测未来现金流、挖掘谈判历史发现隐藏客户偏好。但多数先进模型在该环境中表现挣扎，仅Claude Opus 4.8和GPT-5.5能保持$1M以上初始资金，且两者均无法持续盈利。

Conclusion: CEO-Bench迈出了衡量代理在持续自适应进步过程中所需智能水平的第一步，揭示了当前语言模型代理在长期战略性任务上的显著不足。

Abstract: Language model agents are becoming proficient executors at isolated, short-horizon tasks such as software engineering and customer service. Yet real-world challenges require a combination of sophisticated skills that remain largely untested in agents: (1) navigating long horizons amid uncertainty; (2) acquiring information in noisy environments; (3) adapting to a changing world; (4) orchestrating multiple moving parts toward a coherent goal. We introduce CEO-Bench, which evaluates these capabilities together by simulating a representative real-world task: operating a startup for 500 days. An agent manages pricing, marketing, budgeting, and many other aspects of a fictional company through a programmable Python interface, operating in the same environment and facing the same challenges as a human CEO. Success demands analyzing noisy, interconnected business databases, translating signals into sound strategy, and coordinating many decisions with programming. The strongest agents write sophisticated code that simulates customer cohorts to forecast future cash and mines negotiation history to uncover hidden customer preferences. Even so, most state-of-the-art models struggle in this environment. Only Claude Opus 4.8 and GPT-5.5 finish above the $1M starting balance, and neither consistently turns a profit. CEO-Bench takes a first step toward measuring the intelligence required to drive sustained, adaptive progress over time.

</details>


### [5] [DeFAb: A Verifiable Benchmark for Defeasible Abduction in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18557)
*Patrick Cooper,Alvaro Velasquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DeFAb（可废止溯因基准），一个将四十年来公开知识库转化为形式化可废止溯因实例的数据集和生成流水线。基于规则的逻辑求解器在50微秒内实现100%准确率，而最佳前沿语言模型在渲染鲁棒评估下仅达23.5%。同时发布DeFAb-Hard（235实例，最佳模型53.3% vs 符号系统100%）和CONJURE（内核验证的560个Lean 4/Mathlib变革性创造力实例，发现零个新概念）。同一验证器可作为偏好优化的精确奖励信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在需要形式化逻辑推理（特别是可废止溯因推理）的任务上表现不佳，而评估创造力与理论推理缺乏严格的逻辑基础。作者希望通过构建形式化可验证的基准，用逻辑严谨性来衡量创造力与理论推理能力。

Method: 将OpenCyc、YAGO、Wikidata等分类层次与ConceptNet、UMLS等行为属性图配对，从18个来源生成33.75M条物化规则，产生372,648+个实例，分为三个难度级别，所有假设均通过多项式时间可验证的推导、保守性和极小性检查。同时构建CONJURE，使用Lean 4/Mathlib内核验证创新性（金标准是证明内核先前不包含的定义）。

Result: 符号系统100%准确率且50微秒内完成；最佳语言模型在渲染鲁棒评估下仅23.5%；思维链方差达~36个百分点，超过任何模型间差距；污染控制实验发现+19.4个百分点的Level 3差距。CONJURE试点发现零个新概念。

Conclusion: 前沿语言模型未能可靠地内化可废止推理，在形式逻辑推理任务上远逊于符号系统。形式化验证框架可作为评估和提升模型创造性推理能力的有效工具，同一验证器可作偏好优化的奖励信号。

Abstract: A rule-based logic solver resolves every instance in our benchmark in under 50 microseconds with 100% accuracy; the best frontier language model reaches 65% at best and drops to 23.5% under rendering-robust evaluation (worst case over four surface renderings). We introduce DeFAb (Defeasible Abduction Benchmark), a dataset and generation pipeline that converts four decades of publicly funded knowledge bases into formally grounded instances for defeasible abduction: constructing hypotheses that explain anomalies by overriding defaults while preserving unrelated expectations. Because every hypothesis must pass polynomial-time checks for valid derivation, conservativity, and minimality, DeFAb makes logical rigor the instrument for measuring creativity and theoretical reasoning, scoring the disciplined construction of theory revisions rather than fluent but theory-destroying prose. The pipeline pairs taxonomic hierarchies (OpenCyc, YAGO, Wikidata) with behavioral property graphs (ConceptNet, UMLS) to produce 372,648+ instances across 33.75M materialized rules from 18 sources, in three levels with polynomial-time verifiable gold standards. Four frontier models do not reliably internalize defeasible reasoning: rendering-robust Level 2 accuracy is 7.8-23.5%; chain-of-thought variance (~36 pp) exceeds any inter-model gap; and a matched contamination control isolates a +19.4 pp Level 3 gap. We further release DeFAb-Hard (a 235-instance Level 3 difficulty variant; best model 53.3% vs 100% symbolic) and CONJURE (a kernel-verified transformative-creativity variant of 560 Lean 4/Mathlib instances whose gold answers are definitions the proof kernel did not previously contain, judge-free verifier; a pilot finds zero novel concepts). The same verifier doubles as an exact reward for preference optimization (DPO, RLVR/GRPO). Released under MIT at https://huggingface.co/datasets/PatrickAllenCooper/DeFAb.

</details>


### [6] [Optimizing Lithium Production Decisions under Geological, Demand, and Pricing Uncertainties: A POMDP Framework for Multi-Objective Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18598)
*Anna C. Edmonds,Mansur M. Arief,Robert J. Moss,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Jef Caers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了锂生产决策中的优化问题，将问题建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），并通过信念状态规划方法求解。相比人工启发式策略，POMDP求解器能够动态适应不同的锂价格机制（静态、线性、指数、随机），在勘探、生产和技术选择上实现最优排序，从而在项目生命周期内实现更高的需求满足率和更平衡的经济环境效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锂生产决策面临地质不确定性、价格不确定性、需求不确定性以及不同提取技术（如直接锂提取与硬岩开采）之间的复杂选择。现有模型未能同时考虑定价不确定性、需求不确定性以及多种开采技术，无法制定稳健的策略来确定何时、何地开矿以及采用何种生产方式。

Method: 将问题形式化为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），利用信念状态规划方法进行求解，从而实现对不同锂价格机制（静态、线性、指数、随机）的动态适应和显式不确定性管理。

Result: POMDP求解器在多种定价和矿床场景下均优于人工启发式策略，实现了更高的需求满足率和更平衡的经济环境效益。

Conclusion: 将不同定价模型和提取技术纳入生产决策模型，能够生成更稳健的策略，不仅优化开矿的时间和地点，还能确定最佳的生产方式，实现项目全生命周期内的综合最优。

Abstract: Decision making in lithium production is challenging, whether from an investor's perspective or a strategic production standpoint. Determining which mines to open and when to open them involves not only geological and price uncertainties, but also complexities around the choice of extraction method, from direct lithium extraction to hard rock mining. Prior work explored models of this problem and different methods to optimize mining decisions; these models did not account for uncertainty in pricing, uncertainty in demand, or different mining technologies to extract lithium. Incorporating different pricing models and extraction technology into these models enables more robust strategies for determining not only when and where to open a mine, but also which method of production to pursue. We frame the problem as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP) and solve using belief state planning methods to get optimal decision making. In our study, we show that POMDP solvers outperform human inspired heuristics by dynamically adapting to shifting lithium price regimes (static, linear, exponential, and stochastic) through belief state planning and explicit uncertainty management. By optimally sequencing exploration, production, and technology choice, the framework achieves higher demand fulfillment and more balanced economic environmental outcomes over the projects lifetime in all different pricing and deposit scenarios.

</details>


### [7] [ForecastBench-Sim: A Simulated-World Forecasting Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18686)
*Jaeho Lee,Nick Merrill,Ezra Karger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ForecastBench-Sim，一个基于Freeciv策略游戏模拟环境的预测基准测试，通过游戏回滚生成可立即解析的连续/二元预测问题、条件性反事实问题及罕见事件样本，用于补充真实世界预测基准在时效性、长尾事件和反事实评估方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用AI预测基准受限于真实世界的约束：结果解析缓慢、尾部事件稀少、反事实问题难以评分。需要一种可控制、可即时解析的预测评估环境来弥补这些不足。

Method: 基于Freeciv回合制策略游戏构建模拟世界，通过游戏回滚机制生成固定世界报告（结构化游戏状态快照），提出关于隐藏未来状态的问题；继续模拟以解析结果并评分。可生成连续/二元预测问题、配对干预世界的条件/因果问题，以及稀有或颠覆性事件的已解析示例。

Result: 构建了完整的基准测试流程，包括问题族类、评分协议、发布工件，并报告了模型评估和匿名人类试点实验的验证切片结果。

Conclusion: ForecastBench-Sim旨在通过提供可控、可即时解析的动态世界状态概率推理任务，补充真实世界预测基准的不足。

Abstract: Forecasting benchmarks for general-purpose AI systems usually inherit the constraints of the real world: outcomes resolve slowly, tail events are rare, and counterfactual questions are difficult to score. We introduce ForecastBench-Sim, a simulated-world forecasting benchmark built on game rollouts from Freeciv, a turn-based strategy game modelled on the Civilization series. Forecasters receive a fixed world report (a structured snapshot of the current game state) and answer questions about hidden future states; the benchmark then continues the simulation and scores forecasts. Because the world is simulated, the same setup can generate continuous or binary forecasting questions at arbitrary time horizons, paired intervention worlds for conditional or causal questions, and resolved examples of rare or disruptive outcomes. We describe the benchmark pipeline, question families, scoring protocol, and release artifacts, and report validation slices from model evaluations and an anonymized human pilot. ForecastBench-Sim is intended to complement real-world forecasting benchmarks by providing controlled, immediately resolvable tasks for studying probabilistic reasoning under dynamic world states.

</details>


### [8] [What Must Generalist Agents Remember?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18746)
*Khurram Yamin,Namrata Deka,Maitreyi Swaroop,Albert Ting,Jeff Schneider,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化论证了通用智能体必须在记忆中存储领域相关信息才能实现近似最优决策，提出了分离定理：当两个领域存在观察瓶颈但需要不相容的最优动作时，任何统一近似最优策略都必须在瓶颈处产生不同的记忆分布；进一步证明，若记忆包含足够信息来估计相关目标的价值，则可近似重建局部转移动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化理解通用智能体（generalist agents）在多环境、多目标下实现近似最优行为时，记忆所扮演的必要角色。

Method: 通过信息论和决策理论的形式化框架，定义观察瓶颈（observational bottleneck）与最优动作不相容（incompatible optimal actions）的条件，推导分离定理（separation theorem），并建立记忆内容与价值估计、转移动态重建之间的数学关系。

Result: 1）存在观察瓶颈且最优动作不相容时，近似最优策略必须产生不同的记忆分布；2）充分成功的智能体不能仅依赖当前状态观测，必须保留领域相关信息于记忆中；3）记忆若含有足够信息可近似重建局部转移动态。

Conclusion: 记忆是通用智能体实现领域区分、转移模型重建和规划的核心基板（substrate）。

Abstract: This paper develops a formal account of what generalist agents must store in memory in order to act near-optimally across multiple environments and goals. It shows that when two domains share an observational bottleneck but require incompatible optimal actions, any uniformly near-optimal policy must induce distinct memory distributions at that bottleneck. The result yields a separation theorem: sufficiently successful agents cannot rely only on current state observations, but must preserve domain-relevant information in memory. The paper further shows that if an agent's memory contains enough information to estimate values for related goals, then that memory can be used to approximately reconstruct the agent's local transition dynamics. Together, these results characterize memory as the substrate that supports domain disambiguation, transition-model reconstruction, and planning for generalist agents.

</details>


### [9] [R2D-RL: A RoboCup 2D Soccer Environment for Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18786)
*Haobin Qin,Baofeng Zhang,Hidehisa Akiyama,Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍R2D-RL，一个将RoboCup 2D足球模拟平台（RCSS2D）与HELIOS客户端连接到Python MARL接口的强化学习环境，支持全场/场景训练、混合动作空间、EPV奖励塑形和并行执行，并提供了基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人足球是MARL的挑战性测试平台，但RCSS2D的竞赛导向服务器-客户端架构难以直接用于现代Python-based MARL工作流。

Method: 通过共享内存通信和周期级同步，将RCSS2D与HELIOS客户端连接到Python MARL接口；支持全场和场景训练、可配置对手、Base离散和Hybrid参数化动作空间、动作掩码、EPV奖励塑形及并行执行。

Result: 提供了前场进球场景和11v11全场基准测试的基线结果。

Conclusion: R2D-RL有效解决了RCSS2D平台与Python MARL框架之间的集成难题，为机器人足球领域的MARL研究提供了实用的标准化环境。

Abstract: Robot soccer is a challenging testbed for multi-agent reinforcement learning because it combines partial observability, cooperative and adversarial interaction, sparse rewards, and long-horizon tactical behavior. RoboCup 2D Soccer Simulation (RCSS2D) provides a mature robot-soccer platform, but its competition-oriented server-client architecture is difficult to use directly with modern Python-based MARL workflows. We introduce R2D-RL, a reinforcement learning environment that connects RCSS2D and HELIOS-based player clients to a Python MARL interface through shared-memory communication and cycle-level synchronization. R2D-RL supports full-field and scenario-based training with configurable opponents, Base discrete and Hybrid parameterized action spaces, action masks, expected possession value (EPV)-based reward shaping, and parallel execution. We provide front-goal scenarios and an 11-vs-11 full-field benchmark, together with baseline results.

</details>


### [10] [ProfiLLM: Utility-Aligned Agentic User Profiling for Industrial Ride-Hailing Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18803)
*Tengfei Lyu,Zirui Yuan,Xu Liu,Kai Wan,Zihao Lu,Li Ma,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProfiLLM是一个面向工业网约车调度系统的智能体LLM数据管道，通过工具增强的全局知识挖掘和效用对齐的用户画像探索两个模块，将LLM作为语义特征提取器整合到生产级匹配流水线中，在滴滴生产系统上实现了AUC提升6.14%、GMV提升4.35%及在线A/B测试的全面改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级网约车调度系统依赖结构化数值特征，忽视了诸如司机对某些区域的习惯性回避等具有上下文语义的行为信号。LLM虽然能够生成用户画像来表达这些信号，但将其扩展到实时、毫秒级延迟的调度场景面临三个未同时解决的核心挑战：平台日志远超LLM上下文窗口、长尾用户交互稀疏、表面流畅的画像不一定提升下游预测效用。

Method: ProfiLLM包含两个模块：(1) 工具增强的全局知识挖掘——为LLM智能体配备27种分析工具来挖掘平台级数据，生成可复用的全局知识、自适应用户聚类规则和区域级供需先验；(2) 效用对齐的用户画像探索——每个聚类生成多个候选画像，通过轻量级下游效用代理评估，迭代精化最佳候选并构建偏好对用于DPO微调。

Result: 在滴滴生产调度系统上，ProfiLLM实现结果预测AUC相对提升+6.14%，调度模拟GMV提升+4.35%，14天在线A/B测试中GMV提升+0.47%、完成率提升+0.33%、接单前取消率降低-0.82%。

Conclusion: ProfiLLM有效解决了将LLM作为语义特征提取器集成到生产级网约车调度系统的数据工程问题，通过全局知识挖掘和效用对齐的画像探索，在多个评估维度上取得了一致且显著的性能提升，验证了该方法在工业级实时系统中的实际可行性。

Abstract: Bringing Large Language Models (LLMs) into industrial ride-hailing dispatch as semantic feature extractors over platform-scale behavioral logs is a compelling but under-explored data systems problem. Production matching pipelines remain dominated by structured numerical features, yet decisive behavioral signals (e.g., a driver's habitual aversion to certain regions) are inherently contextual and naturally expressible as LLM-generated user profiles. However, scaling such profiling to a live, millisecond-latency dispatcher faces three intertwined constraints rarely addressed together: on a platform with millions of daily orders, logs exceed any LLM's context window by orders of magnitude; most users are long-tail, with too few interactions for per-user profiling; and surface-fluent profiles do not necessarily improve downstream prediction utility. We present ProfiLLM, an agentic LLM data pipeline that operationalizes utility-aligned user profiling for production matching systems through two modules. (1) Tool-Augmented Global Knowledge Mining equips an LLM agent with 27 analytical tools to mine platform-scale data, producing reusable global knowledge, adaptive user clustering rules, and region-level supply-demand priors. (2) Utility-Aligned Profile Exploration generates multiple candidate profiles per cluster, evaluates them via a lightweight downstream utility proxy, iteratively refines the best candidates and constructs preference pairs for DPO fine-tuning. Deployed on DiDi's production dispatcher, ProfiLLM achieves up to +6.14% relative AUC improvement in outcome prediction, up to +4.35% GMV gain in dispatching simulation, and consistent improvements in a 14-day online A/B test including +0.47% GMV, +0.33% Completion Rate, and -0.82% Cancel-Before-Accept rate.

</details>


### [11] [WorldLines: Benchmarking and Modeling Long-Horizon Stateful Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18847)
*Yehang Zhang,Jianchong Su,Haojian Huang,Yifan Chang,Tianhao Zhou,Xinli Xu,Yingjie Xu,Yinchuan Li,Zexi Li,Ying-Cong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorldLines是一个面向长期家庭辅助的基准测试，包含时间跨度长的家庭交互轨迹、记忆问答和具身任务规划样本。作者提出ObsMem观察者记忆框架，通过维护可见性感知记忆和动作原生状态轨迹来支持状态感知决策。实验揭示了部分可观测性、状态覆盖以及将长期记忆转化为具身计划等持续挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆基准主要评估语言为中心的检索和问答，而具身基准常聚焦短期任务执行，缺乏在动态环境中测试长期记忆使用的能力。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建面向长期家庭辅助的具身记忆基准。

Method: 构建WorldLines基准：生成时间扩展的家庭轨迹（包含对话、动作、执行反馈、对象和设备状态变化），转换为证据关联的记忆问答和具身任务规划样本。提出ObsMem框架：维护可见性感知记忆和动作原生状态轨迹，用于状态感知决策。

Result: 实验揭示了部分可观测性、被覆盖的世界状态以及将长期记忆转化为具身计划等持续性挑战。ObsMem为此场景提供了更强的参考架构。

Conclusion: WorldLines基准和ObsMem框架有效揭示了长期家庭辅助中记忆使用的核心挑战，ObsMem为构建长期具身记忆系统提供了更有力的架构参考。

Abstract: To assist humans over extended periods in real homes, embodied agents must remember user routines, world states, and past interactions. Existing long-term memory benchmarks mainly evaluate language-centric retrieval and question answering, while embodied benchmarks often focus on short-horizon task execution without testing long-term memory use in dynamic environments. We introduce WorldLines, a project-driven benchmark for long-horizon embodied household assistance. It constructs temporally extended household traces with dialogues, actions, execution feedback, object and device state changes, and converts them into evidence-linked samples for Memory QA and Embodied Task Planning. We further propose ObsMem, an observer-grounded memory framework that maintains visibility-aware memories and action-native state trails for state-aware decisions. Experiments reveal persistent challenges in partial observability, overwritten world states, and translating long-term memory into embodied plans, while ObsMem offers a stronger reference architecture for this setting.

</details>


### [12] [Externalizing Research Synthesis and Validation in AI Scientists through a Research Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18874)
*Zijian Wang,Hanqi Li,Ziyue Yang,Zijian Hu,Shenghan Zuo,Yunzhe Zhang,Da Ma,Danyu Luo,Chenrun Wang,Jing Peng,Tiancheng Huang,Sijia Guo,Huayang Wang,Zichen Zhu,Senyu Han,Yilu Cao,Kai Yu,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Xcientist 是一个将科研综合与实验验证过程外部化为可审计、合约化流程的研究框架，通过持久化研究制品来保证科学推理的可归因性和可检查性，并识别了"主张漂移"这一自动化研究中的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统在自动化科学工作流时，连接先验证据、生成想法、实验和最终主张的推理过程往往隐含在模型内部，缺乏可检查性和可归因性。

Method: 提出Xcientist框架，将文献证据、想法状态、实现计划、消融记录和修复轨迹组织为持久化研究制品；通过合约化流程对生成机制进行基于证据的落地、执行、测试和修订。

Result: 在无训练记忆系统、图结构交通预测和多尺度物理信息神经网络三个任务上，Xcientist能够从问题构建到机制设计、验证和有限修订的全过程中保持可追溯轨迹。

Conclusion: AI科学家不仅应通过最终制品来评估，还应通过其综合与验证过程是否保持可归因性、可检查性和科学可问责性来评估。

Abstract: AI systems can increasingly automate scientific workflows, but the reasoning that links prior evidence, generated ideas, experiments and final claims often remains implicit inside model inference. Here we introduce Xcientist, a research harness that externalizes research synthesis and experimental validation into inspectable, contract-governed processes. Xcientist organizes literature evidence, idea states, implementation plans, ablation records and repair traces as persistent research artifacts, so that generated mechanisms can be grounded, executed, tested and revised without losing their evidential basis. We identify claim drift as a failure mode of automated research, where runnable artifacts no longer support the mechanism originally claimed. Across training-free memory systems, graph-structured traffic forecasting and multi-scale physics-informed neural networks, Xcientist preserves traceable trajectories from problem formulation to mechanism design, validation and bounded revision. These results suggest that AI scientists should be evaluated not only by their final artifacts, but by whether their synthesis and validation processes remain attributable, inspectable and scientifically accountable.

</details>


### [13] [Generative-Model Predictive Planning for Navigation in Partially Observable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18888)
*Thomas Quilter,Yifan Zhu,Guorui Quan,Mingfei Sun,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BeliefDiffusion框架，结合扩散模型与模型预测控制，在部分可观测环境中通过显式建模多模态信念分布并规划导航策略，显著优于强化学习和生成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于信念的方法（尤其使用神经网络近似信念空间）在高维部分可观测环境中难以捕捉信念空间的固有模态性，尤其存在感知混淆时。生成模型虽具潜力但需大量数据且缺乏长期规划机制。

Method: BeliefDiffusion包含两步：(1) Imagining：基于观测历史利用扩散模型显式表征多模态信念分布；(2) Planning：在聚合的配置上利用模型预测控制（MPC）高效规划导航策略。

Result: 在合成地图环境的实验中，BeliefDiffusion在导航成功率和路径效率上显著优于无模型强化学习基线及其他生成方法。

Conclusion: 显式地将多模态信念表征纳入规划过程，能够在部分可观测环境中实现更鲁棒的导航。

Abstract: Navigation in partially observable environments presents a significant challenge for autonomous agents, requiring effective decision-making with limited sensory information in unknown environments. Belief-based methods, particularly those using neural networks to approximate the belief space, often fail to capture the inherent multimodality of belief spaces, especially in high-dimensional cases with perceptual aliasing. While generative models present a compelling alternative, they typically require substantial data or expert demonstrations and lack explicit mechanisms for long-term planning. In this paper, we introduce BeliefDiffusion, a novel framework that combines the benefits of both generation and planning. BeliefDiffusion leverages diffusion models to explicitly characterize multimodal belief distributions and utilizes Model Predictive Control (MPC) to simultaneously plan ahead. It consists of two steps: (1) Imagining plausible environment configurations based on observation history and (2) Planning efficient navigation strategies across an aggregated configurations. Through extensive experiments in synthetic map environments, we demonstrate that BeliefDiffusion significantly outperforms both model-free reinforcement learning baselines and other generative approaches in navigation success rate and path efficiency. Our results validate that explicitly incorporating multimodal belief representations into planning enables more robust navigation in partially observable settings.

</details>


### [14] [Skill-Guided Continuation Distillation for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18890)
*Zhimin Fan,Hongwei Yu,Yeqing Shen,Haolong Yan,Guozhen Peng,Tianhao Peng,Yudong Zhang,Xiaowen Zhang,Kaijun Tan,Zheng Ge,Xiangyu Zhang,Daxin Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出技能引导连续蒸馏（SGCD）框架，通过从策略诱导的偏离轨迹状态中生成成功连续轨迹，弥合专家轨迹在闭环执行中的监督缺失，在OSWorld-Verified上将三个基础模型的成功率从约30%提升至50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI代理通常依赖专家轨迹的行为克隆，但当前策略偏离专家策略时，会进入专家轨迹未覆盖的策略诱导偏离轨迹状态，这些状态缺乏有效监督，导致代理无法选择正确动作。

Method: SGCD是一个迭代自改进框架：首先运行无技能引导的普通策略若干步以到达真实的偏离轨迹状态；然后使用技能引导策略从这些状态完成任务并产生成功连续轨迹；将这些成功连续轨迹与专家轨迹混合，为策略诱导的偏离轨迹状态提供监督信号。技能从成功和失败的 rollout 中提取，包括连续计划、关键目标、失败陷阱和成功标准。

Result: 在OSWorld-Verified基准上，SGCD将三个基础模型的成功率从30%左右提升至超过50%，验证了其有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: SGCD通过技能引导和连续蒸馏，有效解决了闭环执行中策略诱导偏离轨迹状态的监督缺失问题，显著提升了GUI代理的性能。

Abstract: Improving GUI agents typically relies on behavior cloning on expert trajectories. However, as the current policy deviates from the expert policy, it inevitably encounters policy-induced off-trajectory states during closed-loop execution, i.e., states that fall outside the expert trajectories. Since expert trajectories provide no demonstrations for these unseen states, such states receive no effective supervision, leaving the policy unable to select the correct action. To close this supervision gap, we propose Skill-Guided Continuation Distillation (SGCD), an iterative self-improvement framework. SGCD first runs the plain policy without skill guidance for a few steps to reach realistic off-trajectory states. From these states, a skill-guided policy then completes the task and produces successful continuations, which are mixed with expert trajectories to supply supervision over policy-induced off-trajectory states. The skills are extracted from both successful and failed rollouts, consisting of Continuation Plans, Critical Targets, Failure Traps, and Success Criteria. On OSWorld-Verified, SGCD improves the success rate of three base models from the low-30\% range to over 50\%, demonstrating its effectiveness and generality.

</details>


### [15] [SciRisk-Bench: A Risk-Dimension-Aware Benchmark for AI4Science Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18936)
*Linghao Feng,Yinqian Sun,Dongqi Liang,Sicheng Shen,Chenfei Yan,Yuxuan Peng,Yilin Zhao,Haibo Tong,Kai Li,FeiFei Zhao,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SciRisk-Bench基准，从显式风险维度和科学学科两个互补视角评估AI4Science安全性，覆盖7个学科、31个子学科和10个风险维度，并对主流LLM和科学专用LLM进行了细粒度诊断评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在科学领域的应用日益增多，从问答到自主发现，但现有AI4Science安全数据集覆盖了多种学科和任务格式，却未充分明确底层风险维度，亟需一个能同时评估科学能力和风险规避能力的基准。

Method: 构建SciRisk-Bench基准，从两个互补视角（显式风险维度和科学学科）设计评估体系，覆盖7个学科、31个子学科和10个风险维度；对主流LLM和科学专用LLM进行跨风险维度、学科和子学科的细粒度评估。

Result: 通过实验评估了主流LLM和科学专用LLM在风险维度、学科和子学科上的表现，实现了对科学模型不安全区域的细粒度诊断。

Conclusion: SciRisk-Bench能够有效评估AI4Science场景中的安全性，揭示模型在特定风险维度和学科中的不安全表现，为科学领域LLM的安全部署提供诊断工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly embedded in AI for Science (AI4Science) workflows, from scientific question answering and literature analysis to laboratory planning and autonomous discovery. This progress creates an urgent need for safety benchmarks that evaluate not only scientific competence, but also whether models recognize and avoid risks in high-stakes scientific contexts. Existing AI4Science safety datasets cover several disciplines and task formats, leaving the underlying risk dimensions underspecified. We introduce \textbf{SciRisk-Bench}, a benchmark designed to evaluate AI4Science safety from two complementary perspectives: explicit risk dimensions and scientific disciplines. SciRisk-Bench covers 7 disciplines, 31 subdisciplines and 10 risk dimensions. In the experimental section, we evaluate both mainstream LLMs and science-oriented LLMs across risk dimensions, disciplines, and sub-disciplines, enabling fine-grained diagnosis of where scientific models remain unsafe.

</details>


### [16] [Decoupling Search from Reasoning: A Vendor-Agnostic Grounding Architecture for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18947)
*Emmanuel Aboah Boateng,Kyle MacDonald,Amardeep Kumar,Siddharth Kodwani,Sudeep Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出解耦搜索接地（DSG）架构，将搜索接地从推理模型中分离，通过MCP兼容网关实现供应商路由、源感知上下文渲染、配置化回退、检索深度控制及精确+语义缓存等一级控制，在保持或略超native搜索精度的同时，将搜索成本降低91-98%，延迟降低68%，并避免搜索诱导冗长问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级LLM代理日益依赖实时搜索，但原生搜索接地将检索策略、供应商选择、证据注入、成本、延迟和生成行为捆绑在单一模型-供应商边界内，导致接地难以检查、调优、重用或移植，且可能引发搜索诱导冗长（Search-Induced Verbosity），破坏严格的输出契约。

Method: DSG是一种与供应商无关的架构，通过MCP兼容网关将接地外置于推理模型之外，提供六大一级控制：供应商路由、源感知上下文渲染、配置化回退、检索深度控制、精确缓存与语义缓存。作为共享生产接地层，支持可互换模型的大规模代理工作负载。

Result: 在SimpleQA、FreshQA和HotpotQA上对五个前沿模型进行评估：native搜索在FreshQA上领先；DSG在SimpleQA上接近native精度（86.1% vs 87.7%），搜索成本降低91%，遵守简洁答案契约，达到99.4%热缓存命中率，延迟降低68%。在电商查询理解（QIU）工作负载上，DSG匹配或略超native搜索精度，搜索成本降低超98%。

Conclusion: 实时接地应被视为可优化的接口边界，而非固定的模型特征。DSG证明了将接地解耦为独立可控制层，能够在保持精度、显著降低成本与延迟的同时，实现更好的可检查性、可调优性和可移植性。

Abstract: Production LLM agents increasingly depend on real-time search, yet native search grounding bundles retrieval policy, provider choice, evidence injection, cost, latency, and generation behavior behind a single model-provider boundary. This coupling makes grounding hard to inspect, tune, reuse, or port, and can trigger Search-Induced Verbosity that breaks strict output contracts. We present Decoupled Search Grounding (DSG), a vendor-agnostic boundary that moves grounding outside the reasoning model through an MCP-compatible gateway, exposing provider routing, source-aware context rendering, configured fallback, retrieval-depth control, and exact plus semantic caching as first-class controls. Across five frontier models on SimpleQA, FreshQA, and HotpotQA, native search leads on recency-sensitive FreshQA, but DSG exposes a stronger frontier when control matters: on SimpleQA it nearly matches native accuracy (86.1% vs. 87.7%) at 91% lower search cost, preserves concise answer contracts, and reaches a 99.4% warm-cache hit rate with 68% lower latency. Deployed as a shared production grounding layer for large-scale agentic workloads with interchangeable models, DSG matches or slightly exceeds native-search accuracy on an e-commerce query-understanding (QIU) workload while cutting search cost by over 98%. Real-time grounding is best treated as an optimizable interface boundary, not a fixed model feature.

</details>


### [17] [RTSGameBench: An RTS Benchmark for Strategic Reasoning by Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18950)
*San Kim,Daechul Ahn,Reokyoung Kim,Hyeonbeom Choi,Seungyeon Jwa,Jonghyun Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RTSGameBench基准，基于大型RTS游戏《Beyond All Reason》评估VLM在战略推理、多智能体协作和长程规划方面的能力，并配套RTSGameAgent框架。实验表明现有VLM在需要紧密协调和大规模任务时表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在竞争与合作场景下的战略推理能力不足，而当前的RTS基准评估范围有限、缺乏系统性能力诊断、且场景覆盖固定无法扩展。

Method: 构建基于《Beyond All Reason》的RTSGameBench基准，包含多样化对战结构评估、针对单一战略能力的迷你游戏诊断评估、以及通过自进化生成框架将自由形式查询转化为新迷你游戏的扩展机制。同时提供RTSGameAgent框架，利用有限状态机和智能体记忆管理单位。

Result: 多个最先进VLM在需要紧密协作、多智能体协调以及任务规模增大时表现不佳。

Conclusion: RTSGameBench能有效诊断VLM在战略推理方面的缺陷，现有模型在复杂多智能体对抗场景下仍存在显著局限。

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language Models (VLMs) often struggle with strategic reasoning, i.e., anticipating and influencing other agents' actions, under uncertainty in competitive and cooperative settings. Real-time strategy (RTS) games can be a natural testbed for diagnosing this limitation, as they demand coordination with allies, adaptation to opponents' strategy, and long-horizon planning under partial observability. However, existing RTS benchmarks offer limited evaluation scope, lack systematic competency diagnosis, and remain fixed in the pre-designed scenario coverage. To address these limitations, we present RTSGameBench, which is built on Beyond All Reason, a large-scale RTS game with an expanded battlefield that demands broader strategy diversity than the existing testbeds. The proposed benchmark provides evaluations through diverse gameplay across various matchup structures, diagnostic assessment via mini-games, each targeting an individual strategic competency, and extensible coverage via a self-evolving generation framework that converts free-form queries into new mini-games, improving over successive cycles. Additionally, for VLMs to operate in large-scale RTS games, we provide RTSGameAgent that manages units by an FSM with agentic memory. We empirically validate that multiple state-of-the-art VLMs do not perform well when matchups demand tighter coordination, multiagent coordination and when task scale increases.

</details>


### [18] [ThinkDeception: A Progressive Reinforcement Learning Framework for Interpretable Multimodal Deception Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18988)
*Jinhao Song,Shan Liang,Yiqun Yue,Zhuhuayang Zhang,Tianqi Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ThinkDeception，一个可解释的多模态欺骗检测框架，首次引入多模态大语言模型，将欺骗检测从二分类任务转变为显式认知推理过程，通过构建多模态思维链数据集和VAC-GRPO训练策略，在主流基准上达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有欺骗检测方法依赖端到端黑盒范式，缺乏可解释性，无法提供透明推理轨迹，也难以捕捉欺骗行为中细微的跨模态不一致性。

Method: 1) 首次将MLLM引入欺骗检测，转化为认知推理过程；2) 构建了第一个细粒度多模态Chain of Thought数据集；3) 提出Visual-Audio Consistency Group Relative Policy Optimization (VAC-GRPO)及渐进式训练策略，包含四级难度分层、动态课程调度器、多维过程感知奖励机制和反思学习范式。

Result: 在主流基准上达到新SOTA，在检测准确率和推理质量两方面均显著超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 本工作成功将欺骗检测领域推向可解释的多模态认知推理方向。

Abstract: Multimodal deception detection is critical for identifying fraudulent intentions, yet existing approaches predominantly rely on end to end black--box paradigms. These methods suffer from a severe lack of interpretability failing to provide transparent reasoning trajectories and struggling to explicitly capture the subtle, cross modal inconsistencies inherent in deceptive behaviors. To transcend these limitations, we propose ThinkDeception, a novel and interpretable multimodal deception detection framework. As a pioneering effort, it introduces Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) into this domain, transforming deception detection from a traditional binary classification task into an explicit cognitive reasoning process. Facilitated by the first meticulously annotated step--by--step multimodal Chain of Thought (CoT) dataset, we develop a foundational model, ThinkDeception Base, empirically validating the critical role of modal inconsistency in decoding deception. Building upon this foundation, our core innovation lies in proposing Visual-Audio Consistency Group Relative Policy Optimization(VAC--GRPO) equipped with a progressive training strategy. Distinct from standard GRPO, we stratify the training data into four progressive difficulty tiers, guiding the model through a psychologically grounded easy--to--hard cognitive transition. By innovatively coupling this dynamic curriculum scheduler with a multi dimensional, process aware reward mechanism and a reflective learning paradigm, we significantly elevate the model's overall reasoning quality. Extensive experiments on mainstream benchmarks demonstrate that ThinkDeception establishes a new SOTA, significantly outperforming existing methods in both detection accuracy and rationale quality. Ultimately, this work successfully drives the field of deception detection toward interpretable, multimodal cognitive reasoning.

</details>


### [19] [RODS: Reward-Driven Online Data Synthesis for Multi-Turn Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19047)
*Ruishan Fang,Siyuan Lu,Chenyi Zhuang,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RODS提出了一种奖励驱动的在线数据合成方法，通过利用GRPO中梯度信号集中在能力边界样本上的特性，使用进度奖励方差作为零成本边界检测器，动态生成与策略共同进化的多轮工具使用训练数据，在仅需约800个活跃样本的情况下达到了与17K样本离线管道相当的性能，实现了约20倍的数据效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮工具使用强化学习受限于静态数据集中信息丰富样本的快速耗尽，因为GRPO的梯度信号遵循Popoviciu上界，集中在奖励方差最大的任务上，而随着策略演进，能力边界不断移动，静态数据中的边界样本逐渐耗尽。

Method: RODS利用进度奖励方差作为零成本边界检测器（无需额外推理），持续识别能力边界样本，通过技能对齐的重采样管道合成具有匹配结构复杂度（如API拓扑和依赖深度）的新多轮变体，并管理一个与策略共同演化的动态回放缓冲区。

Result: 从400个人工种子样本出发，维持约800个活跃训练样本，RODS达到了与17K样本离线管道相当的性能，所需轨迹减少约20倍；在受控设置下优于固定数据RL和环境增强方法。

Conclusion: 通过在线数据合成与策略演化协同，RODS有效解决了多轮工具使用RL中数据耗尽问题，大幅提升了样本效率。

Abstract: Multi-turn tool-use RL is bottlenecked by the rapid depletion of informative samples in static datasets. We observe that the gradient signal in GRPO concentrates on tasks with the highest rollout reward variance, a consequence of the Popoviciu upper bound. Consequently, samples near the agent's capability boundary -- where successes and failures are roughly balanced -- contribute disproportionately large policy gradients. As training progresses, this boundary continuously shifts, which gradually depletes the pool of informative samples in a static dataset. We propose RODS (Reward-driven Online Data Synthesis) to resolve this depletion. RODS closes the loop between RL training and data generation by repurposing the progress reward variance as a practical, zero-cost boundary detector that requires no extra inference beyond the rollouts already computed for training. It continuously identifies such boundary samples, synthesizes new multi-turn variants matching their structural complexity (e.g., API topology and dependency depth) via a skill-aligned resampling pipeline, and manages a dynamic replay buffer that co-evolves with the policy. Starting from 400 human seeds and maintaining an active training pool of ~800 samples, RODS achieves comparable performance to a 17K-sample offline pipeline while requiring roughly 20x fewer trajectories, and improves over fixed-data RL and environment augmentation in our controlled setting.

</details>


### [20] [ARIADNE: Agnostic Routing for Inference-time Adapter DyNamic sElection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19079)
*Enrico Cassano,Michał Brzozowski,Zuzanna Dubanowska,Paolo Mandica,Neo Christopher Chung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARIADNE是一种无需训练、与适配器无关的推理时路由框架，通过计算训练集嵌入的质心表示每个适配器，在推理时根据无标签输入与质心的距离自动选择最合适的适配器，在23个任务上恢复97.44%的上界性能，在44个任务上达到89.7%的平均选择准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PEFT路由方法要么需要访问适配器内部信息（如权重分解或梯度统计），要么需要额外训练路由器，导致可扩展性和可移植性差，无法适应不断增长的异构适配器池。

Method: ARIADNE为每个适配器从其训练集嵌入中计算一组质心，以捕获该适配器的数据分布；推理时对无标签输入，在潜在空间中测量其与各质心的距离，选择最近的适配器。路由完全在输入嵌入空间进行，无需修改适配器或训练过程。

Result: 在Llama 3.2 1B Instruct上对23个NLP任务评估，恢复97.44%的上界性能；扩展到44个任务时，平均选择准确率达89.7%。

Conclusion: ARIADNE是一种高效、可扩展的免训练路由方法，兼容任意PEFT方法，无需适配器内部信息，显著提升了多任务适配器池的自动选择能力。

Abstract: The increasing deployment of parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has led to model ecosystems in which a single backbone is paired with many task-specialized adapters. In this setting, inference-time queries often arrive without task labels, requiring the system to automatically select the most appropriate adapter from a growing and heterogeneous adapter pool. Existing routing methods either depend on access to adapter internals, such as weight decompositions or gradient-based statistics, or require additional router training, which limits scalability and portability as new adapters are added. We introduce ARIADNE, a training-free, adapter-agnostic routing framework for dynamic adapter selection at inference time. ARIADNE represents each adapter through a set of centroids computed from embeddings of its training set, capturing the data distribution associated with that adapter. Given an unlabeled input, it selects an adapter by measuring proximity to these centroids in latent space. Because routing is performed entirely in the input embedding space, ARIADNE is compatible with arbitrary PEFT methods and requires no modification to the adapters or training procedures. Primarily evaluated with Llama 3.2 1B Instruct on 23 diverse NLP tasks, ARIADNE recovers 97.44% of the upper bound performance. Scaling to 44 tasks, it achieves 89.7% average selection accuracy, without additional training or access to adapter internals.

</details>


### [21] [Towards an Agent-First Web: Redesigning the Web for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19116)
*Eranga Bandara,Ross Gore,Ravi Mukkamala,Asanga Gunaratna,Safdar H. Bouk,Xueping Liang,Peter Foytik,Abdul Rahman,Sachini Rajapakse,Isurunima Kularathna,Pramoda Karunarathna,Chalani Rajapakse,Ng Wee Keong,Kasun De Zoysa,Tharaka Hewa,Amin Hass,Wathsala Herath,Aruna Withanage,Nilaan Loganathan,Atmaram Yarlagadda,Sachin Shetty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出Web最初为人类用户设计，AI代理的崛起打破了这一假设，并从访问层、经济层和内容层三个层面提出十项设计原则，构建以代理为第一公民的互联网新架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web底层假设（消费者是人）被AI代理的普及打破，现有机制（封锁、CAPTCHA、将代理访问视为非法提取）无法有效应对，亟需重新设计。

Method: 从三个层面提出系统性方案：1）访问层：代理继承人类访问权限、速率限制、代理标识元数据、双架构内容分发；2）经济层：基于意图的分层框架、代币化订阅、委托内容经济；3）内容层：应对“认知递归”问题，提出Agent文本标记语言（ATML）、四级人类监督模型、加密溯源链。

Result: 提出一套涵盖三个层面、共十项设计原则的Agent优先互联网架构，重新定义代理在访问权、经济义务和内容真实性方面的社会契约。

Conclusion: 需要重新谈判Web的基础社会契约，使AI代理从被排斥对象转变为优先考虑的合法参与者，同时防范认知递归导致的知识脱离人类真实性问题。

Abstract: The World Wide Web was built on an assumption held for three decades: the primary consumer of web content is a human being. This permeates every layer; its access model presumes human visitors, its economics rest on human attention, and its content targets human perception. The rapid emergence of AI agents as intermediaries between humans and web content invalidates this assumption. Yet the web resists agents through blanket blocking, CAPTCHA-based exclusion, and economic models that treat agent access as extraction rather than legitimate interaction.
  This paper proposes a principled redesign across three layers. At the access layer, agents acting for humans should inherit equivalent access rights, governed by rate limiting and agent identification metadata in HTTP requests, analogous to browser headers, alongside a dual-layer architecture serving human-readable and agent-optimized content from the same domain. At the economic layer, we propose an intent-based tier framework grounded in the agent-as-human-proxy principle: an agent's economic obligation mirrors that of the human it represents. A token-based subscription model meters content in tokens rather than pageviews, alongside a commissioned content economy anchoring AI content production in human intentionality. At the content layer, we identify epistemic recursion, the self-referential loop in which AI-generated content is consumed by agents to produce further content, progressively detaching web knowledge from human ground truth. We propose the Agent Text Markup Language (ATML), a four-level human supervision tier model, and a cryptographic provenance chain to counter this threat.
  Together these constitute ten design principles for an agent-first internet, one in which agents are first-class citizens whose integration requires renegotiating the web's foundational social contract across access, economics, and content.

</details>


### [22] [Analysing drivers and interdependencies in European electricity markets using XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19118)
*Antoine Pesenti,Aidan O'Sullivan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文结合深度神经网络与可解释人工智能（XAI）技术，利用SHAP和扩展的SSHAP框架，分析了39个欧洲竞标区的电价决定因素。研究发现，可再生能源（尤其是太阳能）对电价形成的影响远超其发电占比，天然气价格仍是主导驱动因素，互联线路显著影响电价动态。此外，构建了合成性全欧统一电力市场以探索完全一体化市场的反事实情景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力市场具有强非线性、高维交互和跨区域相互依赖等复杂特性，深度神经网络虽预测能力强，但缺乏可解释性，难以理解价格形成的潜在驱动因素。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用深度神经网络模型结合SHAP（Shapley加性解释）量化特征贡献，并应用和扩展SSHAP聚合框架以提升高维场景下的可解释性。同时构建合成性全欧统一电力市场进行反事实分析。

Result: 可再生能源（尤其太阳能）对电价形成的作用远超其发电占比；天然气价格仍是主导且一致的驱动因素；互联线路显著影响电价动态，凸显欧洲电力系统的高度相互依赖。

Conclusion: 可解释AI技术能有效揭示复杂电力市场中电价形成的深层驱动因素，为政策制定和市场设计提供重要洞察；全欧统一市场反事实分析有助于理解市场一体化对电价的影响。

Abstract: Electricity markets are inherently complex systems characterised by strong nonlinearities, high-dimensional interactions, and increasing interdependence across regions. While deep neural networks (DNNs) have demonstrated strong predictive capabilities for electricity prices, their lack of interpretability limits their usefulness for understanding the underlying drivers of price formation. This paper addresses this gap by combining DNN models with explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) techniques to analyse the determinants of electricity prices across 39 European bidding zones. We employ SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations) to quantify feature contributions and apply and extend SSHAP, an aggregation framework to improve interpretability in high-dimensional settings. The analysis identifies that renewable energy sources, particularly solar, play a disproportionately important role in price formation despite their lower share in total power generation. Gas prices remain a dominant and consistent driver across electricity markets, while interconnections significantly shape price dynamics, highlighting the strong interdependence of European electricity systems. In addition, a synthetic EU-wide electricity market is constructed to explore the counterfactual scenario of a fully integrated market with a single price.

</details>


### [23] [Human-AI Coevolution Dynamics: A Formal Theory of Social Intelligence Emergence Through Long-Term Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19144)
*Jingyi Zhou,Senlin Luo,Haofan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出人机共演动力学框架（HACD-H），将情感适应、关系组织、社会记忆和人格一致性整合为统一动力学模型，通过约14,700轮对话数据集验证了社会认知的时间层级性、关系吸引子、阶段式发展模式和社会认知能量景观，发现社会智力与社会认知能量显著负相关（r=-0.391, p<0.001），表明社会智力源于长期社会认知共演而非孤立对话能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话AI系统在语言生成、个性化和长上下文交互方面取得进展，但大多通过情感建模、记忆检索或人格条件化等孤立组件来模拟社会行为，缺乏统一框架来解释长期人机交互中稳定社会关系和社会智能的产生机制。

Method: 提出HACD-H框架，将人机交互建模为自组织的社会认知系统，整合情感适应、关系组织、社会记忆和人格一致性，引入多时间尺度社会认知、关系吸引子、信任盆地、发展阶段转变和社会认知能量动力学等原理；构建约14,700轮对话数据集，开发理论驱动的实证评估框架。

Result: 发现社会认知存在时间持久性层级、稳定的关系吸引子、类似阶段转变的发展模式以及结构化社会认知能量景观；社会智力与社会认知能量呈显著负相关（r=-0.391, p<0.001），交互轨迹随时间呈现渐进性能量降低趋势。

Conclusion: 社会智力源于长期社会认知共演而非孤立的对话能力；HACD-H为建模自适应人机社会互动和发展社会智能AI系统提供了统一理论基础。

Abstract: Current conversational AI systems have made significant progress in language generation, personalization, and long-context interaction. However, most existing methods model social behavior through isolated components such as emotion modeling, memory retrieval, or persona conditioning, lacking a unified framework to explain the emergence of stable social relationships and social intelligence in long-term human-AI interaction.To address this, we propose the Human-AI Coevolution Dynamics Framework (HACD-H), a formal model of human-AI interaction as a self-organizing social cognitive system. HACD-H integrates emotional adaptation, relational organization, social memory, and personality consistency into a unified dynamical framework and introduces principles including multi-timescale social cognition, relational attractors, trust basins, developmental phase transitions, and social cognitive energy dynamics.We construct a conversational dataset with approximately 14,700 interaction turns and develop a theory-driven empirical evaluation framework. Results reveal a hierarchy of temporal persistence in social cognition, stable relational attractors, phase-transition-like developmental patterns, and a structured social cognitive energy landscape. Social intelligence shows a significant negative correlation with social cognitive energy (r = -0.391, p < 0.001), and interaction trajectories exhibit progressive energy reduction over time.These findings suggest that social intelligence emerges from long-term social cognitive coevolution rather than isolated conversational capabilities. HACD-H provides a unified theoretical foundation for modeling adaptive human-AI social interaction and developing socially intelligent AI systems.

</details>


### [24] [Beyond Safe Data: Pretraining-Stage Alignment with Regular Safety Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19168)
*Jinhan Li,Kexian Tang,Yihan Xu,Zhuorui Ye,Kaifeng Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出安全反思预训练方法，通过在预训练语料中插入简短安全反思片段，使语言模型在预训练阶段建立自我监控能力，相比数据过滤和改写，能更有效防止模型从安全数据中泛化出不安全行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练阶段的安全对齐方法主要关注如何让训练数据变得安全（如过滤或改写不安全数据），但作者认为这还不够——即使数据本身是安全的，模型仍可能从看似无害的知识和能力中组合出不安全行为。因此需要在预训练阶段就塑造模型的行为模式，而非仅确保数据安全性。

Method: 提出安全反思预训练（Safety Reflection Pretraining），在预训练语料中定期插入简短的安全反思片段，将自我监控机制直接整合到语言建模过程中，建立基础安全能力，后续通过兼容的后训练进行强化。同时在FineWeb-Edu上训练1.7B模型进行真实实验，并构建了完全可控的合成环境MedSafetyWorld进行消融研究。

Result: 安全反思预训练提升了安全分类准确率，显著降低了推理阶段攻击和微调攻击的成功率。在MedSafetyWorld消融实验中，相比数据过滤和改写方法，安全反思预训练在阻止模型从安全数据中泛化出不安全行为方面具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 预训练阶段的对齐不能仅满足于训练数据的安全，还应塑造模型从安全数据中可能习得的行为模式。安全反思预训练是一种有效的预训练对齐方法。

Abstract: To achieve deeper safety alignment for large language models (LLMs), recent efforts have studied how to push safety interventions earlier into the pretraining stage, primarily by filtering unsafe data or rewriting it into safer forms. We argue that pretraining-stage alignment should go beyond making the data safe: LLMs may compose seemingly benign knowledge and capabilities into unsafe behaviors. To this end, we propose Safety Reflection Pretraining, a pretraining-stage alignment method which regularly inserts short safety reflections into pretraining corpora to integrate self-monitoring directly into language modeling, establishing a foundational capability that is subsequently reinforced by compatible post-training. Our experiments with 1.7B models pretrained on FineWeb-Edu show that Safety Reflection Pretraining improves safety classification accuracy and substantially reduces the success rates of inference-stage and finetuning attacks. Complementary to our real-world experiments, we also introduce a fully controlled synthetic environment, MedSafetyWorld, with a clear definition of safety and a reasoning structure under which models can easily generalize unsafe behaviors from safe data. Ablations in MedSafetyWorld further demonstrate a clear advantage of Safety Reflection Pretraining in preventing models from acting on unsafe behaviors generalized from safe data, compared with data filtering and rewriting. Taken together, our findings suggest that pretraining alignment should not only make the training data safe, but also shape the behaviors that models are likely to acquire from safe data.

</details>


### [25] [User as Engram: Internalizing Per-User Memory as Local Parametric Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19172)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"用户即记忆痕迹"（User as Engram）方法，将用户事实以手术级编辑方式存储在哈希键控记忆表中，推理技能则放在共享适配器中。相比每用户LoRA，该方法避免污染无关文本，内存占用减少约33,000倍，间接推理准确率平均提升5.6倍，且多个用户可无损叠加在同一共享表中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型个性化方法存在根本矛盾：将用户事实写入权重（如LoRA）会污染无关文本，而将事实存储在外部（如记忆文件或检索索引）则受限于检索规模和模型能力。大脑通过海马体（稀疏、局部的记忆痕迹）和新皮层（共享技能）分离存储内容和技能，启发我们设计更优的个性化方案。

Method: 提出User as Engram方法：1) 将用户内容以手术级编辑写入Engram模型的哈希键控记忆表，每个编辑精确地在触发器位置开启查找、添加答案所需值，不改变其他任何位置；2) 推理技能由共享适配器承载；3) 不同用户的事实落在不相交的哈希槽中，可无损叠加。该编辑是"玻璃盒"——透明可解释，若写入错误层则会失败。

Result: 1) 内存占用比每用户LoRA小约33,000倍；2) 间接推理准确率平均比每用户LoRA高5.6倍；3) 从未让任何用户的推理能力低于未修改的基座模型；4) 多个用户可无损叠加在同一共享表中，而LoRA仅支持单一用户；5) 在约100个事实后，比在2.5倍大的模型上运行检索管道的方案表现更好。

Conclusion: User as Engram通过将内容和技能分离存储，实现了高效、可扩展、无损且可解释的语言模型个性化。该方法在内存效率、推理准确率和多用户支持方面显著优于现有方案，为个性化语言模型提供了一种更接近大脑工作机制的新范式。

Abstract: Personal memory in a language model is two problems: content and reasoning skill. The brain keeps the two apart (a sparse, local engram in the hippocampus for each episode, a slow neocortex for the shared skills that interpret it), so a new fact need not overwrite everything else. Most personalization today keeps a user's facts outside the weights, in a natural-language memory file or a retrieval index. When facts are written into the model instead, the standard recipe is the per-user LoRA adapter, which does the opposite of the brain, folding content and skill into one global weight delta. Writing a user's facts as a LoRA contaminates text unrelated to them; writing the same facts as local Engram rows leaves it mathematically untouched, resulting in a roughly 33,000x smaller memory footprint.
  We therefore propose User as Engram: store a user's content as surgical edits to the hash-keyed memory table of an Engram model, and carry the reasoning skill in one shared adapter. This layered design matches per-user LoRA's direct recall while delivering 5.6x higher indirect-reasoning accuracy on average, and never makes a single user worse at reasoning than the untouched base. The edit is a glass box: writing a fact switches on its lookup at exactly the trigger, adds the value the answer needs, leaves every other position unchanged to the last bit, and fails if written into the wrong layer. Because different users' facts land in disjoint hash slots, their edits compose: many users live in one shared table at once, stacking additively and losslessly, where a per-user LoRA, a single global weight delta, admits only one. Upon retrieval, a per-user Engram table does not grow with the population the retriever must search, so past ~100 facts it overtakes a retrieval pipeline on a 2.5x larger model.

</details>


### [26] [TxBench-PP: Analyzing AI Agent Performance on Small-Molecule Preclinical Pharmacology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19245)
*Hannah Le,Ramesh Ramasamy,Alex Urrutia,Mahsa Yazdani,Tim Proctor,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TxBench-PP是一个针对小分子临床前药理学的可验证基准测试，用于评估AI代理从真实实验数据中得出准确结论的能力。在16个模型配置、4800条轨迹的测试中，最强配置（Claude Opus 4.8/Pi）仅通过59.3%的测试，表明尚无系统能可靠地恢复临床前药理学决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理有望加速药物发现，但实际部署需要在真实项目决策场景中进行可信评估。现有方法多依赖文献中记忆的事实而非从真实实验数据恢复结论，缺乏可验证的基准测试。

Method: 构建包含100个评估任务的基准测试，按项目阶段、检测类型和任务结构索引，涵盖作用机制、药效学推理、化合物-靶点结合、因果靶点验证、可开发性与安全性及转化疗效。代理接收真实工作流快照，在编码环境中审查文件，返回结构化答案并进行确定性评分。

Result: 在11个模型、16种配置、4800条轨迹中，最强配置Claude Opus 4.8/Pi通过59.3%（178/300），GPT-5.5/Pi为55.3%（166/300），无系统能可靠恢复临床前药理学决策。

Conclusion: 当前AI代理在临床前药理学决策方面表现不足，尚无系统能可靠完成任务，表明该领域仍有巨大提升空间。TxBench-PP作为TherapeuticsBench系列的第一部分，为后续跨药物发现阶段和模态的评估奠定了基础。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) agents promise to accelerate drug discovery by compressing interpretation and decision-making loops, but practical deployment requires trusted evaluation on realistic program decisions. We introduce TherapeuticsBench Preclinical Pharmacology (TxBench-PP), a verifiable benchmark for small-molecule preclinical pharmacology and the first focused slice of a broader TherapeuticsBench effort across drug-discovery stages and therapeutic modalities. TxBench-PP tests whether agents can recover accurate conclusions from real-world assay data rather than memorized facts from literature. The benchmark contains 100 evaluations indexed by program stage, assay type, and task structure, spanning mechanism-of-action (MoA) and pharmacodynamic (PD) reasoning, compound-target engagement, causal target validation, developability and safety, and translational efficacy. Agents receive realistic workflow snapshots, inspect files in a coding environment, and return structured answers graded deterministically. Across 16 model-harness configurations, comprising 11 models and 4,800 trajectories, no system reliably recovered preclinical pharmacology decisions. The strongest configuration, Claude Opus 4.8 / Pi, passed 59.3\% of endpoint attempts (178/300; 95\% CI, 51.1-67.6), followed by GPT-5.5 / Pi at 55.3\% (166/300; 47.0-63.6).

</details>


### [27] [X+Slides: Benchmarking Audience-Conditioned Slide Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19256)
*Haodong Chen,Xuanhe Zhou,Wei Zhou,Xinyue Shao,Yanbing Zhu,Bo Wang,Jiawei Hong,Anya Jia,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出X+Slides基准，用于评估面向受众条件的幻灯片自动生成。该基准涵盖113个主题和7种演示场景，通过8133个源基础探针和四个互补指标（受众覆盖率、领域覆盖率、效率、正确性）进行动态评估。实验表明现有系统（DeepPresenter、SlideTailor、NotebookLM）能恢复部分但非完整的受众关键信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估幻灯片完整性和技术深度，忽视了目标受众这一关键现实因素（如专家需要严谨证明，决策者关注可行结论），因此需要面向受众条件的幻灯片生成评估基准。

Method: 构建X+Slides基准，包含113个主题和7种演示场景的多领域语料库，设计8133个去重且基于源文本的探针，为不同受众分配特定的效用权重，提出四个互补评估指标：受众覆盖率（Audience Coverage）、领域覆盖率（Domain-wise Coverage）、效率（Efficiency）和正确性（Correctness）。

Result: 在τ_A=0.7阈值下，DeepPresenter最佳受众覆盖率为0.714，SlideTailor为0.594，NotebookLM消融版本为0.853，且存在明显的上下文引用差异。表明现有系统能恢复部分但非完整的受众关键信息。

Conclusion: 视觉质量和广泛主题覆盖率不能替代基于源文本的评估来验证证据支撑。现有系统在面向受众条件的幻灯片生成中仍有不足，需要更严格的源文本支撑评估。

Abstract: Automatically generating slide decks from source documents is an important application of large language models (LLMs). Existing benchmarks primarily assess slide completeness and technical depth, while overlooking the target audience as a critical real-world factor. For instance, specialists demand rigorous proofs, whereas decision-makers prioritize actionable conclusions. To bridge this gap, we introduce X+Slides, a benchmark specifically designed for audience-conditioned slide generation. Built on a diverse corpus spanning 113 topics and seven presentation scenes, X+Slides employs a dynamic evaluation framework constructed from 8,133 deduplicated, source-grounded probes. By assigning audience-specific utility weights to the same source-grounded probes, X+Slides reports four complementary metrics: Audience Coverage measures how much audience-essential information is conveyed, Domain-wise Coverage shows which information types are covered, Efficiency measures delivered utility per unit of attention cost, and Correctness verifies whether slide claims are supported by the source. Experiments on DeepPresenter, SlideTailor, and NotebookLM show that current systems can recover a substantial but still incomplete part of audience-essential information: at $τ_A=0.7$, DeepPresenter reaches a best Audience Coverage of 0.714, SlideTailor reaches 0.594, and the NotebookLM ablation reaches 0.853 while showing clear grounding differences. These results indicate that visual quality and broad topic coverage should not be treated as evidence support without source-grounded evaluation.

</details>


### [28] [NeSyCat Torch: A Differentiable Tensor Implementation of Categorical Semantics for Neurosymbolic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19279)
*Daniel Romero Schellhorn,Till Mossakowski,Björn Gehrke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NeSyCat Torch框架，通过强单子和聚合结构将经典、模糊、概率和神经符号语义统一在单一归纳真值定义下，并在MNIST加法任务上以HaskTorch、JAX和PyTorch实现，在速度和准确率上优于LTN和DeepProbLog，接近DeepStochLog。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号语义领域存在碎片化问题，经典、模糊、概率和神经系统各自定义真值的归纳规则不同，缺乏统一框架。NeSyCat虽已通过强单子和聚合结构初步实现统一，但尚缺少对神经网络学习的谓词和函数的支持。

Method: 提出NeSyCat Torch作为桥梁，通过神经网络解释计算符号，在概率编程和张量后端中实现。使用分布单子进行参考语义和度量评估，补充惰性对数张量单子（基于对数半环）实现数值稳定的可微训练，并引入批处理单子进行高效批次训练。公理即源代码：单子风格的do-notation编写一次，单子绑定自动执行边缘化并惰性剪枝。

Result: 在MNIST加法任务上，HaskTorch、JAX和PyTorch实现在速度和准确率上均优于LTN和DeepProbLog，准确率接近DeepStochLog。

Conclusion: NeSyCat Torch在统一的单子参数化框架下适用于多种一阶神经符号方法，而DeepStochLog则不具备这种统一性。该构造对单子是参数化的，例如实例化Giry单子可扩展到连续概率领域（神经表示留待未来工作）。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic semantics is fragmented: classical, fuzzy, probabilistic and neural systems each define truth by their own inductive rules. NeSyCat, extending ULLER, subsumes them under a single inductive definition of truth, parametric in a strong monad and an aggregation structure on truth-values. NeSyCat has so far lacked an account of predicates and functions learned by neural networks.
  We provide NeSyCat Torch as the missing link and interpret computational symbols via neural networks, implementing the framework in probabilistic programming and tensor-based backends. We use the distribution monad for reference semantics and metric evaluation, and complement it by a monad for numerically stable, differentiable training: the lazy log-tensor monad over the log-semiring. For efficient training in batches, we furthermore employ a batch monad. The axioms are the source code: written once in monad-based do-notation, monadic bind performs marginalisation, lazily pruning unneeded branches.
  On MNIST addition, our HaskTorch, JAX, and PyTorch implementations outperform LTN and DeepProbLog in speed and accuracy, while achieving nearly the accuracy of DeepStochLog. However, unlike DeepStochLog, we stay in a uniform framework that applies to many first-order NeSy approaches. Namely, the construction is parametric in the monad; instantiating it with, e.g., the Giry monad extends the approach to continuous probability (working out a neural representation here is left for future work).

</details>


### [29] [Rethinking Reward Supervision: Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19327)
*Siyi Gu,Jialin Chen,Sophia Zhou,Arman Cohan,Rex Ying*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation（评分标准条件自我蒸馏）框架，利用结构化细粒度评分标准替代单一标量奖励或链条式标注，通过教师模型对学生轨迹提供令牌级指导，在科学推理基准上平均超越GRPO 1.0分、OPSD 0.9分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理语言模型的后训练方法存在局限：蒸馏依赖昂贵且可能含噪声的链式思维标注；强化学习将评估反馈压缩为标量信号，掩盖了响应中哪些方面需要改进。

Method: 提出Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation框架，包含两阶段流水线：第一阶段学习生成任务特定评分标准，第二阶段训练评分标准引导的推理器。教师模型依据评分标准对学生采样的推理轨迹提供令牌级指导，实现比标量奖励优化更细粒度的信用分配。

Result: 在多个科学推理基准上，该方法平均超越GRPO 1.0分，超越OPSD 0.9分。

Conclusion: 评分标准条件自我蒸馏能将评分标准级别的准则有效转化为推理过程中的令牌级指导，是一种优于传统标量奖励优化和单一参考推理监督的方法。

Abstract: Post-training of reasoning language models is commonly driven by supervised distillation and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. Distillation often relies on chain-of-thought annotations that are expensive to obtain and may themselves be noisy, incomplete, or partially incorrect; even when the final solution is correct, an imperfect rationale can interfere with learning. Reinforcement learning with verified rewards, on the other hand, typically compresses evaluative feedback into a scalar signal, obscuring which aspects of a response should be improved. We propose \textbf{Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation}, a framework that incorporates rubrics as structured, fine-grained feedback for on-policy self-distillation. Our method conditions the teacher model on criterion-level rubrics and uses it to provide token-level guidance on the student's own sampled trajectories. This design avoids treating a single reference rationale as the sole supervision target. Instead, rubrics specify what a strong response should satisfy, enabling more fine-grained credit assignment over the reasoning process than scalar reward optimization. We instantiate this framework with a two-stage pipeline that first learns to generate task-specific rubrics and then trains a rubric-guided reasoner. We evaluate on a diverse suite of science reasoning benchmarks and results show that rubric-conditioned self-distillation effectively converts rubric-level criteria into token-level guidance over the reasoning process, surpassing GRPO by 1.0 points and OPSD by 0.9 points on average.

</details>

### [30] [Pointwise is Pointless? A Multimodal Ablation Study for Precipitation Nowcasting with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18436)
*Ophélia Miralles,Máté Mile,Christoffer Artturi,Thomas Nipen,Ivar Seierstad*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过多模态图神经网络在诺德雷达域上进行降水临近预报消融实验，评估了雷达历史、数值天气预报、地面站观测、卫星数据、随机噪声和CRPS集合损失等不同信息源和训练目标对预报性能的影响。结果表明各信息源改善不同预报维度：NWP稳定雷达外推，地面观测提升局地站和降水起始诊断，卫星减少站级偏差但可能过早激活降水，CRPS配置带来最一致的雷达网格增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏点观测数据在降水临近预报中日益可用，但其对密集雷达场预报的具体贡献尚不明确，本文旨在部分回答这一问题。

Method: 采用多模态图神经网络，在诺德雷达域上构建降水临近预报系统，以5分钟间隔预测未来2小时雨率。对雷达历史、MEPS数值预报、Netatmo地面观测、MSG卫星通道、随机噪声和CRPS集合损失进行不同组合的消融实验，使用雷达网格、站位置、降水起始、预言机、位移和振幅等多种诊断指标进行对比评估。

Result: 各信息源改善不同预报维度：MEPS稳定雷达外推；Netatmo观测改善局地站和起始诊断；卫星预测减少站级偏差但确定性使用时会过早激活降水；CRPS配置提供最一致的雷达网格增益；卫星+CRPS组合获得最佳整体预言机/DAS分数。

Conclusion: 稀疏点观测对临近预报有用，但局地观测技能与空间连贯雷达场技能是不同目标。稀疏观测提供局地约束，其对雷达场预报的效益取决于训练损失、不确定性表示以及观测支持在模型中的编码方式。

Abstract: Sparse point observations are increasingly available for precipitation nowcasting, but it is unclear how much they improve dense radar-field forecasts. We partially address this question with a multimodal graph neural network nowcasting system over the Nordic radar domain. The model predicts rain rate every five minutes up to two hours ahead and is trained with different combinations of radar history, MEPS numerical weather prediction, Netatmo surface observations, MSG satellite channels, stochastic noise, and CRPS-based ensemble losses. The study is designed as an ablation of operationally relevant information sources and training objectives. We compare radar-only, NWP-informed, station-informed, satellite-informed, noise-augmented, and CRPS-based configurations using complementary diagnostics on the radar grid, at station locations, for rain onset, and through oracle, displacement, and amplitude scores. The results show that each source improves a different part of the forecast problem. MEPS stabilises radar-only extrapolation, Netatmo observations improve local station and onset diagnostics, and satellite predictors reduce some station-level biases but may activate rain too early when used deterministically. CRPS-based configurations provide the most consistent radar-grid gains, while the combined satellite and CRPS setup gives the best overall oracle/DAS score. These results do not support the conclusion that point observations are uninformative for nowcasting, but they show that local observational skill and spatially coherent radar-field skill are distinct targets. The practical implication is that sparse observations can provide useful local constraints, but their benefit for radar-like fields depends on the training loss, uncertainty representation, and how observation support is encoded in the model.

</details>


### [31] [ToolChain-CRC: Conformal Risk Control for Agentic AI Under Retrieval and Tool-Use Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18467)
*Jeffery Opoku,David Banahene*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出ToolChain-CRC方法，对检索增强和工具使用型AI代理的完整轨迹进行风险控制，通过逐步风险评分、轨迹风险校准和随时报警机制，在分布漂移下保证风险可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI代理在检索文档、调用工具、检查中间信息后产生最终答案，但仅检查最终答案会遗漏检索弱、工具输出错误或中间步骤无支撑等风险问题，需要一种能从轨迹层面控制风险的方法。

Method: 将每次代理运行视为动作、观察和最终输出的完整轨迹，构建逐步风险评分并组合为轨迹风险评分，校准接受或干预规则，通过鞅构造实现随时报警机制，并给出可交换性下的轨迹级风险控制证明及漂移感知扩展。

Result: 在合成工具链漂移、RAG/工具使用压力测试、SQuAD检索任务、无API代理QA案例等多项实验中，仅校准最终答案会遗漏检索和工具失败，而轨迹级校准能将接受轨迹的风险控制在目标水平以下。

Conclusion: 轨迹级别的风险控制比仅关注最终答案更有效，能在分布漂移下保障AI代理的可靠性，为代理系统的安全部署提供了理论保证和实用方法。

Abstract: Modern AI agents retrieve documents, call tools, check intermediate information, and then produce a final answer or action. This creates a risk-control problem that is not visible from the final answer alone. A final response may look acceptable even when the retrieval was weak, a tool output was wrong, or an earlier step was unsupported. We propose ToolChain-CRC, a conformal risk-control method for retrieval-augmented and tool-using agents under drift. The method treats each agent run as a full trajectory of actions, observations, and final output. It builds step-level risk scores, combines them into a trajectory risk score, calibrates an accept-or-intervene rule, and adds an anytime alarm that can stop risky runs before the final answer. We prove trajectory-level risk control under exchangeable calibration runs, give a drift-aware extension with auditable constants, and prove an anytime escalation rule through a supermartingale construction. Experiments cover synthetic tool-chain drift, RAG/tool-use stress tests, public SQuAD-derived retrieval tasks, an API-free agentic QA case study, ablations, target-risk sensitivity checks, 20-seed robustness checks, a drift-margin audit, and a live RAG/tool-use agent benchmark. Across these settings, final-answer-only calibration can miss retrieval and tool failures, while trajectory-level calibration keeps accepted-trajectory risk below the target.

</details>


### [32] [Compact Geometric Representations of Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18520)
*Prashant Gokhale,Piotr Indyk,Yuhao Liu,Sandeep Silwal,Tony Chang Wang,Haike Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了层次化检索中图可达性嵌入的维度下界与上界，证明了有向树可在常数维度3内嵌入，树宽为t的图可在O(t log n)维度嵌入，并给出了匹配或接近的下界，实验表明在真实数据集上能达到更小的嵌入维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层次化检索的可达性嵌入方法在深度层级中维度退化严重（需与节点总数相当），作者希望利用图结构参数（如树宽）构建更紧凑的嵌入表示，从而在实际中实现高效检索。

Method: 理论推导：证明有向树存在常数维度3的嵌入；对树宽为t的图构造O(t log n)维嵌入；通过构造困难实例证明一般DAG需Ω(n)维度，树宽图需Ω(t/log(n/t))维度；同时给出基于交叉边数量的参数化界。实验验证在真实数据集上的表现。

Recent work of You et al. [NeurIPS '25] has extended this approach to hierarchical retrieval, where relevance is determined by the ancestor-descendant relationships in a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG). Abstract: U-calibration studies online forecasting algorithms whose predictions can be consumed by any unknown downstream agent, guaranteeing sublinear regret simultaneously for all proper loss functions. Specifically, we design a single forecast algorithm that simultaneously achieves $\tilde O(\sqrt{T})$ regret for every bounded proper loss and $O(\log T)$ regret for every bounded smooth proper loss. More generally, our algorithm also attains logarithmic regret for losses that are smooth relative to the log-barrier, which include several non-Lipschitz examples.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning is typically analyzed under process-level reward supervision, yet many sequential decision datasets
  record only trajectory-level outcomes. We develop a statistical theory for offline policy optimization from such outcome-level
  supervision. We first study the canonical setting where the target remains the expected cumulative reward, but each offline trajectory
  provides only a scalar label whose conditional mean is the cumulative return. We propose OPAC, a pessimistic actor-critic algorithm
  that learns a latent reward model and optimizes a policy from trajectory-level labels. We prove a high-probability guarantee of order
  $\widetilde O(H^2\sqrt{C_{sa}(π^\star)/n})$ and a matching lower bound, characterizing the sharp statistical cost of replacing
  process-level rewards with one trajectory-level label. We then extend the principle to preference-based feedback, preserving the
  leading horizon and concentrability dependence up to preference-model constants. Finally, we study generalized outcome-based offline
  RL, where both the supervision and the objective are trajectory-level quantities induced by a nonlinear aggregation of latent per-step
  rewards. This problem is not learnable in general: for all-success objectives, any offline learner may require $Ω(2^H)$
  trajectories even with deterministic transitions and constant concentrability. We then identify a tractable regime through two
  structural coefficients, $κ_μ(σ)$ and $χ_μ(σ)$, capturing information loss in outcome aggregation and
  generalized Bellman updates, under which generalized OPAC achieves polynomial sample complexity. Together, our results delineate when
  outcome-level supervision enables sample-efficient offline control and when missing process-level rewards create fundamental
  statistical barriers.

</details>

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个以方法论为中心的迁移学习框架，用于处理领域偏移、类别不平衡和目标标签稀缺条件下的易损性适应问题，同时保持工程可解释性并支持不确定性下的决策。通过四个迁移学习策略（基于实例、基于参数、层次贝叶斯和多源）在三个案例研究中进行验证，结果表明直接迁移源模型（即使用现有最先进模型）在领域偏移和严重类别不平衡下会失败，而有针对性的适应能显著提高低数据环境下的故障检测和预测稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出了一个以方法论为中心的迁移学习框架，包含四种策略：基于实例（通过重要性加权）、基于参数、层次贝叶斯（实现部分池化和后验不确定性量化）、以及多源迁移学习（融合多个分析易损性模型并带学习的源权重和正则化目标域适应）。通过三个互补案例研究进行验证：卡特里娜飓风观测的沿海桥梁易损性、伊恩飓风观测的住宅建筑易损性、以及2001年尼斯夸利地震观测的桥梁地震易损性。

Result: 直接迁移源模型在领域偏移和严重类别不平衡下失效；有针对性的适应显著提高了低数据环境下的故障检测和预测稳定性。

Conclusion: 在开发和适应易损性模型时，需要系统性的诊断、策略选择和不确定性报告指南。

Abstract: This paper presents a methodology-centered transfer learning framework for fragility adaptation under domain shift, class imbalance, and scarce target labels while preserving engineering interpretability and supporting decision-making under uncertainty. Four transfer learning strategies (instance-based, parameter-based, hierarchical Bayesian, and multi-source) are demonstrated through three complementary case studies: (i) instance-based transfer learning via importance weighting, demonstrated on coastal bridge fragility using Hurricane Katrina observations; (ii) parameter-based transfer learning together with hierarchical Bayesian transfer learning, enabling partial pooling across strata and posterior uncertainty quantification, demonstrated on residential building fragility using Hurricane Ian observations; and (iii) multi-source transfer learning that fuses multiple analytical fragility models with learned source weights and regularized target-domain adaptation, demonstrated on seismic bridge fragility using observations from the 2001 Nisqually earthquake. Across these case studies, direct transfer of source models (i.e. using existing state-of-the-art models) fails under domain shift and severe class imbalance, while targeted adaptation substantially improves failure detection and predictive stability in low-data regimes. These findings highlight the need for systematic guidance on diagnostics, strategy selection, and uncertainty reporting when developing and adapting fragility models.

</details>

Result: 在多种真实世界数据集上的异常检测、数据剪枝和标签噪声检测任务中，TimeLAVA 生成的价值分数相比现有方法更具信息量，性能显著更优。

Conclusion: TimeLAVA 提供了一种无学习依赖、理论保障完整、鲁棒性强的时间序列数据估值方案，为时序数据质量管理和鲁棒学习提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Data valuation quantifies the intrinsic quality of individual samples to enable principled data curation, quality control, and robust learning. For time series in critical domains such as healthcare, finance, and industrial monitoring, effective valuation methods are essential yet fundamentally lacking. Existing approaches are either model-dependent, limiting their generalizability, or designed for i.i.d. data and thus fail to capture temporal dependencies, multi-scale patterns, and non-stationary dynamics inherent to sequential data. We introduce TimeLAVA, a learning-agnostic framework that values temporal segments by their marginal contribution to minimizing distributional discrepancy between evaluated and reference data. At its core is a novel Selective Wavelet-based Wasserstein discrepancy combining multi-scale wavelet transforms for temporal localization with unbalanced optimal transport for robustness to distributional shifts. Segment values are efficiently computed via sensitivity analysis without requiring model training and aggregated into point-wise scores. We provide theoretical guarantees linking valuation to model-agnostic generalization and prove bounded sensitivity to outlier contamination. Extensive experiments across anomaly detection, data pruning, and label noise detection demonstrate that TimeLAVA produces significantly more informative value scores than existing methods on diverse real-world datasets.

</details>

*Nicolas Mahler*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将个体决策树重新表述为基于分裂特征上的线性模型，但森林诱导的统一的几何对象尚未明确，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: KPP通过路径度量对森林节点进行特征映射索引，将每个坐标转化为平方欧几里得路径等距嵌入的分量，构建非对角Gram矩阵承载度量。

Abstract: A recent line of work has reframed individual decision trees as linear models on engineered features associated with their splits, opening routes for oracle inequalities and feature-importance reinterpretation, but leaving open the question of what unified geometric object a forest induces when one indexes its feature map by nodes rather than by splits. The present paper studies that object. KPP indexes the feature map by the nodes of the forest, weighted by a path metric that turns each coordinate into a component of a squared-Euclidean path-isometric embedding. KPP unifies four pillars under a single non-diagonal Gram that carries a metric: prediction, exact additive attribution, deterministic Lipschitz robust radius in the KPP metric, and uniform Rademacher risk bounds for regression and classification under fixed, honest, or cross-fit conditioning. All probabilistic guarantees are conditional on the representation and are stated under three explicit conditioning regimes; the robust-radius guarantee is deterministic in the KPP metric rather than in a norm on the raw input. Conjectured fast-rate refinements for both regression and classification are stated as open problems and are not claimed as theorems.

*Connor Simpson,Ricardo J. G. B. Campello*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: FOSC-X是一个从层次聚类树中提取前M个全局最优平面聚类的框架，支持可选的聚类数量约束，通过动态规划实现线性时间复杂度的最优解排序，并能发现被单解方法忽略的替代聚类结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从层次聚类树中提取平面聚类解是实践中的常见任务，现有方法通常只寻找单个最优解，忽略了层次结构中可能存在的多个高质量替代聚类方案，这些方案能捕捉数据的不同方面。

Method: 提出FOSC-X框架，基于动态规划策略：无约束时利用局部最优候选可组合成全局最优解的特性实现多项式时间求解；有聚类数约束时维护可行候选集的上下界，剪枝不可行或占优的组合，保证线性时间复杂度。
