Abstract: Extracting a flat clustering solution from a hierarchy is a common task in practical cluster analysis and can be formulated as an optimisation problem. Existing approaches focus on finding a single optimal solution. We introduce FOSC-X, a framework for extracting the top-M globally optimal flat clusterings from local, non-horizontal cuts of a hierarchical cluster tree, while optionally enforcing constraints on the number of clusters. This enables automatic identification of multiple high-quality alternative clusterings that capture different aspects of the hierarchical structure. Without constraints, the top-M problem can be solved in polynomial time using dynamic programming, exploiting the property that locally optimal partial candidates within subtrees can be combined to form globally optimal solutions while automatically determining the number of clusters. However, this can lead to solutions with numbers of clusters that are ultimately undesirable -- e.g., too large to be meaningful or practically analysed within a particular application domain. Imposing cluster-count constraints breaks the optimality property underlying the unconstrained dynamic programming approach, since locally optimal partial candidates may no longer combine into feasible globally optimal solutions. FOSC-X addresses this challenge through a dynamic programming strategy that maintains compact sets of feasible candidates using lower and upper feasibility bounds while pruning infeasible or dominated combinations. The resulting method guarantees optimal rankings of the top-M solutions with linear-time complexity in the number of cluster nodes and dataset size, both with and without cluster-count constraints. Experiments show that FOSC-X efficiently reveals alternative clustering structures overlooked by single-solution extraction methods.

</details>

*Zheng He,Danica J. Sutherland*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种更鲁棒的序贯条件独立性检验方法，通过自适应优化的核条件独立性统计量、归一化方案及截断-平移校准策略，在Model-X假设存在估计误差时仍能有效控制Type I错误并保持高检验力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件独立性检验本质上困难，在没有额外假设的情况下无法控制Type I错误。Model-X范式通过假设完全已知相关条件分布来应对，但现有序贯检验要求精确已知该分布，当分布需估计时变得脆弱。因此需要一种对估计误差更鲁棒的方法。

Method: 将testing-by-betting应用于自适应优化的核条件独立性统计量，结合归一化方案和截断-平移校准策略。

Conclusion: 所提方法在Model-X假设条件分布需估计时显著提升了鲁棒性，有效解决了序贯条件独立性检验中估计误差导致的问题。

Abstract: Testing conditional independence is fundamental yet intrinsically difficult: without additional assumptions, Type I error control is impossible in general. The "Model-X'' paradigm addresses this difficulty by assuming exact knowledge of a relevant conditional distribution. While small deviations from this assumption can sometimes be tolerated in classical one-shot testing, existing sequential conditional independence tests typically require the Model-X conditional to be known exactly, making them fragile when it must instead be estimated. We propose a new approach that is substantially more robust to such estimation error. Our method applies testing-by-betting to an adaptively optimized Kernel Conditional Independence statistic, together with a normalization scheme and a truncate-and-shift calibration strategy. These modifications greatly reduce Type I error inflation while preserving high power across high-dimensional synthetic benchmarks and real-world fairness tasks, outperforming existing sequential Model-X approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/he-zh/SKCI.

</details>


### [40] [Quantifying and Auditing LLM Evaluation via Positive--Unlabeled Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19057)
*Zilong Zhang,Yi-Ting Hung,Lei Ding,Chi-Kuang Yeh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM作为评判者时的系统性偏差（尤其是冗长偏差）问题，提出了一种基于部分最优传输的几何审计框架，将选择性人工监督下的LLM评估建模为正-无标签学习问题，通过对齐少量人工验证的正样本与可靠的无标签输出子集，无需重新训练即可修正有偏评判，实验表明该方法在人类偏好对齐、鲁棒性和可解释置信度估计上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评判者存在系统性的评价偏差（如冗长偏好），而人工监督成本高昂且通常只提供选择性标注（仅有正标签），导致大量输出处于无标签且质量混杂状态。现有方法无法有效利用这种部分监督信号来修正评判偏差。

Method: 将LLM评估建模为正-无标签（PU）学习问题，提出基于部分最优传输（Partial Optimal Transport）的几何审计框架。在固定嵌入空间中，将少量人工验证的正样本与可靠的无标签输出子集进行对齐，从而识别与人类一致的偏好并修正有偏评判器，无需重新训练模型。

Result: 实验表明该方法在以下三方面取得改进：(1) 与人类偏好的一致性更高；(2) 对呈现偏差（如冗长）的鲁棒性更强；(3) 可提供可解释的置信度估计。

Conclusion: 该工作为现有的LLM-as-a-judge流水线提供了一种可扩展且具有统计理论基础的替代方案，有效解决了选择性监督下的评判偏差问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as judges for scalable evaluation, yet such LLM--as--a--Judge systems exhibit systematic biases that are decoupled from semantic quality, most notably verbosity bias. Meanwhile, human supervision is costly and typically selective, yielding reliable positive judgments but leaving most outputs unlabelled and potentially mixed in quality. We formulate LLM evaluation under selective human supervision as a positive--unlabelled learning problem and propose a geometric auditing framework based on Partial Optimal Transport. By aligning a small set of human--verified positives with a reliable subset of unlabelled outputs in a fixed embedding space, our method identifies human--consistent preferences and corrects biased judges without retraining. Experiments demonstrate improved alignment with human preferences, increased robustness to presentation biases, and interpretable confidence estimates, offering a scalable and statistically grounded alternative to existing LLM--as--a--judge pipelines.

</details>

Abstract: This paper develops local certificates for population-risk increments around a current model. For a local candidate set \(\mathcal D\), the certificate is a two-sided confidence band for \(P({\ell_{θ+v}-\ell_θ})\) over \(v\in\mathcal D\). As an application, the upper endpoint of this band yields a risk-controlled update rule: an update is accepted only when its certified upper endpoint is nonpositive; otherwise the current model is retained.

</details>

Abstract: Recent empirical work shows that semantically equivalent paraphrases can fool financial sentiment classifiers: although a paraphrase remains close to the original under a strong reference embedding, it may shift the target model's representation enough to change the predicted class. Existing robustness theory either assumes a single-model threat model or focuses mainly on empirical attack algorithms. We develop a continuous local model of semantic paraphrase perturbations that captures this two-model structure. We show that the worst-case local displacement of the target representation, subject to a proxy-model budget, is governed by the largest generalised eigenvalue of a matrix pencil $(A,B)$ constructed from the Jacobians of the two embedding maps. The resulting attackability index $λ^*(x)$ is intrinsic to the local paraphrase geometry and the chosen embedders, yields a closed-form prediction-flip condition for affine readouts, and supports conservative population and finite-sample attackability certificates. For uniform control over classes of affine readouts, we derive a distribution-free VC bound for binary attackability indicators and a scale-sensitive margin bound based on an attackability-adjusted margin that subtracts a local geometric penalty from the standard classifier margin. We also connect the continuous theory to discrete paraphrase search, identify an asymmetry between successful and unsuccessful finite searches, and give a covering condition under which the discrete and continuous settings agree. Finally, we propose an empirical verification framework using soft-token relaxations and generated paraphrase sets to assess the local eigenvalue geometry, prediction-flip condition, and finite-search approximation on a deployed financial-text classifier.

</details>

Result: 理论上证明了DR-UCB策略的主阶遗憾（leading-order regret）在时间跨度依赖上匹配下界；数值实验表明DR-UCB全面优于基准策略。

Conclusion: 考虑替换成本与随机雇佣延迟的序贯劳动力管理问题可通过多臂老虎机框架有效求解，DR-UCB策略在理论上达到了最优遗憾界，在实践中也表现出优越性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a sequential workforce management problem in a contingent labor setting with uncertainty in both worker production and labor supply. A firm seeks to maximize cumulative profit by maintaining an active team of fixed size while learning worker productivity over time. We emphasize two critical operational frictions in this problem: replacing workers is costly, and workers may not be available immediately for hiring because of, for example, prior job commitments, scheduling constraints, or onboarding procedures. Thus, hiring decisions take effect only after a random delay. We formulate this problem as a stochastic multi-play bandit with costly switching and delayed actions, and develop a learning-based hiring policy, DR-UCB (DelayedReplacement-UCB), that makes replacement and hiring decisions sequentially through learning cycles. In each cycle, the policy uses real-time production data to determine when to initiate workforce changes and which workers to replace and hire. We show that the leading-order regret of the proposed policy matches its lower bound in its dependence on the time horizon. Our numerical experiments show that DR-UCB outperforms benchmark policies.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了双层优化中超越乐观/悲观极端情形的一种中间型追随者决策打破平局行为（I-BO），其中追随者的最优响应由领导者决策影响的内生随机性决定。通过逆变换和马尔可夫链变换将I-BO转化为带外生不确定性的T-I-BO，并采用样本均值近似（SAA）求解。实验表明该方法在合理规模实例上高效且优于确定性等价方法，同时指出错误建模追随者打平决策行为会导致次优领导者决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双层优化研究主要采用乐观或悲观这两种极端方式来定义追随者在多个最优解中的打破平局行为，缺乏对介于两者之间的中间情形的研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出一种中间型双层优化（I-BO）框架，其中追随者的响应是受领导者决策影响的内生随机事件。

Method: (1) 形式化定义了一类内生测度，包括强-弱决策依赖的I-BO特例；(2) 通过逆变换和马尔可夫链变换将I-BO转化为带外生不确定性的T-I-BO，使追随者响应表示为领导者决策和外生随机性的函数；(3) 采用样本均值近似（SAA）处理T-I-BO的不确定性，并针对不同变换设计了定制化SAA求解方法。

Result: 所提方法能够高效求解合理规模实例，且优于确定性等价方法。实验强调了准确建模追随者打破平局行为的关键性，尤其是其与领导者目标的对齐程度——错误指定该行为会导致领导者决策次优。

Conclusion: 中间型追随者打破平局行为框架（I-BO）能更灵活地建模双层优化中的现实场景，通过变换和SAA方法可高效求解；实践中必须谨慎对待追随者行为建模，避免因简化假设导致性能损失。

Abstract: In bilevel optimization, optimistic and pessimistic follower behaviors are the most commonly used forms to define how the follower ties-breaks among multiple optimal solutions. In this work, we go beyond these extreme tie-breaking behaviors and investigate the intermediate bilevel optimization program (I-BO), where the follower's selected optimal response is a decision-dependent random event, with a probability measure influenced by the leader's decision. We formally introduce a class of such endogenous measures, including the special case of strong-weak decision-dependent I-BO. We reformulate the I-BO as a Transformed I-BO (T-I-BO) with exogenous uncertainty by defining inverse and Markov-chain transformations, which represent the follower's response as a function of the leader's decision and exogenous randomness. We handle the T-I-BO's uncertainty via sample-average approximation (SAA), and we propose tailored approaches for its SAA program according to the chosen transformation. Computationally, our methods solve reasonable-sized instances efficiently and outperform the deterministic equivalent when available. Furthermore, experiments stress the critical need to accurately model follower tie-breaking behavior, particularly depending on its alignment with the leader's objective, as misspecification leads to suboptimal leader decisions.

</details>

*Xin He,Ya-Ping Fang*

TL;DR: 本文研究了凸-凹双线性鞍点问题的Nesterov加速方法，提出了带有消失阻尼α/t的二阶原始-对偶动力系统，并推导了保持结构的有限差分离散化算法。在非临界情形下，证明了原始-对偶间隙的o(1/t²)改进收敛速度和o(1/t)的平稳性残差收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对凸-凹双线性鞍点问题，现有加速方法在仅仅凸-凹（而非强凸-强凹）设定下的收敛理论尚不完善，本文旨在建立连续和离散视角下的统一加速框架并给出精细的收敛率分析。

Method: 采用二阶原始-对偶动力系统（带有α/t的消失阻尼，α≥3）进行连续时间建模，并推导保持结构（structure-preserving）的有限差分离散化方法，得到带Nesterov外推的快速原始-对偶算法。参数序列满足t_{k+1}² - t_k² ≤ ρ t_{k+1}，ρ∈(0,1]。

Result: 连续时间：α>3时，原始-对偶间隙o(1/t²)，速度o(1/t)，在Lipschitz梯度假设下平稳性残差o(1/t)。离散时间：O(1/t_k²)的原始-对偶间隙收敛率，ρ<1时改进为间隙o(1/t_k²)、平稳性残差o(1/t_k)。

Conclusion: 本文在仅仅凸-凹设定下，为双线性鞍点问题提供了连续与离散统一的加速方法，证明了非临界情形下的改进收敛率，填补了该领域在非强凸-强凹设定下的理论空白。

Abstract: This paper studies Nesterov accelerated methods for continuously differentiable convex-concave bilinear saddle point problems. For the continuous-time model, we analyze a second-order primal-dual dynamical system with vanishing damping $α/t$, where $α\geq 3$. Under the merely convex-concave setting, we prove convergence of the primal-dual trajectory to a saddle point. In the noncritical regime $α>3$, we further obtain the improved rate $o(1/t^{2})$ for the primal-dual gap and $o(1/t)$ for the velocity, and, under an additional Lipschitz gradient assumption, $o(1/t)$ for the stationarity residual. We then derive a structure-preserving finite-difference discretization, which leads to a fast primal-dual algorithm with Nesterov extrapolation. For a general accelerated parameter sequence ${t_k}$ satisfying $t_{k+1}^2-t_k^2\le ρt_{k+1}$ with $ρ\in(0,1]$, we prove the $O(1/t_k^{2})$ convergence rate for the primal-dual gap and convergence of the generated sequence. In the noncritical case $ρ<1$, we further establish the improved rate $o(1/t_k^{2})$ for the gap and $o(1/t_k)$ for the stationarity residual. These results provide continuous-discrete acceleration methods for bilinear saddle point problems in the merely convex-concave setting.

</details>

Abstract: This note generalizes the port-Hamiltonian formulation of the continuous time primal-dual gradient algorithm for static constrained convex optimization to the convex optimal control problem.The resulting dynamics is shown to be a port-Hamiltonian system of partial differential equations, involving ordinary physical time as well 'algorithmic' time. Convergence to the optimal control solution is indicated, and it is argued that sub-optimal control strategies could be derived starting from the partial differential equation formulation.

Abstract: This paper studies the optimal portfolio, consumption, and endogenous early retirement problem within a benchmark tracking framework by incorporating a new relative performance evaluation. In this framework, the investor maximizes expected lifetime consumption utility while managing the maximum wealth shortfall relative to a benchmark, with shortfall-management costs that may differ before and after retirement. Mathematically, the problem is a hybrid stochastic control problem involving both regular controls and an optimal stopping time, in which the running maximum process records the investor's largest benchmark shortfall. We introduce an auxiliary reflected state process and establish an equivalent hybrid stochastic control problem. By proving the convex duality theorem, we technically transform the original problem into a two-dimensional pure optimal stopping problem with state reflection. This enables us to characterize the geometric structure of the stopping set and derive the feedback-form optimal retirement boundary, as well as optimal portfolio and consumption policies. Analytical examples and numerical simulations reveal a two-stage structure with more conservative investment and more aggressive consumption after retirement. Driven by the retirement option, the expected largest shortfall risk follows a pronounced U-shaped pattern with respect to wealth. Shortfall management costs, labor income, and leisure preference significantly influence retirement timing, investment, and consumption.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有极小极大方法大多处理无约束或投影友好设定，而带功能性约束的极小极大问题研究不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，为约束极小极大问题提供高效求解算法。

Method: 利用强对偶性将内层最大化功能性约束并入目标函数，获得原函数非精确梯度；设计近端增广拉格朗日方法（PALM），每个子问题通过非精确加速近端梯度法求解，处理由辅助最大化子问题近似求解带来的非精确梯度。

Result: 在凸-强凹情形下，方法返回$\varepsilon$-KKT点和原$\varepsilon$-最优解，一阶Oracle和迭代复杂度为$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-1})$；凸-凹情形下，原梯度计算复杂度不变，但对偶部分复杂度升至$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-\frac{3}{2}})$。

Conclusion: 提出了首个针对功能性约束凸-(强)凹极小极大问题的有效算法，给出了理论复杂度保证，拓展了极小极大优化方法的适用范围。

Abstract: Minimax problems arise in many applications, including robust learning and Stackelberg models. Most existing methods for minimax problems address unconstrained or projection-friendly settings, while functional constrained minimax problems remain far less explored. We study a class of convex-(strongly-)concave minimax problems with functional constraints. By exploiting strong duality, we incorporate the inner-maximization functional constraints into the objective. This allows us to efficiently obtain inexact gradients of the primal function of the reformulation and to design a proximal augmented Lagrangian method (PALM). Each PALM subproblem is solved by an inexact accelerated proximal gradient scheme to handle inexact gradients arising from approximately solving an auxiliary maximization subproblem. We show that the proposed method returns an $\varepsilon$-KKT point and a primal $\varepsilon$-optimal solution, with $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-1})$ first-order oracle and iteration complexity in the convex-strongly-concave case. For the convex-concave case, the complexity remains the same for the primal gradient evaluation but increases to $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-\frac{3}{2}})$ for the dual part.

</details>

Abstract: We propose a class of neural ODEs that universally approximates locally exponentially stable dynamics and the region of attraction from trajectory data. The model dynamics are constrained by the gradient field of a jointly learned maximal Lyapunov function. Under this constraint, we show that exponentially stable dynamics can be approximated arbitrarily well within the region of attraction. Furthermore, the region of attraction of the constrained model is exactly characterized by the 1-sublevel set of the jointly learned Lyapunov function, and we derive conditions under which it approximates the true region of attraction arbitrarily well. We validate the approach experimentally on nonlinear systems with nonconvex regions of attraction.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 本文为集值约束的双层优化问题提供了系统的理论框架和实用的单循环求解算法。

Abstract: We study bilevel optimisation problems in which the inner problem is represented as a set-valued, parametric constraint. We develop relevant optimistic and pessimistic calculus rules, derive corresponding optimality conditions, and formulate nonsmooth adjoint inclusions based on both the Fréchet and limiting coderivatives. Founded on these results, we propose a single-loop algorithm that accommodates a wide range of inner and adjoint steps, including those of primal-dual methods. We prove its convergence. Numerical experiments on total variation regularised inverse problems demonstrate the practicality of the approach.

*Haiqing Gao,Seyed M. R. Iravani,Sean R. Sinclair*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究动态公平顺序分配问题，中央规划者在需求不确定下跨多个地点分配可分割资源，并联合优化分配与访问顺序，采用事后和前瞻两种最大最小公平目标。研究发现最优分配具有均衡性质及阈值结构，并揭示了在某些条件下按需求变异系数降序访问是最优的，提出了PPA-deCV启发式算法。实验表明不同公平目标与公平指标之间存在不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受人道主义救援和食品分配等应用的启发，将路径决策纳入规划者问题，在需求不确定下联合优化资源分配和访问顺序，以实现公平-效率权衡。

Method: 理论分析揭示最优分配的均衡性质和阈值结构；推导最优路径策略，提出PPA-deCV启发式算法；通过数值实验比较多种公平目标在不同公平指标下的表现。

Result: 最优分配在固定访问顺序下具有均衡性质，且呈阈值结构。按需求变异系数降序访问在条件下最优。PPA-deCV算法能逼近联合最优策略的公平-效率前沿。不同公平目标的改善并不必然转化为公平指标的提升。

Conclusion: 识别了不同公平目标与公平指标间的对应关系，为实际中选择公平目标以实现期望的公平-效率权衡提供了指导。

Abstract: We study a dynamic fair sequential allocation problem in which a central planner distributes a divisible resource across multiple locations under demand uncertainty. Motivated by applications such as humanitarian relief and food distribution, we incorporate routing decisions into the planner's problem and jointly optimize allocation and visitation order under two max-min fairness objectives, ex-post and forward. We first reveal an equating property of the optimal allocation for any fixed visitation order. This property is crucial to establish that the optimal allocation follows a threshold structure: at each location, demand is fully satisfied when sufficiently low and otherwise met proportionally. We then characterize the optimal routing policy and show that, under certain conditions, visiting locations in decreasing order of coefficient of variation (CV) of demand is optimal. Building on this insight, we propose a simple heuristic, PPA-deCV, which closely approximates the fairness-efficiency frontier of the jointly optimal policy. Next, through extensive numerical experiments, we compare multiple fairness objectives across different fairness metrics, demonstrating that improvements in surrogate max-min objectives do not necessarily translate into improvements in fairness metrics. Furthermore, we identify which objective is aligned with which fairness metric, providing practical guidance on objective selection to achieve fairness-efficiency trade-offs.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在在线反馈优化中，系统随机参数的分布会随控制动作而偏移（即分布偏移问题），现有方法难以处理这种耦合关系；同时真实对偶约束集可能未知或难以精确建模，需要设计可操作的代理方法。

Method: 提出投影原始-对偶算法，用替代对偶约束集替换真实对偶约束集；推导均方跟踪误差上界，并分解为四项：问题随机性、输出测量误差、问题时变性、替代集与真实集之间的失配。

Result: 给出了均方跟踪误差的显式上界表达式，每个项对应不同的误差来源，物理意义清晰。

Conclusion: 所提方法可在线处理约束随机优化问题，适用于分布随控制变化的场景，电力系统数值实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Abstract: Online feedback optimization (OFO) leverages real-time output measurements to optimize the operation of networked systems without requiring full knowledge of system dynamics or disturbances. We develop an OFO approach for constrained stochastic optimization problems in which the distribution of the system's random parameters shifts in response to the control actions. We propose a projected primal-dual algorithm where the true dual constraint sets are replaced by surrogate sets. Our main result is an upper bound on the mean-square tracking error, which decomposes into four interpretable terms reflecting: (i) the stochasticity of the problem, (ii) output measurement errors, (iii) time-variability of the problem, and (iv) the mismatch between surrogate and true dual constraint sets. The theory is illustrated in a numerical experiment for power grids with price-responsive assets.

</details>


### [54] [Sparse-dense flight copy-based interactive mechanism to airline integrated with cruise speed control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19332)
*Jiajin Lin,Jianlin Jiang,Yan Gu,Yuzhen Guo,Cheng-Lung Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稀疏-密集航班副本的交互机制，结合巡航速度控制，用于解决航班、飞机和旅客的一体化恢复问题，并设计了定制化Benders分解算法及加速技术，显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统序贯恢复方法忽视了航班、飞机和旅客重调度之间的相互依赖关系，且现有集成模型因航班副本数量过多难以实时求解，尤其考虑巡航速度控制后问题更为复杂。

Method: 提出稀疏-密集航班副本方法，构建集成航班、飞机与旅客恢复模型（IFAPRM），设计定制化Benders分解（CBD）算法，并引入可行性证书、规模管理和有效不等式等加速技术。

Result: 真实数据实验表明，稀疏-密集航班副本交互机制在求解效率和解的质量上均优于传统航班副本方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为复杂运营中的一体化重调度问题提供了通用决策支持框架，可推广至物流、运输等领域。

Abstract: Flight recovery, aircraft rerouting, and passenger reallocation are critical in airline recovery. To preserve their interdependence that is neglected by the regular sequential recovery, we consider these recovery phases from an integration perspective. In addition, we incorporate cruise speed control to enhance the recovery performance. While using flight copies is a common modelling method in airline disruption management, the resulting integrated mathematical model is challenging to solve in real time due to the large number of flight copies, especially when considering cruise speed control. This paper introduces a new sparse-dense flight copy approach and proposes an innovative interactive mechanism that alternately adjusts aircraft routes on the sparse flight copy-based network and reallocates passenger itineraries on the dense flight copy-based network. Under the interactive mechanism, the involved sparse and dense networks are much smaller than those in the conventional flight copy approach. To implement such a mechanism, we develop an integrated flight, aircraft, and passenger recovery model (IFAPRM) and propose a customized Benders decomposition (CBD) to solve the model. Besides, we further propose some acceleration techniques to speed up the CBD method, including an effective feasibility certificate, scale management, and valid inequalities. Computational experiments on real-world data demonstrate that the sparse-dense flight copy-based interactive mechanism outperforms the conventional flight copy approach. In essence, the proposed interactive mechanism, along with its corresponding modelling method, algorithm, and acceleration techniques, provides a comprehensive methodology and a general decision-support framework for integrated rescheduling problems in complex operations, with potential applications in logistics, transportation, and beyond.

</details>

### [55] [Gaussian Mixture Attention: Linear-Time Sequence Mixing via Probabilistic Latent Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18283)
*Yongchao Huang,Hassan Raza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出高斯混合注意力（GMA），一种概率式的注意力机制替代方案。它通过K个高斯混合组件进行隐式路由，将查询和键映射为责任向量，利用责任重叠计算亲和度，并采用K槽潜在记忆读写值。通过矩阵乘法的结合性，GMA避免显式构建N×N亲和矩阵，将存储复杂度降至O(NK)。实验表明GMA在长文本分类任务上具有竞争力，因果GMA在WikiText-103上优于某些线性/随机特征注意力变体，但落后于优化的因果SDPA和Mamba。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准点积注意力中密集的token-to-token交互模式是Transformer架构扩展到长上下文的核心瓶颈，其二次复杂度限制了模型处理长序列的能力。

Method: 提出高斯混合注意力（GMA），用K个可学习的高斯混合组件替代显式的查询-键比较。查询和键被映射为潜在路由空间上的后验责任向量，通过责任重叠隐式定义亲和度；值通过K槽潜在记忆进行读写。利用矩阵乘法的结合性避免显式构建N×N矩阵，实现O(NK)复杂度。设计了双向和因果变体，并提供了端到端可微的参数化方案。

Result: GMA实现了预期的固定K线性内存缩放，在长文本分类任务上与注意力基线竞争。因果GMA在WikiText-103上优于测试的线性/随机特征注意力变体，但落后于优化的因果SDPA和Mamba。责任向量分析显示组件使用广泛，与词形类别有一定对齐。

Conclusion: GMA是一种概率式、可解释、固定K线性时间的注意力风格替代方案，而非优化softmax注意力或状态空间模型的通用替代品。其责任调制梯度结构、约束非负低秩亲和度解释和局部路由稳定性等分析进一步支持了方法的理论合理性。

Abstract: The dense token-to-token interaction pattern of standard dot-product attention remains a central bottleneck in scaling Transformer architectures to long contexts. We introduce \textbf{Gaussian Mixture Attention (GMA)}, a probabilistic attention-style sequence mixer that replaces explicit pairwise query--key comparison with routing through $K$ learned Gaussian mixture components. Queries and keys are mapped to posterior \textit{responsibility} vectors over a shared latent routing space; their overlap defines an implicit responsibility-space affinity, while values are written into and read from a $K$-slot latent memory. By exploiting the associativity of matrix multiplication, GMA avoids materializing the induced $N\times N$ affinity matrix and instead uses two responsibility matrices whose dominant activation storage scales as $\mathcal{O}(NK)$ rather than $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ for fixed $K$. We formulate bidirectional and causal variants of GMA, provide an end-to-end differentiable parameterization of the Gaussian mixture components, and analyze its responsibility-modulated gradient structure, constrained non-negative low-rank affinity interpretation, and local routing stability. Empirically, GMA exhibits the intended fixed-$K$ linear memory scaling and is competitive with attention-style baselines on long-context classification, while causal GMA improves over tested linear/random-feature attention variants on WikiText-103 but remains behind optimized causal SDPA and Mamba in the current implementation. Analysis of learned responsibilities further shows broad component usage and moderate alignment with surface-form token categories, supporting GMA as a probabilistic, interpretable, fixed-$K$ linear-time attention-style alternative rather than a universal replacement for optimized softmax attention or state-space models.

</details>


### [56] [Breaking the Solver Bottleneck: Training Task Generators at the Learnable Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18284)
*Lorenz Wolf,Connor Watts,Roger Creus Castanyer,Geoffrey Bradway,Maxwill Lin,Augustine N. Mavor-Parker,Matthew Daborn-Sargent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PROPEL提出了一种基于求解器摊销（solver-amortized）的框架，通过轻量级激活探针（activation probe）替代昂贵的求解器 rollout，使得在数学、代码和软件工程任务上可以高效训练任务生成器，使其生成位于可学习前沿（learnable frontier）的任务，从而突破强化学习中的任务供给瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着推理和智能体模型的提升，固定任务分布逐渐饱和，而朴素合成生成的任务要么过于简单、要么无法求解或不适定。传统的任务生成器训练需要重复调用求解器进行 rollout，这对软件工程等耗时任务（单次 rollout 数十分钟）来说不可行。

Method: PROPEL 首先在一个一次性标注的生成任务与求解器结果语料上训练一个轻量级激活探针，该探针利用冻结的生成器参考模型预测目标求解器的通过率（pass rate），从而在生成器优化过程中作为求解率的代理，将生成器评估简化为单次前向传播，避免了昂贵的求解器调用。

Result: 在代码任务上，位于可学习前沿的任务比例从 10.1% 提升到 20.0%（Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct）以及从 5.3% 提升到 12.6%（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct）。在软件工程任务上，该比例从 9.8% 提升到 19.6%（Qwen3.5-27B），且针对的是探针和生成器训练阶段未见过的仓库。

Conclusion: PROPEL 通过求解器摊销策略，以极低的计算开销实现了大规模任务生成器的有效训练，显著提升了生成任务位于目标求解率前沿的比例，为突破 RL 训练中的任务供给瓶颈提供了可行的方案。

Abstract: The limiting resource for training agents via reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly frontier task supply: valid, solvable tasks just difficult enough to train the current model. As reasoning and agentic models improve, fixed task distributions saturate, while naive synthetic generation yields tasks that are trivial, impossible, or ill-posed. Training a task generator with RL to optimize validity and learnability can address this bottleneck, but direct optimization requires repeated solver rollouts per candidate. For software-engineering (SWE) tasks, a single rollout can take tens of minutes; solver-in-the-loop generator training is intractable. We introduce PROPEL, a solver-amortized framework for training task generators at the targeted solve rate. PROPEL trains a lightweight activation probe on a one-time labeled corpus of generated tasks and solver outcomes. The probe predicts target-solver pass rate from a frozen generator reference model and serves as a proxy for solve rate during generator optimization, reducing generator evaluation to a single forward pass. Across math, code, and software-engineering at multiple model scales, PROPEL shifts generation toward the targeted solve rate: for coding, tasks generated at the learnable frontier increase from $10.1\% \rightarrow 20.0\%$ for a Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct solver and from $5.3\% \rightarrow 12.6\%$ for a Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct solver. For SWE, PROPEL increases the share of generations at the targeted solve rate from $9.8\% \rightarrow 19.6\%$ for Qwen3.5-27B on repositories not seen during training of probe and generator.

</details>


### [57] [CODEBLOCK: Learning to Supervise Code at the Right Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18286)
*Zhijie Deng,Ling Li,Jinlong Pang,Kaiqin Hu,Qi Xuan,Zhaowei Zhu,Jiaheng Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CodeBlock，一种面向代码的结构感知稀疏监督微调框架，通过选择结构完整的代码片段而非孤立token进行监督，在仅使用1.9%响应token的情况下实现优于全token SFT的pass@1性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码LLM的监督微调对所有响应token采用均匀交叉熵损失，但代码依赖语法完整性和定义-使用关系，直接将NLP领域的token级选择方法迁移到代码领域会破坏代码的结构完整性。

Method: 先筛选高质量指令-响应对，再将代码响应划分为语法连贯的coding items，通过聚合核心逻辑token上的广义交叉熵估计其效用，并用数据流可达性和桥接信号进行重排序，训练时保留完整上下文但仅对选中的代码项和信息性NL token计算损失。

Result: 在六个代码生成基准上，CodeBlock的pass@1优于全token SFT和有竞争力的选择基线，且仅使用1.9%的监督响应token。

Conclusion: 结构感知的稀疏监督策略能够有效提升代码LLM微调效率，验证了在代码领域保留结构完整性的必要性。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning of code LLMs typically applies uniform cross-entropy loss to all response tokens, implicitly assuming that every token provides equally useful learning signal. Recent token-level selection methods challenge this assumption in natural-language SFT by supervising only high-value tokens. However, directly transferring token-level masking to code can break syntactically and semantically coherent program units, because code depends on structural completeness and definition-use relations. We therefore propose CodeBlock, a structure-aware sparse supervision framework that selects structure-complete code evidence rather than isolated tokens. CodeBlock first selects high-quality instruction-response pairs, then partitions code responses into syntactically coherent coding items, estimates their utility by aggregating generalized cross-entropy over core logic tokens, and reranks them with data-flow reach and bridge signals to prioritize blocks that propagate or connect important program dependencies. During training, the full response remains available as context, while loss is applied only to selected code items and informative natural-language tokens. Experiments on six code-generation benchmarks show that CodeBlock achieves stronger average pass@1 than full-token SFT and competitive selection baselines, while using only 1.9% of supervised response tokens.

</details>


### [58] [Artemis: Anatomy-Resolved inTervention for Eliminating Multimodal NeuroImage confounderS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18287)
*Siyuan Dai,Yang Du,Kun Zhao,Zhusuyi Chen,Heng Huang,Paul Thompson,Chao Shi,Haoteng Tang,Liang Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Artemis框架，通过在脑区级别进行因果干预，学习区域特异性混杂因素表示，消除人口统计学因素(年龄、性别)对GNN分析多模态脑网络时的虚假关联影响，在三个基准数据集上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态神经影像分析中，人口统计学因素(年龄、性别)会系统性地混淆脑连接与临床结果之间的关系，导致GNN利用虚假捷径而非学习因果不变的表征。现有因果GNN方法缺乏对临床神经影像数据中真实混杂因素的领域感知调整，且不同脑区对人口统计学因素的敏感性不同，需要区域感知的调整策略。

Method: 提出Artemis框架，在脑区级别进行因果干预：1) 为每个脑区独立学习轻量级的区域特异性混杂因素表示；2) 利用多模态功能(fMRI)和结构(DTI)特征进行图推理；3) 作为即插即用模块兼容任意GNN骨干网络。

Result: 在ADNI(疾病诊断)、OASIS(痴呆分期)和HCP(性别分类)三个基准数据集上，Artemis一致性地优于代表性GNN基线方法，并通过多项支持性实验证明了统计显著性和神经科学可解释性。

Conclusion: 区域级别的因果干预能够有效消除人口统计学混杂因素对脑网络分析的影响，提升GNN在临床神经影像任务中的泛化能力和可解释性。

Abstract: Multimodal neuroimaging, integrating functional connectivity from fMRI and structural connectivity from DTI, enables non-invasive analysis of brain networks using graph neural networks. However, demographic factors such as age and sex systematically confound the relationship between brain connectivity and clinical outcomes, causing GNNs to exploit spurious shortcuts rather than learning causally invariant representations. While recent causal GNN methods introduce causality at the graph-modeling level, their causal mechanisms remain domain-agnostic without accounting for the real-world confounders inherent in clinical neuroimaging data. Moreover, brain networks are constructed from atlas-based parcellations where each region exhibits distinct sensitivity to demographic factors, necessitating region-aware adjustment. We propose Artemis, a region-level causal framework that bridges this gap with causal intervention at each brain region independently by learning region-specific confounder representations with lightweight parameters. Our adjustment comprehensively utilized the multimodal functional and structural features for graph reasoning as a plug-in module compatible with arbitrary GNN backbones. Experiments on three benchmarks, ADNI for disease diagnosis, OASIS for dementia staging, and HCP for sex classification, demonstrate consistent improvements over representative GNN-based baselines. Multiple supporting experiments further demonstrate statistical significance and neuroscientific interpretability.

</details>


### [59] [A Link between Shock-wave Theory and Symmetry-reduced Stochastic Gradient Descent for Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18303)
*Taiki Miyagawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过微分几何、李群和流体力学，建立了激波理论与随机梯度下降对称商化学习动力学之间的数学联系。在商去参数对称性并进行局部熵粗粒化后，有效动力学满足商流形上的粘性Hamilton-Jacobi方程；进一步假设原始参数动力学可由商空间上的梯度场概括，粗粒化损失函数的梯度服从Burgers型方程，并可严格证明激波形成。该理论适用于MLP、CNN、Transformer和平均场网络，并猜想可为深度学习提供实用诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习理论缺乏对参数空间对称性与学习动力学中不连续性（如相变、激波）之间关系的严格数学刻画。参数范数等常见观测指标因对称性冗余而失真，亟需一种基于对称性约化的理论框架来准确监测和预测训练过程中的相变。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 对参数对称性进行商化（quotienting）；(2) 引入局部熵粗粒化（local-entropy coarse-graining）；(3) 在商流形上推导粘性Hamilton-Jacobi方程；(4) 在梯度场假设下推导Burgers型方程并证明激波形成；(5) 在MLP、CNN、Transformer和平均场网络上验证理论适用性。

Result: 成功建立了激波理论与SGD对称商化动力学之间的严格数学联系，证明了在特定条件下粗粒化损失函数梯度服从Burgers型方程且可形成激波。多种主流架构（MLP、CNN、Transformer、平均场网络）均满足推导的Hamilton-Jacobi或Burgers型方程。对称校正的商观测值优于原始参数范数，可作为监测训练相变的原则性基础。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解深度学习中的训练相变提供了严格的数学基础，对称商化观测值可作为监测、预测和控制训练阶段转换的实用诊断工具。未来工作可进一步探索该理论在实践中的应用，如早停、学习率调度等。

Abstract: We develop a mathematically explicit link between shock-wave theory and the symmetry-quotiented learning dynamics of stochastic gradient descent, drawing on differential geometry, Lie group theory, and fluid mechanics. Specifically, after quotienting parameter symmetries and applying local-entropy coarse-graining, the effective dynamics satisfy a viscous Hamilton--Jacobi equation on the quotient manifold. Moreover, under the assumption that the raw parameter dynamics can be summarized by a gradient field on the quotiented space, the gradient of the coarse-grained loss function obeys a Burgers-type equation, and shock formation can be established rigorously. We apply our theory to multilayer perceptrons, convolutional neural networks, Transformers, and mean-field networks, and show that they obey the Hamilton--Jacobi or Burgers-type equations. We conjecture that this framework also yields practical diagnostics for deep learning. In architectures such as Transformers, raw parameter norms are often distorted by symmetry redundancy and may therefore be misleading, whereas symmetry-corrected quotient observables provide a principled basis for monitoring, forecasting, and controlling training-phase transitions.

</details>


### [60] [Attribution-Guided and Coverage-Maximized Pruning for Structural MoE Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18304)
*Yifu Ding,Jiacheng Wang,Ge Yang,Yongcheng Jing,Jinyang Guo,Xianglong Liu,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MoE模型部署时内存占用大、推理开销高的问题，本文提出一种结构化剪枝框架，通过将剪枝比例分配建模为通道分数覆盖率最大化问题，并利用基于归因的近似方法高效求解。实验表明，在50%或25%结构化剪枝结合4-bit量化下，模型准确率保持良好；在Qwen3-30B-A3B上实现5.27倍内存压缩，全面超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE压缩方法大多在专家层面进行粗粒度剪枝，无法捕捉细粒度冗余，导致剪枝预算分配不当、压缩效果有限。本文观察到专家内部信息高度集中在少数通道上，即使重要专家也存在大量冗余，因此需要更细粒度的通道级结构化剪枝方法。

Method: 提出结构化剪枝框架，将剪枝比例分配建模为通道分数覆盖率最大化问题，并采用基于归因的近似方法高效求解，实现对MoE模型中通道级别的细粒度冗余移除。

Result: 在DeepSeek和Qwen MoE模型上，50%或25%结构化剪枝结合4-bit量化后仍保持模型准确率；在Qwen3-30B-A3B上内存占用减少5.27倍，在多个基准测试中持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通道级结构化剪枝相比专家级粗粒度剪枝能更有效地挖掘MoE模型中的冗余，实现更高的压缩比和更好的性能保持，为MoE模型高效部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale compute efficiently, yet remain expensive to deploy due to their substantial memory footprint and inference overhead. Prior compression methods mainly operate at the expert level, either removing entire experts or ranking experts by coarse-grained importance scores. However, such expert-wise decisions are often too coarse to capture fine-grained redundancy, leading to misallocated pruning budgets and limited compression. To address this problem, we observe that information within MoE experts is highly concentrated in a small subset of channels, leaving substantial redundancy even in experts deemed important. Based on this observation, we propose a structural pruning framework tailored for MoE models. Our method reformulates prune-ratio allocation as a channel-score coverage maximization problem and solves it efficiently using an attribution-based approximation. Experiments on DeepSeek and Qwen MoE models show that our method preserves model accuracy under 50% or 25% structured pruning when combined with 4-bit quantization. On Qwen3-30B-A3B, our approach reduces memory footprint by 5.27$\times$ and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [61] [Fisher Width: A Geometric Measure of Complexity on Statistical Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18306)
*Vu Khac Ky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fisher宽度——统计流形上高斯宽度的黎曼几何类比，用Fisher信息度量替换欧几里得度量，使复杂度度量对局部统计曲率敏感且具有重参数化不变性，并证明了其在泛化界、浓度不等式等方面的理论性质及实证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯宽度是压缩感知、凸优化和学习理论中衡量集合有效维度的核心几何复杂度度量，但它本质上是欧几里得的。统计模型天然具有Fisher信息度量诱导的黎曼几何结构，其中方向根据统计可区分性而非欧氏长度来缩放。现有方法无法捕捉统计流形上的曲率效应，因此需要引入一种与Fisher几何相容的宽度概念。

Method: 在参数点θ处，将欧几里得单位矩阵替换为局部度量张量G(θ)^{1/2}，定义Fisher宽度为Fisher重缩放后的集合的高斯宽度。该量对局部统计曲率敏感且在光滑重参数化下不变。进而建立Fisher宽度的理论框架，包括浓度不等式、度量扰动稳定性、与欧几里得基线的谱比较界，并应用于Fisher-Lipschitz假设类的泛化界证明及可计算估计量的设计。

Result: Fisher宽度保留了高斯宽度的关键结构特征（浓度、扰动稳定性、谱比较界），同时能捕捉欧几里得度量无法观测的各向异性几何效应。在MNIST上对三类模型的实证评估验证了该度量的有效性。

Conclusion: Fisher宽度之于统计流形，正如高斯宽度之于欧几里得凸体。该工作为研究弯曲统计流形上的复杂度和学习问题奠定了基础。

Abstract: Gaussian width is a central geometric complexity measure in high-dimensional probability, compressed sensing, convex optimization, and learning theory. It quantifies the average extent of a set along random directions, thereby capturing the effective dimension of constraint sets, hypothesis classes, and descent cones. However, this notion is intrinsically Euclidean. Statistical models instead carry a natural Riemannian geometry induced by the Fisher information metric, where directions are scaled according to statistical distinguishability rather than ambient Euclidean length.
  We introduce Fisher width, a Fisher-geometric analogue of Gaussian width for statistical manifolds. At a parameter point $θ$, Fisher width replaces the Euclidean identity by the local metric tensor $G(θ)^{1/2}$, measuring the Gaussian width of the Fisher-rescaled set. This makes the resulting quantity sensitive to local statistical curvature and invariant under smooth reparameterizations.
  We develop the basic theory of Fisher width, showing that it retains key structural features of Gaussian width, including concentration, metric perturbation stability, and spectral comparison bounds with the Euclidean baseline, while also capturing anisotropic geometric effects invisible to Euclidean measures. As an application, we prove a generalization bound for Fisher-Lipschitz hypothesis classes and propose computable estimators, which we evaluate empirically on MNIST across three model classes.
  Fisher width is to statistical manifolds what Gaussian width is to Euclidean convex bodies. This work lays the foundation for studying complexity and learning on curved statistical manifolds.

</details>


### [62] [DRIFT: Refining Instruction Data via On-Policy Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18307)
*Zefan Wang,Lincheng Li,Tianyu Yu,Yuan Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DRIFT方法，通过基于策略的 influence functions 优化SFT训练数据分布，解决标准IF方法在数据归因中的近距差距和梯度范数偏差问题，提升LLM能力上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据筛选方法在有限预算下加速训练表现优异，但难以提升模型能力上限。挑战已从识别保持性能的小子集，转变为优化数据分布以选取最能提升最终模型的样本。

Method: 提出DRIFT方法：1) 利用模型on-policy rollout作为验证目标，最小化参数近距差距并更好对齐IF局部邻域假设；2) 基于轨迹正确性进行符号加权；3) 对影响分数进行去偏，消除梯度范数偏差；4) 仅用少量验证查询作为可靠锚点归因整个数据集。

Result: 在7B参数指令微调和推理模型上的实验表明，DRIFT持续提升两种场景的性能上限，优于现有数据筛选基线。

Conclusion: DRIFT通过解决标准IF的两项结构局限性，有效改进了SFT数据归因方法，能可靠地提升模型性能上限。

Abstract: Optimizing the training data distribution for Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) dictates the capability of Large Language Models (LLMs). While existing data curation methods excel at accelerating training under constrained budgets, they are less suited to elevating the capability upper bound. The challenge here is no longer to identify a smaller subset that preserves performance, but to refine the data distribution toward instances most capable of improving the final model. To address this problem, we explore instance-level data attribution using Influence Functions (IF). We identify that standard IF formulations struggle in this setting due to two structural limitations: a proximity gap caused by off-policy validation targets, and a severe bias towards gradient norm. We propose DRIFT (Data Refinement via On-Policy Influence Functions for Supervised Fine-Tuning). Instead of relying on external reference data, DRIFT utilizes the model's on-policy rollouts as validation targets, which empirically minimizes the parameter proximity gap and better aligns with the local neighborhood assumption of IF. It further applies signed weighting based on trajectory correctness and debiases influence scores against the gradient hacking issue, allowing a small set of validation queries to act as reliable anchors for attributing the full dataset. Experiments on 7B-parameter instruction and reasoning models show that DRIFT consistently raises the performance ceiling on both, outperforming existing data curation baselines.

</details>


### [63] [TRIDENT: Breaking the Hybrid-Safety-Physics Coupling for Provably Safe Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18308)
*Zijie Meng,Ziwei Li,Yufei Liu,Zhiyu Li,Jiyuan Liu,Wenhua Nie,Bingcai Wei,Miao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRIDENT框架，通过三种协同设计组件（Richardson-Romberg梯度校正、Lyapunov约束顺序信任域更新、物理信息残差评论家）解决网络化CPS中安全MARL面临的混合离散-连续动作、训练时安全约束和物理驱动动力学的三元耦合偏差问题，实现了O~(1/√K)收敛速度和O(√K)累积违规界限，在多场景中训练违规降低95.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化信息物理系统中的安全协调要求学习算法同时处理混合离散-连续动作、硬训练时安全约束和物理驱动动力学，这三个特征形成有向偏差循环，而任何现成模块的简单组合都无法解决这一三元耦合问题。

Method: 提出TRIDENT框架，包含三个协同设计的组件：(1) Richardson-Romberg梯度校正，将Gumbel-Softmax偏差从O(τ)降至O(τ²)；(2) Lyapunov约束顺序信任域更新，保证每迭代可行性；(3) 物理信息残差评论家，分解价值而非奖励。

Result: 证明了O~(1/√K)收敛到约束纳什均衡和O(√K)累积违规界限。在多UAV移动边缘计算、自主交叉口管理和混合SMAC变体上，相比MADDPG训练违规降低95.5%，相比MACPO降低76.3%，同时相比最强无约束基线奖励提升13.5%。

Conclusion: TRIDENT通过协同设计的三个组件有效解决了安全MARL中的三元耦合偏差问题，在保证安全约束的同时实现了优越的性能。

Abstract: Safe coordination in networked cyber-physical systems forces learning algorithms to simultaneously handle hybrid discrete-continuous actions, hard training-time safety constraints, and physics-governed dynamics. We show that these three features form a directed cycle of biases that defeats any naive composition of off-the-shelf modules, and formalize this as a three-way coupling lemma. We then introduce TRIDENT, the first MARL framework whose three components are co-designed to cancel each leak: a Richardson-Romberg gradient correction reducing Gumbel-Softmax bias from O(tau) to O(tau^2), a Lyapunov-constrained sequential trust-region update enforcing per-iterate feasibility, and a physics-informed residual critic that decomposes value rather than reward. We prove an O~(1/sqrt(K)) convergence rate to a constrained Nash equilibrium and an O(sqrt(K)) cumulative-violation bound. On multi-UAV mobile-edge computing, autonomous intersection management, and a hybrid SMAC variant, TRIDENT cuts training-time violations by 95.5% over MADDPG and 76.3% over MACPO, while improving reward by 13.5% over the strongest unconstrained baseline.

</details>


### [64] [Compute Efficiency and Serial Runtime Tradeoffs for Stochastic Momentum Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19179)
*Depen Morwani,Alexandru Meterez,Pranav Nair,Sham Kakade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机动量方法（HB和ASGD）在一致线性回归中的批量大小权衡，证明HB在不损害计算效率的前提下允许更大的批量窗口（比SGD大√κ倍），而ASGD的计算效率与串行运行时间之间存在依赖于谱分布的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机动量方法在现代训练中广泛使用，但其随机性收益依赖于串行运行时间和计算效率两个不同量，而批量大小对二者影响复杂，需要理论分析其权衡关系。

Method: 针对高斯协变量的一致线性回归问题，建立了随机HB和ASGD在有限维离散时间下的批量大小权衡下界，并通过合成线性回归实验进行验证。

Result: HB不会改善SGD的计算效率边界，但允许更大批量窗口（SGD临界批量的√κ倍）来减少串行运行时间；ASGD在快速衰减幂律谱下改善小批量计算效率，但随着批量增大将计算效率优势转化为串行运行时间优势。

Conclusion: 动量方法的批量大小权衡高度依赖于谱分布特性：HB提供稳定的批量窗口扩展，ASGD则呈现谱依赖的CE-串行时间权衡。

Abstract: Stochastic momentum methods such as heavy ball (HB), Nesterov momentum, and variants of Accelerated SGD (ASGD) [Kidambi et al., 2018] are widely used in modern training, but their stochastic benefits depend on two distinct quantities: serial runtime, the number of iterations needed to reach a target accuracy, and compute efficiency (CE), the inverse total gradient-query or FLOP cost. Larger batches reduce serial runtime without hurting CE only when the contraction gap grows linearly with batch size.
  We study stochastic HB and ASGD for consistent linear regression with Gaussian covariates and prove finite-dimensional, discrete-time lower bounds on their batch-size tradeoffs. Our first result shows that HB does not improve the CE frontier over SGD for arbitrary spectra; rather, it preserves SGD-level CE over a larger batch-size window, allowing larger batches to reduce serial runtime until HB reaches its deterministic accelerated scale. This window can be a factor $\sqrtκ$ larger than the SGD critical batch size. For ASGD, the picture is more spectrum-dependent: for rapidly decaying power-law spectra, ASGD improves small-batch CE over HB/SGD, but as batch size grows it trades this CE advantage for improved serial runtime. Synthetic linear-regression experiments verify these qualitative regimes, including near-overlap of ASGD and HB for slowly decaying spectra and the predicted CE--serial tradeoff for rapidly decaying spectra.

</details>


### [65] [SAGE: Retain-Aware Post-Hoc Sanitization of Final Unlearning Vector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18309)
*Jingyuan Zhang,Yucheng Bai,Peixi Wen,Zhehao Huang,Zhengbao He,Hanling Tian,Xinwen Cheng,Haiyin Ran,Xiaolin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE方法，通过后置修正最终更新向量来缓解LLM遗忘中的保留-遗忘权衡，无需重新运行原始遗忘流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM遗忘方法都面临保留能力与遗忘效果之间的权衡困境，且一旦完成遗忘过程，受损的保留性能难以恢复。

Method: 提出SAGE（Spectral Activation-GEometry Sanitization）：收集小型保留代理的真实模块输入，提取其主导激活几何结构，通过封闭形式求解源锚定优化目标，抑制与高能量保留方向对齐的更新分量，同时保留源方法的遗忘载体。

Result: 在多种遗忘方法、模型规模和基准测试上，SAGE持续缓解了保留-遗忘权衡，证明了最终向量后置修正作为机器遗忘中实用且未被充分探索的维度。

Conclusion: 发现保留激活偏差可量化遗忘方法对保留的损伤，提出后置修正范式无需重新运行原始遗忘流程即可修复保留性能，SAGE作为源无关的修正方法有效缓解了权衡问题。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) unlearning aims to remove undesirable knowledge or behaviors while preserving retained capabilities. Current unlearning methods all involve a trade-off between unlearning and retention. We have found that the retention activation bias can also be used to quantify the damage an unlearning method inflicts on retention, without considering the specific implementation of the unlearning process. This allows us to restore retention performance for any unlearning method using a post-hoc approach. Therefore, we propose a complementary post-hoc setting to sanitize the final update vector without rerunning the original unlearning pipeline. In this setting, we design SAGE, Spectral Activation-GEometry Sanitization, a source-agnostic correction for final unlearning updates. SAGE collects real module inputs from a small retain proxy, extracts their dominant activation geometry, and solves a source-anchored optimization objective in closed form, which suppresses update components aligned with high-energy retained directions while preserving the source method's forgetting carrier. Across multiple unlearning methods, model scales, and benchmarks, SAGE consistently relieves the retain-forget trade-off, identifying post-hoc sanitization of final vectors as a practical and underexplored axis for machine unlearning.

</details>


### [66] [Ghost Attractor Networks: Basin-Structured Dynamical Decoders for Closed-Loop Sequential Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18315)
*Tianyu Wang,Ying Wang,Zhihao Liu,Xi Vincent Wang,Lihui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Ghost Attractor Networks，一种受理论推导的动力学解码器，其潜变量在具有漂移的学习势能下演化，通过构造产生盆地吸引子结构，满足多模态、解码器级单步切换和恒定内存三个需求。实验表明，230万参数的 Ghost 在离线精度上超越10.7亿参数的扩散Transformer，参数量减少462倍、延迟降低32倍，并在LIBERO-10闭环基准上达到95.7%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模Transformer和扩散解码器在顺序输出生成中面临随序列长度增长的内存成本和迭代逐步计算的开销；而小型前馈解码器虽恢复效率但产生非结构化的潜变量表示，限制了闭环控制（如相位条件动作生成和跨步潜变量传递需要具有稳定盆地的潜几何结构）。为此，本文希望设计一种既能保持高效解码又能提供结构化潜变量空间（盆地吸引子结构）的动力学解码器。

Method: 提出Ghost Attractor Networks：1) 理论推导潜变量在具有漂移的学习势能下演化，通过构造形成盆地吸引子结构；2) 模式转换表现为鞍结分岔（saddle-node bifurcation）伴鬼吸引子逃逸；3) 层级相空间分解分离一阶盆地收敛和二阶本体感受精化；4) 使用行为克隆和对比目标进行端到端训练。

Result: 1) 势能梯度范数在5个积分步内衰减67%，验证了梯度流收缩；2) 230万参数的Ghost离线精度匹配10.7亿参数扩散Transformer，参数量减少462倍、延迟降低32倍；3) 在离线MSE上优于5种2M参数解码器（MLP、神经ODE、CVAE、Transformer、1步扩散）5.9%-29%；4) LIBERO-10闭环基准中，相位条件化比MLP基线提升13.5个百分点成功率，持久潜变量集成达到95.7%最终成功率。

Conclusion: Ghost Attractor Networks通过理论驱动的势能-漂移设计，在极小参数规模下实现了与超大扩散Transformer相当的离线精度，并在闭环控制中凭借盆地吸引子结构显著优于前馈基线，验证了结构化潜变量空间对机器人动作解码和闭环策略的重要性。

Abstract: Sequential output generation with large-scale Transformer and diffusion decoders pays a memory cost that grows with sequence length, plus iterative per-step computation. Replacing them with small feed-forward decoders restores efficiency but produces unstructured latent representations that limit closed-loop control: phase-conditioned action generation and cross-step latent carry-over both require a latent geometry with stable basins. This article proposes Ghost Attractor Networks, a theoretically derived dynamical decoder whose latent evolves under a learned potential with drift and produces a basin-attractor structure by construction. Three desiderata (multi-modality, decoder-level single-pass switching, and constant memory) motivate the potential-drift form, and mode transitions arise as saddle-node bifurcations with ghost-attractor escape. A hierarchical phase-space decomposition separates first-order basin convergence from second-order proprioceptive refinement. Empirically, a Ghost trained end-to-end with a behavioral-cloning and contrastive objective exhibits the predicted gradient-flow contraction in its potential, with the gradient norm decaying by 67 percent across five integration steps on 1430 held-out samples. Ghost is evaluated as a robotic action decoder. A 2.3-million-parameter Ghost matches the offline accuracy of a 1.07-billion-parameter Diffusion Transformer at 462 times fewer parameters and 32 times lower latency, and beats five alternative 2M-parameter decoders (MLP, Neural ODE, CVAE, Transformer, 1-step Diffusion) on offline mean squared error by 5.9 to 29 percent. On the LIBERO-10 closed-loop benchmark, phase conditioning on Ghost's basin-structured latent yields a 13.5 percentage-point success-rate gain over a feed-forward MLP baseline, and persistent-latent ensembling reaches a 95.7 percent final success rate.

</details>


### [67] [A Survey on Data-Driven Models for Soil Moisture Regression and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18316)
*Ilektra Tsimpidi,George Georgoulas,Vidya Sumathy,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于人工智能的土壤湿度建模方法，将现有方法分为五类：统计时间序列、地统计学、经典机器学习、深度学习和概率/贝叶斯方法，利用多源环境数据进行回归或分类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土壤湿度建模是一个复杂的时空学习问题，物理模型存在计算成本高和可扩展性有限的问题，而数据驱动的AI方法提供了灵活替代方案，因此需要系统梳理现有AI方法。

Method: 将AI模型分为五类：(a)统计时间序列模型、(b)地统计学方法、(c)经典机器学习模型、(d)深度学习模型、(e)概率/贝叶斯方法，利用历史土壤湿度记录、气象变量、植被指数、地形、土壤特征和地理位置数据进行回归或分类。

Result: 提供了一份结构化的土壤湿度估计与分类AI模型综述，涵盖五类方法的框架性梳理。

Conclusion: AI方法在土壤湿度建模中具有灵活性和有效性，该综述为相关研究提供了系统性的方法分类和参考框架。

Abstract: Soil Moisture (SM) modelling constitutes a complex spatiotemporal learning problem characterised by nonlinear environmental interactions, heterogeneous data sources, and limited ground observations. Physics-based approaches, such as water balance models, rely on explicit hydrological equations and high-quality inputs, but their computational cost and scalability limitations restrict large-scale deployment. Data-driven artificial intelligence (AI) methods have emerged as flexible alternatives, enabling the extraction of empirical relationships between soil moisture and environmental variables with reduced modelling assumptions. This work presents a structured survey of AI-based models for soil moisture estimation and classification. Existing approaches are organized into five categories: (a) statistical time-series models, (b) geostatistical methods (c) classical machine learning (ML) models, (d) Deep Learning (DL) models and (e) Probabilistic/Bayesian methods. These models leverage historical soil moisture records, meteorological variables, vegetation indices, topography, soil characteristics, and geolocation data to perform regression or classification tasks.

</details>


### [68] [Enhanced Graph Neural Networks using K-Hop Gaussian Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18317)
*Xuling Zhang,Peng Wang,Daiyan Li,Aoran Huang,Zeiwei Chen,Yongkui Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种K跳高斯扩散核（KHG）作为图数据预处理模块，通过多跳扩散和高斯权重平衡局部与全局信息传播，在噪声或结构复杂图上优于传统消息传递GNN及PPR、Heat Kernel扩散方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN基于消息传递仅聚合单跳邻居，难以处理真实图中边噪声大、结构复杂的问题；PPR和Heat Kernel等扩散核虽能全局传播，但处理复杂局部结构和远处节点噪声时仍存在局限。

Method: 提出K-Hop Gaussian (KHG)扩散核作为图数据预处理模块，采用多跳扩散机制并对远程节点施加高斯权重，在应用标准GNN前实现局部与全局信息传播的平衡。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，KHG显著优于传统消息传递GNN以及PPR和Heat Kernel扩散方法，尤其在噪声或结构复杂图中表现突出。

Conclusion: KHG扩散核作为预处理模块能有效解决GNN在复杂图结构上的传播局限，是一种实用的图数据增强方法。

Abstract: Most graph neural network (GNN) cores rely on graph convolutions, typically implemented as message passing between direct (single-hop) neighbors. In many real-world graphs, edges can be noisy or poorly defined, limiting information propagation to local neighborhoods. Existing diffusion kernels, such as Personalized PageRank (PPR) and Heat Kernel, alleviate this issue through global propagation, but still struggle with complex local structures and distant node noise. To address these limitations, we propose a K-Hop Gaussian (KHG) diffusion kernel as a preprocessing module for graph data. KHG introduces multi-hop diffusion with Gaussian weighting for remote nodes, balancing local and global information propagation before applying standard GNNs. Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that KHG significantly outperforms traditional message-passing GNNs, as well as PPR and Heat Kernel diffusion, particularly in noisy or structurally complex graphs.

</details>


### [69] [The Implicit Bias of Steepest Descent with Mini-batch Stochastic Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11557)
*Jichu Li,Xuan Tang,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多分类问题中 mini-batch 随机最速下降法的隐式偏差，分析了批量大小、动量和方差减少如何在一般 entry-wise 和 Schatten-p 范数下影响最大间隔行为和收敛速度。无动量时仅 full-batch 可保证收敛；动量通过 batch-momentum 权衡使小批量收敛到近似最大间隔解；方差减少可恢复 full-batch 隐式偏差但速度更慢；纯随机更新（batch-size-1）会收敛到根本不同的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解随机最速下降法在不同范数诱导几何下的隐式偏差，特别是批量大小、动量和方差减少如何影响分类器的最大间隔行为和收敛性，以阐明随机优化何时与 full-batch 行为对齐。

Method: 理论分析框架：研究 mini-batch 随机最速下降法在一般 entry-wise 和 Schatten-p 范数下的多分类问题，分别考察无动量、有动量、方差减少以及 batch-size-1 四种设置下的收敛性和隐式偏差。

Result: 1) 无动量时只有 full-batch 能保证最坏情况收敛和分类成功；2) 动量通过 batch-momentum 权衡使小批量收敛到近似最大间隔解，但收敛速度变慢；3) 方差减少可以恢复 exact full-batch 隐式偏差，无论批量大小，但收敛更慢；4) batch-size-1 无动量最速下降收敛到完全不同的偏差。

Conclusion: 该统一分析框架明确了随机优化与 full-batch 行为对齐的条件，为深入探索随机梯度最速下降算法的训练行为奠定了基础。

Abstract: A variety of widely used optimization methods like SignSGD and Muon can be interpreted as instances of steepest descent under different norm-induced geometries. In this work, we study the implicit bias of mini-batch stochastic steepest descent in multi-class classification, characterizing how batch size, momentum, and variance reduction shape the limiting max-margin behavior and convergence rates under general entry-wise and Schatten-$p$ norms. We show that, without momentum, worst-case convergence and successful classification can only be guaranteed with full-batch gradient. In contrast, momentum enables small-batch convergence to an approximate max-margin solution through a batch-momentum trade-off, though it slows convergence. This approach provides fully explicit, dimension-free rates that improve upon prior results. Moreover, we prove that variance reduction can recover the exact full-batch implicit bias for any batch size, albeit at a slower convergence rate. Finally, we further investigate the batch-size-one steepest descent without momentum, and reveal its convergence to a fundamentally different bias via a concrete data example, which reveals a key limitation of purely stochastic updates. Overall, our unified analysis clarifies when stochastic optimization aligns with full-batch behavior, and paves the way for perform deeper explorations of the training behavior of stochastic gradient steepest descent algorithms.

</details>


### [70] [ASTRA: A Scalable Next-Generation ATCO Training Simulator with Autonomous Simpilots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18319)
*Ethan Chew,Enjia Wu,Iruss Eng Wei Yeow,Ian Weiqin Lim,Ranen Sim,Brandon Koh Ziheng,Kaleb Nim,Caden Toh Jun Yi,Wei Dong Soin,Darius Kai Keat Koh,Galen King Yu Tay,Prannaya Gupta,Jonathan Ee Fang Koong,Yong Zhi Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASTRA是一个端到端训练模拟器，通过自动化模拟飞行员和ATC角色、微调本地化语音识别模型（WER从107.80%降至23.45%）以及集成AI评估框架（准确性91.7%、简洁性88.2%、完整性86.9%），解决了新加坡语境下空管培训中依赖专业模拟员和西方语音模型表现差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空管培训高度依赖称为simpilot的专业人类模拟员，他们需要同时扮演飞行员和ATC角色，导致培训容量受限；同时现有的自动化解决方案使用基于西方口音的语音模型，在新加坡运营环境下表现极差（WER高达107.80%），无法满足实际需求。

Method: 构建ASTRA端到端训练模拟器，包含语音转录、指令解析和响应生成管道；采用微调后的本地化ASR模型；基于DSPy和Unsloth等开源框架；集成AI辅助性能评估框架，从准确性、简洁性和完整性三个维度评估受训者的通信表现。

Result: 微调ASR管道将WER降低至23.45%，显著优于现有方案；AI评估框架在优化后分别达到91.7%（准确性）、88.2%（简洁性）和86.9%（完整性）的评分。

Conclusion: ASTRA实现了可扩展、标准化的空管评估，减少了教员工作负担，为新加坡语境下的空管培训提供了一种有效的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Air Traffic Control Operators (ATCOs) are vital in ensuring the safe, orderly, and efficient flow of air traffic, yet training capacity is constrained by reliance on specialized human trainers known as simpilots, who must role-play both pilots and ATCOs in a simulated airspace. Existing automated solutions rely on Western-centric speech models that perform poorly in Singaporean operational contexts, with off-the-shelf systems exhibiting Word Error Rates (WER) of up to 107.80% on Singaporean-accented aviation speech. We introduce ASTRA, an end-to-end training simulator that automates these simpilot roles through a pipeline that transcribes ATCO speech, interprets instructions, and generates appropriate pilot and ATCO responses using locally adapted voice models. Our fine-tuned Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) pipeline reduces WER to 23.45%, substantially outperforming existing approaches in this domain. Beyond traffic simulation, ASTRA incorporates an AI-assisted performance evaluation framework that assesses trainee radiotelephony communications across accuracy, brevity, and completeness, achieving post-optimization scores of 91.7%, 88.2%, and 86.9%, respectively. Built on open-source foundations such as DSPy and Unsloth, this approach enables scalable, standardized ATCO assessment while reducing instructor workload.

</details>


### [71] [SAE Interventions are Unreliable: Post-Intervention Recovery of Suppressed Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18322)
*Mingyue Cui,Linghui Shen,Xingyi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示稀疏自编码器（SAE）存在一个可恢复性失败模式：即使成功干预了目标SAE特征（如钳制有害特征），模型行为仍可通过残差空间优化恢复。实验表明，在安全关键的拒绝引导场景中，恢复率达95.8%，而目标特征漂移仅0.131。归因分析将恢复定位到SAE重构残差——SAE未能解释的残差分量。这暴露了特征级控制与行为完备性之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SAE的潜在空间防御假设识别出的"不安全"特征可作为可靠的行为控制手柄，但该假设是否成立尚缺乏验证。作者旨在揭示SAE特征干预可能存在的可恢复性失败模式。

Method: 提出后干预恢复（post-intervention recovery）框架，将其形式化为约束残差空间优化问题。在干预保持激活的威胁模型下，通过编码器正交更新（单层）和特征映射Jacobian（跨层）优化残差扰动，同时保持目标SAE特征值不变。

Result: 在TPP、遗忘、IOI和拒绝引导实验中均观察到可恢复行为。安全关键场景下恢复率达95.8%，防御特征相对漂移仅0.131，远低于基于后缀的基线。归因分析表明恢复主要来自SAE重构残差分量。

Conclusion: SAE特征支持因果干预，但控制特征并不保证控制底层行为。特征级控制与行为完备性之间存在根本性差距，这对基于SAE的安全防御提出警示。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) decompose residual-stream activations into interpretable features. Recent latent-space defenses increasingly rely on these decompositions, assuming that identified "unsafe" SAE features serve as actionable handles for monitoring and intervention. In this paradigm, clamping a specific harmful feature is expected to reliably prevent model misbehavior. However, we show that this success may hide a recoverable failure mode: the clamp may block one visible route to a behavior without eliminating the behavior itself. We formulate this vulnerability as post-intervention recovery, a constrained residual-space optimization problem. Starting from the post-intervention residual state, we optimize residual perturbations to recover the pre-intervention behavior while preserving the post-intervention values of the targeted SAE features. Even under a strong threat model where the intervention remains active throughout optimization and generation, recovery remains possible. To rule out that recovery simply undoes the intervention, we use encoder-orthogonal updates for single-layer interventions and the corresponding feature-map Jacobian in the cross-layer setting. Across TPP, unlearning, IOI, and refusal steering experiments, this stress test reveals recoverable behavior despite successful feature-level intervention. Especially in the safety-critical refusal-steering setting, we achieve a 95.8% recovery rate on valid samples while keeping defended-feature relative drift to 0.131, substantially below suffix-based baselines. A recovery-path attribution analysis further localizes this recovery to the SAE reconstruction residual, the component left unexplained by the SAE. These results expose a gap between feature-level control and behavioral completeness: SAE features can support causal intervention, but controlling them does not guarantee control over the underlying behavior.

</details>


### [72] [Measurement noise limits the advantage of nonlinear models over linear models in biomedical prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18420)
*Marc-Andre Schulz,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出在生物医学表格数据中，灵活模型（如深度网络、梯度提升树、核方法）常被线性/逻辑回归匹敌或超越，原因在于测量噪声而非模型缺陷：噪声会先抹除非线性结构（高阶交互项衰减更快），导致非线性优势消失，且无法通过更多数据或更复杂模型恢复，只能通过改进测量解决。通过140个UK Biobank任务验证了这一噪声特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何在生物医学表格数据中，灵活模型（深度网络、梯度提升树等）反复被线性/逻辑回归匹敌或超越这一反常现象，指出根本原因是测量噪声而非模型能力不足。

Method: 整合测量误差统计、心理测量学和高斯分析中的经典结论，推导出精确的超额风险恒等式；在140个UK Biobank任务上进行实验验证。

Result: k阶交互项被特征可靠性的k次幂衰减，而线性部分仅被衰减一次；在典型生物医学测量信度下，即使底层生物学存在强非线性，非线性优势也会消失；140个UK Biobank任务中灵活模型与线性模型的差距携带了预测的噪声特征。

Conclusion: 非线性被隐藏而非不存在；线性与灵活模型持平不能作为对生物学的判断；测量可靠性、样本量和特征表示三个条件必须同时满足，灵活模型才能发挥作用，而大多数生物医学任务落在这个狭窄窗口之外。

Abstract: On biomedical tabular data, flexible models such as deep networks, gradient-boosted trees, and kernel methods are repeatedly matched or beaten by linear and logistic regression given the same features. The usual reaction is to treat this as a model-side shortfall, to be fixed with more data, a better architecture, or tuning, on the assumption that the nonlinear structure is there and the model has failed to capture it. We argue that these fixes cannot help when the binding limit is the measurement rather than the model, as it frequently is in biomedicine. Additive noise blurs the population-optimal predictor, and because blurring removes a function's fine, rapidly varying detail before its broad shape, it erases nonlinear structure faster than linear structure. A degree-$k$ interaction is attenuated by the $k$-th power of feature reliability, while the linear part is attenuated only once. At the reliabilities typical of biomedical measurement, the nonlinear advantage can vanish even when the underlying biology is strongly nonlinear, and what the noise removes cannot be recovered by a larger cohort or a more flexible model, only by better measurement. The nonlinearity is hidden, not absent, and a tie between linear and flexible models is not by itself a verdict on the biology. These pieces are classical, drawn from measurement-error statistics, psychometrics, and Gaussian analysis, and we assemble them into an exact excess-risk identity. Measurement reliability is one of three conditions, alongside sample size and feature representation, that must align for a flexible model to help, and together they leave only a narrow window that most biomedical tasks fall outside. Across 140 UK Biobank tasks, the gap between flexible and linear models, where it exists, carries the predicted noise signature, and the three conditions can be separated by intervention but not by a benchmark alone.

</details>


### [73] [Why SWAVE May Not Be All You Need:A Concept-Evolution Retrospective on Complex-Valued Recurrent Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18324)
*Ramprasath Ganesaraja,Swathika N,Sahil Dilip Panse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SWave是一个169.26M参数的复数值循环语言模型，基于复数波表示、Cayley参数化酉变换和旋转隐藏状态三大设计原则。经历了三个开发阶段，解决了cos-domination collapse问题，最终实现了稳定训练（最佳PPL 22.0），并总结了六个可迁移的复数值循环训练工程原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索复数域表示在语言模型中的潜力，认为复数波相比实数值能编码更丰富的信息，且通过Cayley参数化的酉变换可数学上保证状态不会衰减或爆炸，旋转隐藏状态能在任意长上下文中保持信号完整性。

Method: 基于复数波表示、Cayley参数化酉变换和旋转隐藏状态三大前提设计SWave。经历了三个开发阶段：最初使用Resonance Head但发现其存在cos-domination collapse（虚部通道坍塌）；第二阶段采用Phase-Associative Memory (PAM)架构的解耦头，使用独立的实部和虚部嵌入表解决了该问题；最终架构保留了ComplexNorm、Wave Propagation Scan，将ProtectGatedScan作为结构先验而非学习行为，用实数值squared-ReLU通道混合器替代ComplexGatedUnit。多尺度保留概念和辅助训练目标被证明无效。

Result: 成功训练了200,000步，最佳PPL 22.0（第89,861步）。给出了cos-domination collapse的形式化表征，开发了对数空间反向传播的并行扫描以实现数值稳定性，总结了六个可迁移的工程原则，以及用于捕获结构偏离的plan-to-code可追溯性方法。

Conclusion: 复数域循环语言模型是可行的，但需要精心设计架构避免cos-domination collapse等退化模式。六个工程原则和可追溯性方法对复数值循环训练具有普适意义。

Abstract: SWave is a complex-valued recurrent language model (169.26M parameters, D=384, L=16, T=2048) trained on FineWeb-Edu using 2xH100 NVL. It was designed around three founding premises: that representing language as complex waves rather than real-valued numbers enables richer information encoding; that a Cayley-parameterised unitary transition provides a mathematical guarantee against state decay or explosion; and that a hidden state which rotates rather than shrinks preserves signal integrity over arbitrarily long contexts. The core of SWave evolved substantially across three development phases. The Resonance Head was found to structurally admit imaginary-channel collapse as a global loss minimum (a failure mode we term cos-domination collapse) and was superseded by an untied head with independent real and imaginary embedding tables from the Phase-Associative Memory (PAM) architecture. This resolved the degenerate minimum and enabled stable 200,000-step training (best-step PPL 22.0 at step 89,861). ComplexNorm and the Wave Propagation Scan proved load-bearing throughout all three phases and were retained to the final architecture. ProtectGatedScan was reframed as a structural prior rather than a learned behaviour. The four multi-scale retention concepts showed no measurable improvement under controlled evaluation and were found non-load-bearing. The ComplexGatedUnit was superseded by a real-valued squared-ReLU channel mixer with fewer parameters. The auxiliary training objectives showed no benefit once structural constraints were resolved. The investigation yields a formal characterisation of cos-domination collapse, a parallel scan with a log-space backward pass for numerical stability, six transferable engineering principles for complex-valued recurrent training, and a plan-to-code traceability methodology for catching structural divergences that conventional test suites miss.

</details>


### [74] [Quantum Annealing Enhanced Reinforcement Learning for Accurate Remaining Useful Lifetime Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18503)
*Manoranjan Gandhudi,Arunkumar V.,G. R. Anil,Gangadharan G. R*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种量子退火增强的Q-Learning（QAQL）框架，用于工业预测性维护中的剩余使用寿命（RUL）估计。该框架将量子退火的采样行为与Q-learning的序列决策相结合，通过在D-Wave Advantage系统上求解QUBO问题来优化动作选择，在NASA C-MAPSS涡轮风扇发动机数据集和设备群预测性维护数据集上显著优于经典和量子基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计退化模型无法捕捉真实系统中的强非线性，而数据驱动模型在高维非凸搜索空间中容易收敛到次优解。现有方法在处理非线性退化轨迹时存在局限性，需要一种更好的优化方法。

Method: 提出QAQL框架，将每个Q值更新编码为小型QUBO问题，通过D-Wave Advantage系统上的量子退火求解。量子退火器不充当确定性优化器，而是通过多次读取返回近最优动作的分布，这种随机动作选择提供探索机制，抑制非线性退化轨迹上的早熟收敛。

Result: 在两个公开基准（NASA C-MAPSS涡轮风扇发动机数据集和设备群预测性维护数据集）上验证，在多次独立运行和六种误差指标上，QAQL的性能均优于经典和量子基线方法，且改进具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 量子退火不仅是理论上的优化器，而且是可以在工业预测性维护应用的强化学习循环中实际使用的优化器。

Abstract: Remaining useful life (RUL) estimation is central to predictive maintenance, where an unplanned failure can cost far more than the asset itself. Statistical degradation models miss the strong nonlinearity of real systems, and data-driven models often converge to suboptimal solutions in high-dimensional, non-convex search spaces. We propose a Quantum Annealing enhanced Q-Learning (QAQL) framework that couples the sampling behaviour of quantum annealing with the sequential decision making of Q-learning. Each Q-value update is encoded as a small quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) whose ground state is the greedy action; rather than acting as a deterministic optimizer, the annealer returns a distribution over near-optimal actions across many reads, and this stochastic action selection supplies the exploration that curbs premature convergence on nonlinear degradation trajectories. The QUBO is solved on the D-Wave Advantage system using minor embedding, with the annealer woven into the reinforcement-learning loop rather than bolted on after training. We validate QAQL on two public benchmarks: the NASA C-MAPSS turbofan engine datasets and a device-fleet predictive maintenance dataset. Averaged over many independent runs and across six error metrics, QAQL outperforms the classical and quantum baselines considered in this study, with statistically significant improvements. The results indicate that quantum annealing is a usable, not merely theoretical, optimizer inside a reinforcement-learning loop for industrial predictive-maintenance applications.

</details>


### [75] [Neural Network Implementation of the Renormalization Group for Fault Diagnosis with Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18326)
*Evgeny Nikulchev,Dmitry Ilin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RGNet，一种基于重正化群概念的神经网络架构，通过对特征空间进行分层粗粒化处理，解决机器学习的类不平衡和多维噪声问题。该模型依次压缩输入维度并在分类前连接所有尺度，从而捕获局部细节和全局模式。引入RG流作为可解释的低维表示，并在AI4I数据集上验证了其在故障预测中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在实际任务中面临类不平衡和多维噪声的挑战，需要一种兼顾局部细节与全局模式、具有可解释性的通用解决方案。

Method: 提出RGNet，基于重正化群概念对特征空间进行分层粗粒化；依次压缩输入维度并在分类前连接所有尺度（多尺度融合）；引入RG流作为可解释的低维表示。

Result: 在不平衡AI4I数据集上验证，t-SNE可视化揭示离散曲线结构，证实粗粒化有效性；RGNet在故障预测任务中表现具有竞争力。

Conclusion: RGNet是一种通用、可解释且有竞争力的解决方案，适用于不平衡类别下的故障预测应用。

Abstract: The application of machine learning models in practical tasks faces challenges such as class imbalance and multidimensional noise. This paper proposes RGNet, a neural network architecture based on the concept of the renormalization group (RG), for hierarchical coarse-graining of the feature space. The model sequentially compresses the input dimensionality and concatenates all scales before classification, allowing it to capture both local details and global patterns. The notion of RG-flows is introduced - interpretable low-dimensional representations whose visualization via t-SNE reveals a discrete curvilinear structure confirming the effectiveness of coarse-graining. Experimental results are presented on the imbalanced AI4I dataset. The obtained results demonstrate that RGNet is a universal, interpretable, and competitive solution for fault prediction in applications with imbalanced classes.

</details>


### [76] [Concept Modulation Models: A Unified Framework for Identifiability and Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18509)
*Soheun Yi,Yizhou Lu,Chandler Squires,Pradeep Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出概念调制模型（CMMs），通过属性势（attribute potentials）统一刻画条件潜变量模型的可识别性（identifiability）与外推（extrapolation）条件，将现有模型特定的分析统一为基于势的代数判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件潜变量模型的可识别性与外推保证大多针对特定模型（如非线性ICA、因果表示学习、扰动建模），缺乏统一理论框架。作者希望建立一个通用框架，将可识别性的"提升"（lifting）步骤与外推的代数条件分离，从而统一理解并推广已有结果。

Method: 提出CMMs（概念调制模型），结构为A→Λ→C→X（属性→调制器→潜概念律→观测特征）。通过属性势（log密度比）将特征一致性从观测属性提升到潜变量空间，并利用该势的代数性质建立外推判据。

Result: CMMs将基于转移的可识别性推广到条件设置；属性势同时控制可识别性和外推；外推成立当且仅当属性势等式可扩展到未见属性；恢复并统一了多个现有模型特定的可识别性与外推结论。

Conclusion: CMMs为条件潜变量模型提供了统一的分析框架，可识别性和外推共享同一套基于属性势的证明对象（proof objects），模型特异性仅体现在刚性（rigidity）参数上。

Abstract: Reliable generalization in conditional latent variable models requires understanding both identifiability and extrapolation: how observed variation across attributes determines latent structure, and how that structure determines distributions at unseen attributes. However, existing identifiability and extrapolation guarantees are largely model-specific, with separate analyses in nonlinear ICA, causal representation learning, perturbation modeling, and related conditional latent variable models. We introduce concept modulation models (CMMs), an attribute-indexed class of conditional generative models with structure $A\to Λ\to C\to X$, where attributes select modulators, modulators induce latent concept laws, and concepts generate observed features. CMMs lift transition-based identifiability to conditional settings by showing that feature agreement on observed attributes induces a latent concept transition constrained by the CMM class. We express these constraints through attribute potentials, log-density ratios between attribute-conditioned concept laws, separating the generic lifting step from model-specific rigidity arguments. The same potentials control extrapolation: agreement at unseen attributes holds exactly when the transported attribute-potential identities extend to those attributes. This yields algebraic extrapolation criteria, identifies the common potential-based proof objects behind several existing identifiability and extrapolation results, and, when combined with the model-specific rigidity arguments in those works, recovers their stated conclusions.

</details>


### [77] [Self-CTRL: Self-Consistency Training with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18327)
*Itamar Pres,Laura Ruis,Melat Ghebreselassie,Belinda Z. Li,Jacob Andreas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-CTRL方法，通过强化学习优化语言模型的自我解释与行为之间的一致性，在概率推理和宪法AI两个领域显著提升了模型的可信度、透明度和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型通常无法准确描述自身行为，导致用户难以审计、理解和信任模型。现有方法缺乏一种通用机制来对齐模型的自解释与其实际行为。

Method: 提出Self-Consistency Training with Reinforcement Learning (Self-CTRL)，通过强化学习优化模型自我解释与行为之间的一致性：一方面更新解释以更好地预测行为，另一方面更新行为以更好地匹配解释。

Result: 在概率推理任务中，自报告偏差与行为测量偏差的相关性从R²=0.24提升至0.64；在宪法AI任务中，第三方审计者的拒绝预测准确率从36%提升至92%，HarmBench失败率从15.0%降至0.5%。

Conclusion: Self-CTRL提供了一种通用框架，通过对齐解释与行为来训练更安全、更透明、更可控的AI模型。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) that faithfully describe their own behavior can more easily be audited, understood, and trusted by users. This paper describes Self-Consistency Training with Reinforcement Learning (Self-CTRL), a method that optimizes for consistency between a LM's self-explanations and behavior on related inputs by updating explanations to better predict behavior or updating behavior to better match explanations. We apply our method in two domains. First, we study a formal probabilistic reasoning task in which LMs must learn to imitate a family of biased samplers and evaluated on their ability to report the associated biases. We find that consistency training improves the correlation between self-reported and behaviorally-measured latent biases from $R^2=0.24$ to $R^2=0.64$ on a set of held-out distributions, matching the generalization of direct ground-truth supervision. Second, we study a constitutional AI domain in which LMs must describe when they will refuse or comply with user requests. Here, Self-CTRL produces rules that faithfully describe the model's behavior on held-out requests, improving the refusal predictions of a third-party auditor model from $36\%$ to $92\%$. In the other direction, behavior updates improve alignment, reducing HarmBench failure rate from $15.0\%$ to $0.5\%$ without substantially increasing refusal on harmless prompts. By aligning explanations and behavior, our work provides a general recipe for training AI models to be safer, more transparent, and more controllable.

</details>


### [78] [Effects of sparsity and superposition on loss in simple autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18538)
*Mriganka Basu Roy Chowdhury,Eric McLaughlin Weiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对神经网络中叠加现象（superposition）的数学基础进行了分析，针对幂激活函数，在稀疏输入条件下给出了L2重建损失的上下界，并验证了Elhage等人（2022）的部分发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络机制可解释性面临的主要困难之一是多义性（polysemanticity），即单个神经元通常负责多个不同任务，阻碍了对网络功能的清晰解释。Elhage等人（2022）认为这是由于叠加现象造成的，但缺乏严格的数学分析。

Method: 采用数学分析方法，针对具有稀疏输入的自编码器，对幂激活函数下的L2重建损失推导了上下界，并在极稀疏条件下证明这些界是紧的。

Result: 在极稀疏条件下，对于幂激活函数，给出了L2重建损失的紧的上下界，严格验证了叠加现象的部分经验发现。

Conclusion: 本文为叠加现象的最优性提供了数学基础，并指出了若干值得进一步研究的开放问题。

Abstract: One of the major difficulties in the mechanistic interpretability of neural networks is the occurrence of polysemanticity, which suggests that each neuron is typically responsible for multiple different tasks, impeding a clean interpretation of their function. The seminal paper of Elhage et al. (2022) argues that this occurs due to superposition, a phenomenon where the neural network represents distinct features as non-orthogonal directions in a lower-dimensional space, a strategy that allows much greater compression of the data without sacrificing fidelity due to the feature sparsity of input vectors. Elhage et al. (2022) empirically validates these hypotheses in a rather natural and simple autoencoder with sparse inputs.
