The contribution of the present work is to analyze the mathematical basis for the occurrence and optimality of superposition, while rigorously corroborating some of their findings. In particular, we provide upper and lower bounds for the L2 reconstruction loss, tight in the very sparse regime, for power activation functions. A short list of interesting open problems are also included at the end.

</details>


### [79] [ThousandWorlds: A benchmark for climate emulation of potentially habitable exoplanets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18338)
*Edward T. Stevenson,Mei Ting Mak,Eric Wolf,Denis E. Sergeev,Tobi Hammond,N. J. Mayne,Miles Cranmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ThousandWorlds是一个面向系外行星气候仿真的机器学习基准数据集，包含来自五个全球气候模型的约1800次模拟，将8个行星参数映射到3D大气场。该基准定义了三组难度递增的子任务和两种评估协议。实验表明，高斯过程方法表现最佳，而现成的深度学习方法在该低数据、多模拟器回归任务中尚未成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解读系外行星大气中的生物标志物信号需要理解行星气候，但全球气候模型（GCM）的单个运行需要数百万核心小时和大量专家时间。机器学习仿真器有望消除这一瓶颈，但缺乏一个经过整理的多模型系外气候数据集限制了相关进展。

Method: 构建了一个包含约1800个模拟、来自五个GCM的数据集，将8个行星参数映射到3D大气场（温度、湿度、风、云、辐射）。定义了三组嵌套子任务：单模拟器回归、完整观测的多模拟器回归、结构化缺失的多模拟器回归。提出两种评估协议：方法排序和相对于GCM间不一致性的性能度量。评估了七种基线方法，包括简单方法、深度学习和高斯过程。

Result: 基于高斯过程的方法表现最佳，表明ThousandWorlds揭示了一个现成深度学习尚无法成功应对的低数据、多模拟器回归场景。

Conclusion: ThousandWorlds为系外气候仿真和低数据、多模拟器、参数到场回归领域提供了一个标准化的ML基准，有助于推动机器学习在该领域的应用。

Abstract: The search for life beyond Earth will depend on detecting faint signatures in the atmospheres of potentially habitable exoplanets. Interpreting those signatures requires understanding the host planet's climate: the same molecule may signal life on one planet and abiotic chemistry on another. Global climate models (GCMs) provide this understanding, but individual runs can require up to millions of core-hours and substantial domain expert time. Machine-learning emulators could remove this bottleneck, but progress has been limited by the absence of a curated, multi-model exoclimate dataset. We introduce ThousandWorlds, an ML-ready benchmark for exoclimate emulation and for the broader regime of low-data, multi-simulator, parameter-to-field regression. The dataset contains approximately 1800 simulations from five GCMs, mapping eight planet parameters to 3D atmospheric fields including temperature, humidity, winds, clouds, and radiation. Three nested subsets define progressively harder challenges: single-simulator regression, multi-simulator regression with complete observations, and multi-simulator regression with structured missingness. We propose two evaluation protocols: one for ranking methods, and one that measures performance relative to the disagreement between GCMs themselves. We evaluate seven baselines spanning simple methods, deep learning, and Gaussian processes. GP-based methods perform best, suggesting that ThousandWorlds exposes a regime where off-the-shelf deep learning does not yet succeed. Data: https://doi.org/10.57967/hf/8695. Code: https://github.com/edstevenson/ThousandWorlds.

</details>


### [80] [TS-Fault: Benchmarking Time Series Forecasters Against Structural Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18539)
*Yuyang Zhao,Lian Xu,Hao Miao,Chenxi Liu,Hao Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-Fault基准，系统评估时间序列预测模型在四种结构化故障场景（观测级/机制级 × 单变量/多变量）下的鲁棒性。在6个数据集上评估21个模型，发现：干净数据准确性与鲁棒性负相关；观测级故障下排名不变但机制级故障下排名重排；基础模型干净精度最高但最脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测模型评估仅依赖干净数据上的单一指标（如平均误差），假设其能预测部署可靠性。但真实故障并非独立同分布噪声，而是具有时间形状、变量依赖破坏、机制变化伴随缺失、因果传播等结构化事件。需要将鲁棒性测试从通用噪声敏感性提升到针对模型实际依赖结构的显式故障注入。

Method: 将故障模式组织为两个正交维度（观测级vs机制级；单变量vs多变量）的四种模式，通过统一重要性分数将故障注入预测最关键的窗口。采用成对干净/损坏协议，在6个数据集、4种故障模式、5个难度级别上评估21个模型。

Result: 三项关键发现：（1）干净数据准确性与鲁棒性呈反相关；（2）观测级故障下排名保持不变，但机制级故障下排名完全重排；（3）所有灾难性失败均发生在机制级故障下，基础模型干净精度最高但最脆弱。

Conclusion: TS-Fault揭示了传统排行榜直觉的误导性，表明仅靠干净数据性能无法反映部署可靠性，机制级故障是模型崩溃的真正挑战，需要将结构化鲁棒性纳入模型评估标准。

Abstract: Time series forecasting (TSF) underpins consequential decisions in energy, transportation, finance, and healthcare, yet TSF models are almost universally ranked by a single number (e.g., average error) on clean held-out data, under the implicit assumption that it predicts deployed reliability. However, real faults are not i.i.d noise but structured events with temporal shape, broken cross-variable dependencies, regime change coupled with missingness, and causal propagation across a sensing pipeline. Treating TSF robustness as a data-quality problem, we present TS-Fault, a benchmark that evaluates forecasting models under explicit, parameterized fault scenarios with controllable semantic difficulty. TS-Fault organizes recurring failures into four modes along two orthogonal axes (observation- vs mechanism-level; univariate vs multivariate) and injects each fault into the most prediction-critical window via a unified importance score. This design enables robustness to be tested against the structures models actually rely on, rather than reduced to generic noise sensitivity. We evaluate 21 models across 6 datasets, 4 modes, and 5 difficulty levels under a paired clean/corrupt protocol. The results reveal three findings that contradict common leaderboard intuition: (i) clean-data accuracy anti-correlates with robustness; (ii) clean rankings are preserved under observation-level faults but reshuffled under mechanism-level faults; and (iii) all catastrophic failures occur under mechanism-level faults, with foundation models achieving the highest clean-data accuracy yet exhibiting the greatest fragility. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Ray-zyy/TS-Fault.

</details>


### [81] [Do Time Series Foundation Model Benchmarks Hide Regime-Dependent Failures? Evidence from Traffic Speed Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18367)
*Yingshuo Wang,Xian Sun,Lingdong Kong,Wei Gao,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan,Zexin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于交通状态分层的评估方法，揭示时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在交通速度预测中，当状态在自由流和拥堵之间切换时，准确率和预测区间覆盖率严重下降，而聚合指标掩盖了这一问题。作者进一步提出双峰混合增强（BMA）方法，结合历史分布知识来缓解过渡状态下的预测失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准基准测试使用聚合指标评估TSFMs，但这些指标可能掩盖模型在关键运行状态下的严重失败，尤其是在交通状态急剧切换的过渡阶段。

Method: 提出状态分层评估方法，将交通状态分为自由流、拥堵和过渡状态；对比三种TSFMs与历史条件基线；提出双峰混合增强（BMA），一种结合TSFM预测和历史分布知识的后处理方法。

Result: 过渡状态下TSFMs的MAE达到11 mph（整体仅3 mph），90%预测区间的经验覆盖率低至55%；简单历史条件基线在过渡状态覆盖率上优于所有TSFMs但整体准确率差；BMA方法在保持TSFM准确性的同时，接近历史基线的过渡覆盖率。

Conclusion: TSFM基准测试应纳入状态感知的评估方法，以揭示聚合指标所隐藏的失败模式。

Abstract: Standard benchmarks evaluate time series foundation models (TSFMs) using aggregate metrics, but these can mask severe failures in critical operating regimes. We introduce regime-stratified evaluation and apply it to three TSFMs on two standard traffic speed benchmarks. Traffic exhibits abrupt regime switching between free-flow and congested states, producing bimodal speed distributions during transitions. When we stratify by traffic regime, both accuracy and prediction-interval coverage degrade sharply during transitions: transition-regime MAE reaches 11 mph (versus 3 mph overall), and empirical coverage of 90% prediction intervals drops as low as 55%. These failures are invisible in aggregate metrics because free-flow observations dominate the sample. A simple historical conditional baseline (sampling from per-sensor training distributions) achieves better transition coverage than any TSFM, but has far worse overall accuracy. We propose bimodal mixture augmentation (BMA), a post-hoc method that combines TSFM forecasts with historical distributional knowledge, approaching the historical baseline's transition coverage while preserving the TSFM's accuracy. Our results suggest that TSFM benchmarks should incorporate regime-aware evaluation to surface failures that aggregate metrics hide.

</details>


### [82] [Online Distributional Prediction via Latent Cluster Geometry Under Drift and Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18778)
*Navyansh Mahla,Prateek Chanda,Ganesh Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于潜聚类几何的在线分布预测方法，通过变维后验采样（可逆跳MCMC）和重启式准贝叶斯更新，在非平稳流式数据（含漂移和对抗性污染）中实现Wasserstein-1遗憾次线性界，无需参数化假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线非平稳流式学习中，现有方法多聚焦于点估计的追踪，但许多应用需要预测完整的数据生成分布。同时，流式数据面临漂移（分布随时间变化）和对抗性污染（异常值攻击）的双重挑战，亟需一种无需参数化假设、能结构化表达不确定性并具有理论保证的在线分布预测方法。

Method: 1) 将每个候选分布表示为潜聚类几何（变尺寸的中心配置组织概率质量，诱导预测分布）；2) 构造Gibbs拟后验分布，通过后验平均进行在线预测；3) 使用可逆跳MCMC对变维后验进行采样；4) 针对漂移问题，提出重启式变体，在时间上局部化同一准贝叶斯更新。

Result: 推导出高概率遗憾界，分解为三个项：PAC-Bayesian复杂度项、污染敏感的拟后验扰动项、以及由A_T^OT=∑ W_2^2驱动的动态最优传输项。在有限支撑、稳定潜几何、预测映射正则性、预言可实现性、局部化重启窗口、次线性传输作用量和次线性污染预算条件下，重启式预测器实现次线性累积Wasserstein遗憾。

Conclusion: 该方法无需对数据流、漂移机制或污染过程做任何参数化假设，即可在非平稳流式分布预测中达到次线性Wasserstein遗憾，兼顾了结构化的潜空间表达与理论可证性。

Abstract: Online learning in non-stationary streams is often formulated as tracking a point estimate, but many applications require predicting the full data-generating distribution. We study online distributional prediction under drift and adversarial corruption. Our approach represents each candidate law through a latent cluster geometry: a variable-size configuration of centers that organizes probability mass and induces a predictive distribution. A Gibbs quasi-posterior over these configurations yields an online predictor by posterior averaging, and the resulting variable-dimensional posterior can be sampled with reversible-jump MCMC. The method therefore avoids specifying a parametric streaming law while retaining a structured latent space for uncertainty, regularization, and comparison.
  We evaluate performance by cumulative Wasserstein-1 regret against the time-varying true law. The analysis separates two effects: corruption perturbs the loss-based posterior update, whereas drift makes long-horizon posterior memory stale. We address the latter with a restarted variant that temporally localizes the same quasi-Bayesian update. The resulting high-probability bounds decompose into a PAC-Bayesian complexity term, a corruption-sensitive posterior perturbation term, and a dynamic optimal-transport term driven by \(A_T^{\mathrm{OT}}=\sum_{t=2}^T W_2^2(p_{t-1}^*,p_t^*)\). Under bounded support, stable latent geometry, predictive-map regularity, oracle realizability, localized restart windows, sublinear transport action, and sublinear corruption budget, the restarted predictor achieves sublinear cumulative Wasserstein regret. These guarantees require no parametric model for the stream, drift mechanism, or corruption process.

</details>


### [83] [From Sparse Features to Trustworthy Proxies: Certifying SAE-Based Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18383)
*Dibyanayan Bandyopadhyay,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事后泛化框架的稀疏自编码器（SAE）解释忠实性认证方法，通过代理风险、重构差距、概念池失配和稀疏复杂度四个可测量量来界定基础模型期望风险的上界，并在GPT-2 Small、Gemma-2B和Llama-3-8B上验证了该界的非平凡性。实验表明深层更易认证，特征打乱消融可区分语义对齐与统计稀疏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE被广泛用于从语言模型中提取可解释特征，但核心问题在于：基于SAE的解释何时能被视为底层冻结语言模型的忠实视图？现有方法缺乏系统性的忠实性认证机制。

Method: 提出事后泛化认证框架：用预训练SAE重构替换原生隐藏激活构建稀疏代理模型，推导基础模型期望风险的上界（由代理风险、SAE重构差距、概念池失配、稀疏复杂度构成），将该上界作为解释忠实性的操作性判据。

Result: 在GPT-2 Small、Gemma-2B、Llama-3-8B上，该界在实用样本量下非平凡。Llama-3-8B逐层分析显示强烈深度依赖，深层更易认证（局部保真度高、下游误差放大弱）。特征打乱表明该分解能区分语义对齐与统计稀疏性。

Conclusion: 该框架为SAE解释的忠实性提供了可量化的认证标准，揭示了层间差异对解释可靠性的影响，并为诊断SAE解释何时不可靠提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are increasingly used to extract interpretable features from language models (LMs), yet a central question remains: when can an SAE-based explanation be treated as a faithful view of an underlying frozen LM We study this through a post-hoc generalization framework that certifies the LM via a sparse proxy, obtained by replacing a native hidden activation with its pretrained SAE reconstruction. Our framework derives an upper bound on the base model's expected risk using four measurable quantities: proxy risk, SAE reconstruction gap, concept-pool mismatch, and sparse complexity. We interpret this certificate as an operational criterion for explanatory faithfulness. In particular, a non-vacuous bound indicates that the extracted sparse features retain meaningful predictive information, while small reconstruction and mismatch errors indicate that the proxy remains behaviorally close to the original model. Empirically, we show that the bound becomes non-vacuous on GPT-2 Small, Gemma-2B, and Llama-3-8B at practical sample sizes. A detailed layerwise analysis of Llama-3-8B reveals a strong depth dependence, with later layers becoming much easier to certify, associated with both stronger local fidelity and weaker downstream error amplification. Finally, through feature-shuffling ablations, we show that the decomposition distinguishes genuine semantic alignment from mere statistical sparsity, providing a useful diagnostic for when SAE-based explanations become less reliable.

</details>


### [84] [Strategic Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18867)
*Jivat Neet Kaur,Pratik Patil,Divya Shanmugam,Emma Pierson,Michael I. Jordan,Nika Haghtalab,Meena Jagadeesan,Ahmed Alaa,Serena Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在算法预测影响资源分配的高风险场景（如医疗保健）中，通过特征选择与岭正则化结合来应对策略性操纵问题。主要发现是仅基于可操纵性排除特征通常次优，并提供了特征子集在最优正则化下性能的细粒度刻画，进而开发了联合选择特征集与正则化参数的实用算法。通过医疗支付基准案例验证了算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险领域（如医疗保健）中，算法预测结果会影响资源分配，而输入特征可能被策略性操纵。现有方法通常重新设计预测器来显式应对策略交互，但实际中决策者往往受限于调整现有预测流程中的粗粒度杠杆（如特征选择）。因此，亟需在特征选择与正则化相结合的约束下研究策略分类问题，为实际政策设计提供理论指导。

Method: 1) 对策略分类问题中特征选择与岭正则化的交互进行形式化建模；2) 理论刻画特征子集在最优正则化下的性能表现；3) 开发联合优化特征集与岭正则化参数的实用算法；4) 在医疗支付基准数据集上进行案例研究验证。

Result: 仅基于特征可操纵性排除特征通常是次优的；特征子集在最优正则化下的性能可以被细粒度刻画；提出的联合优化算法在真实医疗支付数据集上能够有效指导粗粒度政策杠杆的设计。

Conclusion: 提供了一个原则性且实用的框架，用于在算法决策系统中通过特征选择与正则化相结合的方式缓解策略性操纵行为的影响，为实际政策设计提供了理论依据和可操作工具。

Abstract: When algorithmic predictors inform resource allocation in high-stakes domains such as healthcare, these predictors must account for strategic manipulation of input features. The typical solution is to redesign the predictor itself to explicitly account for strategic interactions. In practice, however, decision makers are often constrained to adjusting coarser levers within existing prediction pipelines. For example, healthcare organizations often select which features to exclude based on perceived manipulability, while using standard regularization procedures to shrink the coefficients of retained features. In this work, we initiate a formal study of strategic classification through feature selection and its interaction with ridge regularization. Our main finding is that excluding individual features based on their manipulability alone is generally suboptimal. We provide a fine-grained characterization of the performance of a feature subset under optimal regularization, yielding new insights for policy design. Motivated by this characterization, we develop a practical algorithm for jointly choosing the feature set and the level of ridge regularization. Through a real-world case study on a healthcare payments benchmark, we illustrate how our algorithm can guide the design of coarse policy levers in practice. Our results provide a principled, practical framework for mitigating the effects of strategic behavior in algorithmic decision-making systems.

</details>


### [85] [SCOPE-FL: A Strategy-proof Chain-based Optimal pareto efficient Federated Learning System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18384)
*Seyed Salar Ghazi,Kaiwen Zhang,Mehdi feizi,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCOPE-FL框架，将分层联邦学习中的客户端选择问题建模为双边学校选择问题，通过Top Trading Cycle算法同时保证帕累托效率与策略证明性，并利用区块链智能合约实现可信执行环境。实验表明其在模型精度、收敛速度和奖励效率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HFL客户端选择机制存在策略性低效：优先考虑稳定性而牺牲帕累托效率导致资源分配次优；缺乏策略证明性导致参与者有动机虚报真实偏好，两者共同降低了系统在帕累托意义上的整体福利。

Method: 提出SCOPE-FL同步HFL框架：(1)将客户端选择建模为双边学校选择问题，采用Top Trading Cycle(TTC)算法求解，同时保证帕累托效率(PE)和策略证明性(SP)；(2)采用基于One-Round Reconstruction(OR)的可扩展Shapley值近似进行奖励分配，确保贡献与补偿成正比；(3)整个机制通过区块链智能合约执行，提供防篡改环境以保障SP在实际中成立。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和CIFAR-10上的综合评估表明，SCOPE-FL在模型精度、收敛速度和奖励效率上均优于DA、IAS等现有方法，通信延迟与DA相当，区块链接口开销在规模扩展时显著低于DA。

Conclusion: SCOPE-FL通过将经济学中的TTC机制引入联邦学习客户端选择，同时实现了帕累托效率和策略证明性，结合区块链保障了可信执行，为HFL中的激励兼容问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Hierarchical Federated Learning (HFL) enables scalable collaborative model training across distributed devices while preserving data privacy. However, existing HFL client selection mechanisms suffer from a fundamental strategic inefficiency. By prioritizing stability over Pareto efficiency (PE), they produce suboptimal resource allocations, and without strategy proofness (SP), participants are incentivized to misrepresent their true preferences, both failures degrading system overall welfare in the Pareto sense in practice. To address it, we propose SCOPE-FL (Strategy-proof Chain-based Optimal pareto efficient Federated Learning), a synchronous HFL framework that formulates client selection as a two-sided school choice problem solved through the Top Trading Cycle (TTC) algorithm that simultaneously guarantees PE and SP. For reward distribution, SCOPE-FL employs a scalable Shapley value approximation based on One-Round Reconstruction (OR), ensuring compensation proportional to each client's contribution. The entire mechanism executes via blockchain smart contracts, providing the tamper-proof environment required for the SP guarantees to hold in practice. A comprehensive evaluation on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10 demonstrates that SCOPE-FL outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, including DA, IAS, and other methods across model accuracy, convergence rate, and reward efficiency, while achieving communication latency comparable to DA and blockchain overhead significantly lower than DA at scale.

</details>


### [86] [Smoothness-Based Derandomization of PAC-Bayes Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19105)
*Alexandre Lemire Paquin,Brahim Chaib-Draa,Philippe Giguère*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于PAC-Bayes去随机化技术，利用损失函数和平滑预测器类的光滑性，推导出确定性预测器的高概率泛化界。通过将Gibbs预测器转化为后验均值处的确定性预测器，其代价由Jensen间隙类的泛化差距控制，并通过Rademacher复杂度进行约束，得到涉及参数Jacobian和Hessian的平坦性量。方法适用于有界/无界光滑损失，并针对线性预测器和光滑神经网络给出特例。理论中的Jacobian和Hessian量启发了一种实用正则化器，在CIFAR-10上实验验证了其在不同batch size下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的PAC-Bayes边界通常针对随机Gibbs预测器，而实际部署中通常使用确定性预测器。本文旨在利用损失函数和预测器类的光滑性，推导确定性预测器的高概率泛化界，弥补随机预测器与确定性预测器之间的理论空白。

Method: 1) 将Gibbs预测器到后验均值确定性预测器的转化代价形式化为Jensen间隙类的泛化差距；2) 通过Rademacher复杂度控制该类；3) 推导出涉及参数Jacobian和Hessian的平坦性量的泛化界；4) 将框架推广到有界/无界光滑损失、线性预测器和光滑神经网络；5) 基于理论中的Jacobian和Hessian设计实用正则化器，对BatchNorm网络通过折叠BatchNorm变换计算正则化项。

Result: 建立了从Gibbs预测器到后验均值确定性预测器的精确转化代价，得到了依赖参数Jacobian和Hessian的平坦性量的高概率泛化界。在CIFAR-10上的实验展示了所提正则化器在不同batch size下的行为。

Conclusion: 本文为光滑损失函数下的确定性预测器提供了基于PAC-Bayes去随机化的理论框架，理论导出的平坦性正则化器在实际任务中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: We study PAC-Bayes derandomization for smooth loss functions. Our goal is to obtain generalization bounds that hold with high probability for deterministic predictors by exploiting smoothness properties of both the loss and the predictor class. We show that passing from the Gibbs predictor to the deterministic predictor at the posterior mean has a precise cost, given by the generalization gap of the Jensen gap class. We control this class through its Rademacher complexity, leading to bounds for deterministic predictors that involve flatness quantities expressed in terms of parameter Jacobians and Hessians of the score map. The framework applies to both bounded and unbounded smooth loss functions, and we specialize the results to linear predictors and smooth neural networks. Finally, the Jacobian and Hessian quantities appearing in the theory motivate a practical regularizer. For BatchNorm networks, we compute this regularizer with respect to effective BatchNorm weights obtained by folding the BatchNorm transformation into the adjacent affine weights. Experiments on CIFAR-10 illustrate the behavior of this regularizer under different batch sizes.

</details>


### [87] [LLMZero: Discovering Adaptive Training Strategies for RL Post-Training via LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18388)
*Haoyang Fang,Wei Zhu,Boran Han,Alex Zhang,Zhenyu Pan,Shuo Yang,Shuai Zhang,Jiading Gai,Peng Tang,Cuixiong Hu,Xuan Zhu,Huzefa Rangwala,George Karypis,Bernie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现RL后训练策略中，容量参数单调累积而正则化参数振荡变化，并提出LLMZero系统通过树搜索自动发现多阶段训练策略，在4个GRPO任务上相比基线提升9%-140%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定调度策略将所有参数绑定在固定轨迹上，无法表达正则化需要跟踪的非平稳探索-利用权衡，因此需要可操作的阶段性训练设计规则。

Method: 提出LLMZero系统，利用LLM代理通过树搜索遍历训练轨迹，在每个检查点诊断病理并提出协调的多参数转换策略。

Result: 在4个不同GRPO任务上，LLMZero发现的策略相比基础模型提升9%-140%，相比网格搜索提升6%-15%，一致优于随机搜索和基于技能的代理。

Conclusion: 容量参数与正则化参数的不同动态模式是跨任务可迁移的结构性原理，解释了不同任务中策略形式不同但参数动态相似的现象，为多阶段训练提供了可操作的设计规则。

Abstract: RL post-training strategies are dataset-dependent and reveal a recurring empirical pattern: capacity parameters accumulate monotonically across stages, while regularization parameters predominantly oscillate in response to shifting training dynamics. This distinction matters because fixed schedules commit all parameters to fixed trajectories and therefore cannot express the non-stationary exploration-exploitation tradeoffs that regularization must track; the principle provides actionable design rules for multi-stage training. We discover this through LLMZero, a system where LLM agents search over training trajectories via tree search, diagnosing pathologies at each checkpoint and proposing coordinated multi-parameter transitions. Across 4 diverse GRPO tasks, LLMZero discovers strategies that improve over the base model by 9% to 140% relative and over grid search by 6% to 15% relative, consistently outperforming random search and the skill-based agent. The structural principle transfers across tasks, providing an explanation for why discovered strategies take qualitatively different forms yet share similar parameter dynamics.

</details>


### [88] [INDEQS: Informed Neural controlled Differential EQuationS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19138)
*Michael Detzel,Gabriel Nobis,Kristiyan Blagov,Juri Schubert,Jackie Ma,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出INDEQS，一种在神经受控微分方程（NCDE）框架中融入有向图先验知识的图基时间序列预测方法。通过在架构的不同位置（内部混合与外部混合）注入图信息，INDEQS可自适应地利用已知图结构或从数据中学习额外连接。设计了连续平流模拟以系统研究图信息对预测的影响，并在河流水位预测和交通流预测两个真实任务上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准图基NCDE扩展通常完全从数据中学习空间结构，忽略了已知有向图先验信息。本文旨在探索如何将已知图结构有效融入NCDE框架，并系统研究图在不同架构位置的有用性。

Method: INDEQS将图信息注入在架构的两个不同位置：(1) 内部混合（inner mixing）——在隐藏状态的图节点间混合时施加图约束；(2) 外部混合（outer mixing）——在向量场与控制信号之间交互时施加图约束。提供轻量级图约束变体和更具表达力的自适应图卷积变体，后者可从数据中学习额外连接。同时设计了基于连续平流模拟的合成数据集生成流程。

Result: 在合成平流模拟和真实世界基准（河流水位预测、PeMS08交通流预测）上，外部图信息持续提升预测精度（降低MAE），尤其在大规模图上更为显著；内部图信息则在严格遵循已知邻接关系时提供更参数高效的替代方案。连续解码器相比离散卷积解码器在真实任务上具有更好的精度和时间灵活性。

Conclusion: INDEQS有效融合了有向图先验知识与NCDE框架，外部图信息的注入是提升预测性能的关键策略；连续时间解码器在实际应用中优于离散解码器。

Abstract: Neural Controlled Differential Equations (NCDE) provide a powerful continuous-time framework for forecasting time series, but standard graph-based extensions typically learn spatial structure purely from data, even in settings where a directed graph structure is known a priori. We introduce Informed Neural controlled Differential EQuationS (INDEQS), a graph-based NCDE forecasting method that incorporates prior knowledge of a directed graph at distinct architectural positions. INDEQS separates inner mixing of hidden states across graph nodes from outer mixing between vector field and control, and offers both a lightweight graph-constrained variant and a more expressive variant, learning additional graph connections from data via adaptive graph convolutions. To systematically study when graph informedness is beneficial in forecasting, we devise a continuous advection simulation on directed graphs, yielding synthetic spatio-temporal datasets with known ground-truth flow structure. We then evaluate INDEQS on two real-world tasks: river discharge forecasting on a hydrological network and traffic flow prediction on PeMS08. Across these synthetic and real-world benchmarks, outer informedness consistently improves mean absolute error over an uninformed NCDE with comparable parameter count, particularly on larger graphs, while inner informedness offers a more parameter-efficient alternative when strict adherence to a known adjacency is desired. A comparison of discrete convolutional and continuous-time decoders further shows that continuous decoders yield better accuracy and greater temporal flexibility on real-world tasks. An implementation of INDEQS and the advection simulation is available at https://github.com/Mitchi1/indeqs.

</details>


### [89] [MOLAR: Learning Multimodal Molecular Representations from Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18390)
*Yingxu Wang,Kunyu Zhang,Nan Yin,Yu Li,Eran Segal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOLAR是一个针对分子多模态表示学习的噪声感知框架，通过分离潜在干净属性推断与记录标签观测，有效处理标签噪声问题，在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子属性预测中，标签噪声普遍存在（来自实验测定、数据库或弱标注流程），现有多模态分子表示学习（如图-文本融合或对齐）可能放大标签错误在模态间的传播，导致模型记忆错误标注并学习误导性分子证据。

Method: MOLAR将干净属性推断与记录标签观测分离：图视图和文本视图为干净属性分布贡献残差证据，通过分类标签观测通道将该分布映射到记录标签进行训练，从而推导后验标签可靠性和模态特异性分子证据。

Result: 在自然噪声分子基准和受控标签翻转基准上，MOLAR一致优于代表性基线方法；可视化分析表明MOLAR能提供可解释的可靠性诊断和模态证据分析。

Conclusion: MOLAR通过显式建模标签噪声传播机制，有效提升多模态分子表示学习在噪声标签场景下的鲁棒性和可解释性。

Abstract: Motivation: Noisy labels are a common challenge in molecular property prediction because molecular annotations are often obtained from assays, curated databases, or weak annotation pipelines rather than directly observed clean biological states. Treating recorded labels as reliable supervision can cause models to memorize corrupted observations and learn misleading molecular evidence. In multimodal molecular representation learning, this issue can be amplified by graph-text fusion or alignment, which may propagate label-induced errors across modalities. Results: We propose MOLAR, a noise-aware framework for learning multimodal molecular representations from noisy labels. MOLAR separates latent clean-property inference from recorded-label observation: graph and text views contribute residual evidence to a clean-property distribution, and a categorical label-observation channel maps this distribution to recorded labels for training. This formulation derives posterior label reliability and modality-specific molecular evidence from the model. Experiments on naturally noisy molecular benchmarks and controlled label-flipping benchmarks show that MOLAR consistently outperforms representative baselines. Visualization analyses further show that MOLAR provides interpretable reliability and modality-evidence diagnostics.

</details>


### [90] [P$^2$CE: Model-Agnostic Plausible Pareto-Optimal Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18418)
*Arthur Hendricks Mendes de Oliveira,Giovani Valdrighi,Marcos Medeiros Raimundo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出P²CE算法，利用隔离森林异常检测器和SHAP值生成帕累托最优的反事实解释，在可行性、合理性和计算效率之间取得平衡，实验证明其在三个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法难以同时平衡可行性、合理性和计算效率，亟需一种能提供多样化最优权衡方案的高效算法。

Method: P²CE算法结合隔离森林异常检测器确保解释符合数据分布，并利用SHAP值加速计算，生成帕累托最优的反事实解释集合，为用户提供不同可行性概念之间的最优权衡。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，P²CE在解质量和计算效率方面均优于相关对比技术。

Conclusion: P²CE能够高效生成可行且合理的多样化反事实解释，为机器学习公平性和透明性提供实用工具。

Abstract: The increasing use of machine learning algorithms in social applications has raised concerns about fairness and transparency, leading to the development of counterfactual explanations. These explanations supports individuals to understand and potentially alter unfavorable decisions in areas such as loan applications, job selections, and more, by providing actionable changes to input features that would lead to a desired outcome. Existing methods often struggle to balance feasibility, plausibility, and computational efficiency. To address this, we introduce P$^2$CE, an algorithm for generating plausible Pareto-optimal counterfactual explanations, offering users a diverse set of optimal trade-offs between different notions of feasibility. P$^2$CE employs an auxiliary isolation forest outlier detector to ensure that explanations are in accordance with the data distribution and leverages SHAP values to obtain optimal results with short computing times, regardless of the underlying model. Our algorithm was empirically evaluated on three datasets, demonstrating superior performance in terms of both solution quality and computational efficiency compared to related techniques.

</details>


### [91] [A Human-in-the-Loop Bayesian Optimization Framework for Constraint-Aware Bioprocess Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19230)
*Samuel Stricker,Claus Wirnsperger,Alessandro Butté,Laura Helleckes,Gonzalo Guillén Gosálbez,Antonio del Rio Chanona,Mehmet Mercangöz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的帕累托前沿引导采样（PFGS）框架，这是一个人在环贝叶斯优化方法，通过将高斯过程代理模型派生的量重构为多目标优化问题的目标，并将帕累托前沿暴露给领域专家进行交互式候选选择。扩展包括：约束优化（将满足输出规格限制的后验概率作为显式帕累托目标）和鲁棒优化（通过蒙特卡洛采样策略估计输入扰动下的预期下限性能）。该框架在八维补料分批CHO细胞培养模拟器上进行了展示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的贝叶斯优化通常返回单一自动化推荐，缺乏与领域专家的交互；同时，现有方法难以同时处理约束满足、模型不确定性和输入鲁棒性等多维度权衡。作者旨在开发一个可交互的框架，让专家能够在性能、不确定性、约束满足和鲁棒性之间进行可视化的多目标权衡决策。

Method: 在PFGS框架基础上进行双向扩展：(1) 约束优化：将满足输出规格限制的后验概率（从GP后验分布解析计算）作为显式的帕累托目标；(2) 鲁棒优化：采用蒙特卡洛采样策略，在用户定义的输入扰动空间上估计预期下限性能。最终形成多维帕累托表示，通过交互式仪表盘的二维投影展示性能、不确定性、约束满足概率和鲁棒性之间的权衡。

Result: 在八维补料分批CHO细胞培养模拟器上，该框架系统地识别出了高性能、满足可行性约束且对扰动具有鲁棒性的操作条件，并展示了专家定义的需求如何提供原则性停止准则并支持实验资源的合理分配。

Conclusion: 该扩展框架使得专家能够在贝叶斯优化过程中通过交互式多目标可视化逐步细化选择标准，有效融合了自动化优化与人类专业知识，为复杂工业过程优化提供了一种实用的工具。

Abstract: This work presents an extension to Pareto Front Guided Sampling (PFGS), a Human-in-the-Loop (HitL) Bayesian Optimization (BO) framework in which Gaussian process (GP) surrogate-derived quantities are reformulated as objectives of a multi-objective optimization problem, and the resulting Pareto front is exposed to a domain expert for interactive candidate selection rather than returning a single automated recommendation. The framework is extended in two directions: constrained optimization is addressed by incorporating the posterior probability of satisfying output specification limits as an explicit Pareto objective, computed analytically from the GP posterior distribution; robust optimization is addressed by a Monte Carlo sampling strategy that estimates expected lower-confidence performance over a user-defined variability of input perturbations, capturing performance degradation under likely implementation deviations. The resulting multi-dimensional Pareto representation renders trade-offs between predicted performance, model uncertainty, probabilistic constraint satisfaction, and input robustness simultaneously visible through pairwise two-dimensional projections on an interactive dashboard, enabling selection criteria to be iteratively refined as the surrogate model improves and development objectives evolve. The framework is showcased on an eight-dimensional fed-batch Chinese Hamster Ovary (CHO) cell culture simulator demonstrating systematic identification of high-performing, feasibility-compliant, and perturbation-resilient operating conditions, and illustrating how expert-defined requirements provide a principled stopping criterion and support informed allocation of experimental resources.

</details>


### [92] [Signature filtering: a lightweight enhancement for statistical watermark detection in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18430)
*Chih-Duo Hong,Yen-Pang Chen,Fang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出签名过滤（Signature Filtering）模块，通过学习少量“签名”标记并在检测前移除它们，在不改变水印嵌入和生成过程的前提下，显著提升LLM文本水印检测的鲁棒性，尤其在弱信号、低熵和文本扰动场景下效果突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印检测器在弱水印信号、重复文本或水印被编辑时表现不佳，亟需一种不修改水印嵌入和生成流程、可即插即用的检测增强方法。

Method: 提出签名过滤（Signature Filtering）模块：学习一小部分“签名”标记（通过混合整数线性规划求解），这些标记的存在会干扰水印测试，检测前将其移除；推导了多种攻击模型下的有限样本和渐近界。

Result: 在4种水印族（Kgw, Sweet, Unigram, Exp）、4个语料库（C4, MBPP, HumanEval, Code-Search-Net）和6个LLM上，2-gram和3-gram签名过滤将弱信号和低熵场景的检测率从8-31%提升至78-99%，假阳性可控。在25-50% token扰动的压力测试中，2-gram过滤保持大部分检测增益，匹敌甚至超过先进WinMax检测器。

Conclusion: 签名过滤是一种简单、可扩展、模型无关的水印检测增强模块，可有效强化LLM文本水印溯源检查，适用于信息处理流程。

Abstract: Statistical watermarks help organizations attribute large language model (LLM) outputs, yet existing detectors often struggle when watermark signals are weak, texts are repetitive, or watermarks are edited. We propose signature filtering, a detection-time module that enhances watermark detection without modifying watermark embedding and text generation. It learns a small set of ``signature'' tokens whose presence makes watermark tests unreliable, and removes these tokens before detection. The signatures are obtained by solving a mixed-integer linear program on a small training set, with constraints that maximize the true positive rate. We additionally derive finite-sample and asymptotic bounds under several attacker models (color-blind, color-adaptive, and distributionally correlated). On four well-known watermark families (Kgw, Sweet, Unigram, Exp), four benchmark corpora (C4, MBPP, HumanEval, Code-Search-Net), and six LLMs (Opt-1.3b, Opt-6.7b, Llama2-13b, Llama3.1-8b, Qwen2.5-14b, Phi-3-medium-14b), 2- and 3-gram signatures raise detection rates in weak-signal and low-entropy settings from 8~31% without filtering to 78~99% with filtering, while keeping false positives controllable and often negligible. In stress tests where we scramble sentences and perturb 25~50% of tokens by dilution, deletions, and substitutions, 2-gram filters for Kgw-style watermarks preserve most of the clean-text detection gains, often matching or outperforming the advanced WinMax watermark detector. Signature filtering thus provides a simple, scalable, and model-agnostic add-on to strengthen watermark-based provenance checks for LLM text in information processing workflows.

</details>


### [93] [Beyond Prediction: Tail-Aware Scheduling for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18431)
*Yueying Li,Yuanfan Chen,Jiayang Chen,Esha Choukse,Haoran Qiu,G. Edward Suh,Rodrigo Fonseca,Ziv Scully,Udit Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需预测、分布感知的LLM服务调度框架，通过轻量级统计信号的软优先级提升和缓存感知抢占机制，替代传统的SJF/SRPT预测驱动策略。在多种工作负载下，相比具有完美长度知识的SRPT，该方法将P99 TTLT降低35-50%，TTFT降低34-47%，显著优化了尾部延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM调度器多采用基于预测的SJF/SRPT策略（利用预测的解码长度或排名），但这些策略在分布偏移、突发到达和GPU内存压力下表现脆弱，且对主导用户体验的尾部延迟（P90-P99）控制能力有限。

Method: 提出一种无需显式长度预测的分布感知调度框架，核心包括：1) 用轻量级统计信号驱动的软优先级提升替代长度预测；2) 协同优化调度与缓存感知的抢占机制，以应对内存耦合的解码动态。

Result: 在生产和开源轨迹上评估，相比具有完美长度知识的SRPT，P99 TTLT降低35-50%，TTFT降低34-47%，涵盖推理密集型、聊天密集型等多样工作负载。

Conclusion: 该工作为在线LLM服务的尾部延迟优化提供了一种鲁棒的替代方案，证明无需精确长度预测即可实现优于完美预测策略的延迟性能。

Abstract: LLM serving exhibits extreme length variability, making size-based scheduling difficult in practice. Recent LLM schedulers approximate SJF/SRPT using predicted decode lengths or ranks and primarily report mean-centric metrics such as TTFT and TBT. We show that these prediction-driven policies can be fragile under distribution shifts, bursty arrivals, and GPU memory pressure, while offering limited control over the tail latency (P90-P99) that dominates user experience, even with perfect decode-length knowledge. We introduce a distribution-aware, prediction-free scheduling framework that replaces explicit length prediction with soft priority boosting driven by lightweight statistical signals. Our design co-optimizes scheduling and cache-aware preemption to account for memory-coupled decode dynamics across workload mixes. Evaluated on production and open-source traces, our method reduces P99 TTLT by up to 35-50% relative to SRPT with perfect length knowledge and reduces TTFT by 34-47% across workloads, including reasoning-heavy and chat-heavy tasks. These results demonstrate a robust alternative for optimizing tail latency in online LLM serving.

</details>


### [94] [TMR-GGNN: Credit Card Fraud Detection based on Time-Aware Multi-Relational Guided Graph Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18444)
*Rohit Tewari,Shubhankar Shilpi,Navin Chhibber,Devendra Singh Parmar,Sunil Khemka,Piyush Ranjan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TMR GGNN框架，通过时间感知的多关系图神经网络、时间感知注意力机制、对比学习模块以及InfoNCE+Focal Loss复合损失函数，解决信用卡欺诈检测中的数据不平衡、欺诈模式演变和实体关系复杂等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用卡欺诈检测面临数据高度不平衡、欺诈模式不断演变以及交易实体间存在复杂关系结构三大挑战，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: (1) 扩展编码器-解码器GNN架构，建模客户、商户、设备、IP间跨时间窗口的异构交互；(2) 构建动态多关系图，引入时间感知关系注意力机制，根据时间邻近性和语义上下文自适应加权交易相关性；(3) 解码器采用对比学习模块区分真实与合成交易模式；(4) 提出InfoNCE对比损失与Focal Loss结合的复合损失函数，处理类别不平衡并增强判别学习。

Result: 提升了欺诈识别的准确性，同时有效减少了假阴性（漏报）情况。

Conclusion: TMR GGNN通过融合时间信息、多关系图结构和对比学习，显著提升了不平衡数据场景下信用卡欺诈检测的性能。

Abstract: In recent years, credit card fraud detection has faced significant challenges due to highly imbalanced data, evolving fraud patterns, and complex relational structures among transaction entities. To address these issues, this research proposes a novel framework called Timeaware Multi Relational Guided Graph Neural Network (TMR GGNN). Particularly, the proposed TMR GGNN extends the encoder decoder Graph Neural Network GNN architecture by modeling heterogeneous interactions across customers, merchants, devices, and IPs over temporal windows. Subsequently, the proposed TMR GGNN approach constructs a dynamic, multi relational graph and incorporates a time aware relational attention mechanism within the encoder to adaptively weigh the transaction relevance based on temporal proximity and semantic context. Consequently, the decoder employs a contrastive learning module to distinguish between real and synthesized transaction patterns, while improving the models generalization of rare fraud cases. Additionally, to effectively manage severe class imbalances and emphasize discriminative learning, a composite loss function combining Information Noise Contrastive Estimation (InfoNCE) based contrastive loss with Focal Loss is introduced. This integration assists in improving fraud identification while mitigating false negatives.

</details>


### [95] [A Cross-Model VLM-Judge Protocol for Single-Image 3D Mesh Quality (and Why Cheap Proxies Fall Short)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18451)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种基于VLM的3D生成质量评估协议，证明其可靠性，同时揭示几何有效性和CLIP相似度等廉价代理指标无法替代该协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图转3D生成器发展迅速，但缺乏公认的、无需人工干预的自动化质量评估方法。现有廉价代理指标（渲染空间CLIP相似度、网格几何有效性统计）与感知质量之间的关系尚未被验证。

Method: 1) 提出可重复的VLM评估协议：固定24视角无头渲染管线、两个独立VLM判别器家族、强制位置偏差校正（查询两种呈现顺序，仅保留顺序一致的判定）。2) 以该协议为基准，评估几何有效性指标和CLIP相似度等廉价代理的表现。

Result: 1) 两个VLM判别器家族间一致性良好（Cohen's kappa=0.66），远高于随机水平。2) 几何有效性仅为弱信号（具有双峰分布特征），低于预注册目标；渲染CLIP表现与随机水平无异。3) 学习型Bradley-Terry头退化为单一流形统计量（给渲染CLIP负权重），与仅用几何指标完全一致。

Conclusion: 建议在测试条件下（两个前馈生成器、Google Scanned Objects数据集、面片退化机制）采用VLM评估协议作为可靠、可重复的评估工具，并反对将几何/CLIP代理用作优化目标。

Abstract: Single-image-to-3D generators are improving quickly, but there is no agreed, human-free way to tell whether one generated mesh is better than another. Practitioners commonly rely on cheap automatic proxies (render-space CLIP similarity and mesh geometry-validity statistics), yet how well these track perceived quality is unestablished. We make two contributions. First, we propose and validate a reproducible VLM-judge evaluation protocol: a fixed 24-view headless render rig, two independent vision-language judge families, and a mandatory position-bias correction that queries both presentation orders and keeps only order-consistent verdicts. The two judge families agree substantially with each other (Cohen's kappa = 0.66), well above the chance-agreement floor. Second, using this protocol as the reference, we show the cheap proxies do not substitute for it. Geometry validity is only a weak signal on average (because, as we show, it is bimodal) and stays below our pre-registered target, while render-CLIP is at chance. A learned Bradley-Terry head collapses onto a single manifoldness statistic (giving render-CLIP a negative weight) and matches geometry-only exactly, so learning the feature weights buys nothing. The proxy is also bimodal: it is significantly above chance on contrasts with visible geometric defects but at chance on ambiguous contrasts, consistent with geometry validity tracking the judge only when the defect is visually salient. We therefore recommend the VLM-judge protocol as a reliable, reproducible evaluator under the conditions tested (two feed-forward generators on Google Scanned Objects, with a face-drop degradation regime) and advise against geometry/CLIP proxies as optimization targets.

</details>


### [96] [Veriphi: Attack-Guided Neural Network Verification with Dataset-Dependent Training Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18454)
*Pratik Deshmukh,Kartik Arya,Vasili Savin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Veriphi是一个GPU加速的神经网络验证系统，结合快速对抗攻击与α,β-CROWN形式化边界认证方法。实验表明训练方法有效性依赖数据集：IBP在MNIST上达78%认证准确率，但在CIFAR-10上几乎无效；PGD对抗训练在小扰动下达94%认证准确率。系统通过攻击引导的假证实现5倍加速，可扩展至1.058亿参数的生产级模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"认证训练普遍优于对抗训练"的假设，证明验证策略的选择高度依赖于具体应用场景和数据集特性，而非存在通用最优方法。

Method: 将快速对抗攻击与α,β-CROWN形式化边界认证相结合，实现GPU加速的神经网络验证；采用攻击引导的假证策略加速验证过程；在MNIST和CIFAR-10上对比标准训练、对抗训练和认证训练三种方法。

Result: IBP在简单数据集MNIST上达78%认证准确率，但在复杂数据集CIFAR-10上几乎无认证效果；PGD对抗训练在CIFAR-10小扰动下达94%认证准确率；系统实现5倍验证加速；可扩展到1.058亿参数的生产模型。

Conclusion: 训练方法有效性具有数据集依赖性，认证训练并非在所有场景下优于对抗训练，验证策略选择需结合具体上下文。

Abstract: We present Veriphi, a GPU-accelerated neural network verification system that combines fast adversarial attacks with formal bound certification using alpha,beta-CROWN methods. Through systematic experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 using three training methodologies (standard, adversarial, certified), we demonstrate that training method effectiveness is fundamentally dataset-dependent. Interval Bound Propagation (IBP) achieves 78% certified accuracy on simple MNIST (784 dimensions) but provides negligible certification performance on the more complex CIFAR-10 dataset, where PGD adversarial training dominates with 94% certification at small perturbations. We achieve 5x verification speedup through attack-guided falsification and scale our approach to production-size models (105.8M parameters) for real-world aerospace logistics optimization. Our results challenge the assumption that certified training universally outperforms adversarial training, showing context matters critically for verification strategy selection.

</details>


### [97] [Task-Restricted Symmetries in Recurrent Weight Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18457)
*Simon Dräger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用有序实Schur坐标分析单层tanh RNN权重空间的冗余性，发现不同任务和训练解中，Schur耦合的可删除性存在差异，揭示了近似的功能不变性而非通用对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环网络的权重空间存在大量功能冗余：改变循环矩阵可能对任务分布上的输入-输出 rollout 影响很小，而类似尺度的变化却可能破坏相同的行为。作者希望系统研究这种冗余性，识别哪些结构化扰动能保留训练好的循环解，哪些会破坏其计算。

Method: 采用有序实Schur坐标将循环矩阵分解为谱块和有向非正规耦合，在不改变输入和读出映射的前提下进行结构化消融实验。在固定长度的复制任务、触发器、正弦生成和上下文依赖整合等任务上进行分析。

Result: 在固定长度复制任务中，部分非正规Schur耦合可以移除而性能损失很小，另一些耦合则对自主回放至关重要。不同任务和训练解之间，保留损失的消融轮廓存在差异。

Conclusion: 这些结果识别出的是近似的功能不变性，而非循环权重空间的通用对称性。Schur坐标消融为判断哪些结构化扰动能保留训练解的 computations 提供了实用诊断工具。

Abstract: Recurrent networks can contain substantial functional redundancy in
  weight space: changing a recurrent matrix may leave the input-output
  rollout nearly unchanged on a task distribution, while similar-scale
  changes can destroy the same behavior. We study this redundancy in
  one-layer tanh RNNs using ordered real Schur coordinates. The Schur
  form separates spectral blocks from directed nonnormal couplings,
  giving a diagnostic basis for structured ablations that keep the input
  and readout maps fixed. In a fixed-length copy task, selected
  nonnormal Schur couplings can be removed with little loss in some
  trained solutions, whereas other couplings are necessary for accurate
  autonomous replay. Across flip-flop, sine generation, and
  context-dependent integration, the loss-preserving ablation profile
  varies across tasks and trained solutions. These results identify
  candidate approximate functional invariances, not universal symmetries
  of recurrent weight space. Schur-coordinate ablations provide a
  practical diagnostic for which structured perturbations preserve a
  trained recurrent solution and which ones disrupt its computation.

</details>


### [98] [What Does the Weight Norm Control in Grokking? Logit-Scale Mediation under Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18465)
*Truong Xuan Khanh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了"Grokking"（从记忆到泛化的延迟跳跃）中，权重范数实际上通过控制logit尺度（输出温度）来影响softmax饱和程度，进而调节grokking延迟；权重范数本身只是上游操控手柄，而非直接变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Grokking现象中权重范数究竟控制什么，澄清其作用机制是否直接还是通过其他中间变量间接影响泛化延迟。

Method: 通过固定权重范数（钳制）并改变输出温度来滑动grokking延迟；在范数和温度网格上分析延迟与logit尺度的相关性；使用交叉熵和均方误差对比；进行softmax崩溃审计和无LayerNorm的transformer消融实验。

Result: logit尺度单一变量可解释97%的grokking延迟变化（R²=0.97），权重范数仅额外贡献1-2%；交叉熵下有效logit尺度回退到基线可恢复约85%的延迟；MSE下logit尺度被固定，范数通过不同路径起作用。

Conclusion: Grokking延迟的直接近端变量是logit尺度及其驱动的softmax饱和程度，权重范数仅是一个上游操控手柄，并非直接作用因素。

Abstract: Grokking, the delayed jump from memorization to generalization, is usually tied to the weight norm: a smaller norm generalizes sooner. We ask what the norm actually controls. Holding the weight norm fixed by clamping and varying only an output temperature, we slide the grokking delay across its entire norm-induced range under cross-entropy; matching the effective logit scale back to baseline recovers about 85% of the delay at two moduli. Across a grid of norms and temperatures the delay collapses onto the logit scale alone (R2 = 0.97), with the norm adding 1-2% beyond it. The effect is loss-dependent: under mean-squared error the logit scale is pinned and the norm acts through a different route. A memorization control, a float64 softmax-collapse audit, and a no-LayerNorm transformer point to the same channel. Forking arms from one identical state, the delay follows the held norm value and not the clamp operation, which closes a rescaling-artifact concern. The proximal variable is the logit scale and the softmax saturation it drives; the weight norm is only an upstream handle. All numbers, tables, and figures reproduce from released code and data.

</details>


### [99] [Structured Representation Learning with Locally Linear Embeddings and Adaptive Feature Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18469)
*Somjit Nath,Jackson J Cone,Derek Nowrouzezahrai,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 受神经科学启发的强化学习框架，通过局部线性嵌入（LLE）解耦动态特性和奖励特性，并利用注意力机制自适应融合这些表征，在基准任务上提升了学习效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经科学研究表明大脑通过结构化低维流形和自适应门控机制实现高效决策，作者希望将这些神经科学原理引入强化学习，以改进传统RL方法在处理复杂环境时的学习效率和性能。

Method: 1) 利用局部线性嵌入（LLE）捕获环境的内在局部线性结构；2) 通过标准RL目标推导奖励特定特征；3) 引入注意力机制（类比皮层门控）在每个状态上自适应融合这两类互补的表征。

Result: 在基准任务上，该方法相比传统RL方法提升了学习效率和整体性能，验证了显式建模局部状态结构和自适应特征选择的有效性。

Conclusion: 将神经科学中的结构化低维表征和自适应信息整合机制引入RL是有效的，未来可进一步探索更丰富的神经计算原理与RL算法的融合。

Abstract: Neuroscientific research has revealed that the brain encodes complex behaviors by leveraging structured, low-dimensional manifolds and dynamically fusing multiple sources of information through adaptive gating mechanisms. Inspired by these principles, we propose a novel reinforcement learning (RL) framework that encourages the disentanglement of dynamics-specific and reward-specific features, drawing direct parallels to how neural circuits separate and integrate information for efficient decision-making. Our approach leverages locally linear embeddings (LLEs) to capture the intrinsic, locally linear structure inherent in many environments, mirroring the local smoothness observed in neural population activity, while concurrently deriving reward-specific features through the standard RL objective. An attention mechanism, analogous to cortical gating, adaptively fuses these complementary representations on a per-state basis. Experimental results on benchmark tasks demonstrate that our method, grounded in neuroscientific principles, improves learning efficiency and overall performance compared to conventional RL approaches, highlighting the benefits of explicitly modeling local state structures and adaptive feature selection as observed in biological systems.

</details>


### [100] [The Illusion of Improvement: Reject Inference Strategies in Credit Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18479)
*Bruno Scarone,Ricardo Baeza-Yates*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了信用评分中拒绝推断方法的效果，发现存在结构性失效模式：模型准确率提升但召回率崩溃，造成改进假象，实际拒绝质量却在下降。提出一种无需统计假设的受控探索策略，通过少量批准被拒申请者打破反馈循环，并证明准确率与拒绝质量在是否探索上给出相反建议。即使2-5%的探索率也能以近乎零成本诊断问题严重性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拒绝推断方法被广泛用于缓解信用评分中的生存偏差，但其有效性尚不明确。标准评估指标在选择性偏差下具有误导性，现有方法存在导致错误决策的反馈循环问题。

Method: 系统评估多种拒绝推断方法，识别其结构性失效模式；提出受控探索策略，通过故意批准部分被拒申请者并观察真实结果来打破反馈循环，无需统计假设。

Result: 准确率与拒绝质量在是否探索上给出相反建议；即使2-5%的极低探索率也能有效诊断反馈循环的严重程度；该发现跨两种机器学习方法和三个真实数据集保持一致。

Conclusion: 标准评估协议不足以评估在生存偏差下训练的模型；受控探索策略能以极小成本有效缓解反馈循环问题。

Abstract: Reject inference methods are widely used to mitigate survival bias in credit scoring, yet their effectiveness remains poorly understood. We systematically evaluate several such methods and uncover a structural failure mode: in a natural retraining cycle, models whose accuracy improves while recall collapses create an illusion of improvement that leads practitioners to believe the system is getting better when, in fact, its rejection quality -- the ability to correctly screen out defaulters -- is deteriorating. We then propose a controlled exploration strategy that breaks the feedback loop without statistical assumptions: the lender deliberately approves a fraction of rejected applicants and observes their true outcomes. We show that accuracy and rejection quality give opposite recommendations on whether to explore: accuracy favors no exploration, while rejection quality improves with it, confirming that standard evaluation metrics are misleading under selection bias. Even minimal exploration rates (2--5\%) prove sufficient in our experiments to diagnose the severity of the feedback loop at near-zero cost. Our findings are consistent across two machine learning methods and three real-world datasets, and suggest that standard evaluation protocols are inadequate for assessing models trained under survival bias.

</details>


### [101] [SFT Overtraining Predicts Rank Inversion via Entropy Collapse Under RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18487)
*Siddharth Aphale,Kelly Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SFT深度过大会压缩 rollout 分布，导致 GRPO 训练失效（pass@10 从 0.806 降至 0.481），而预 RL 熵与 GRPO 结果正相关（ρ=+0.69），简单的 KL 正则化和标签平滑无法挽救。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准启发式选择 pass@1 最高的 SFT 检查点进行 GRPO 可能会失败，因为 SFT 压缩了 rollout 分布，导致组内优势方差消失，模型无法从组相对信号中学习。

Method: 在 Qwen2.5-Coder-3B（5 深度×3 种子）和 DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B（4 深度×3 种子）上构建 SFT 深度阶梯，分析预 RL pass@1、熵与 GRPO 结果的关系；提出两步诊断法（预 RL 熵分诊 + 早期 GRPO 熵监控）；测试 KL 正则化和标签平滑变体。

Result: Qwen 上预 RL pass@1 随 SFT 深度上升，但 GRPO pass@10 从 0.806 降至 0.481；预 RL 熵与 GRPO 结果正相关（ρ=+0.69）。DeepSeek 上 pass@1 远高于 p*(8)=0.083，GRPO 结果压缩而非反转。简单 KL 正则化和标签平滑无法挽救。

Conclusion: SFT 深度过深会因熵压缩导致 GRPO 失效，预 RL 熵可作为有效的诊断指标；该失效不是简单的 GRPO 超参数伪影，需要更根本的解决方案。

Abstract: The standard heuristic of selecting the SFT checkpoint with the highest pass@1 for GRPO can fail when SFT compresses the rollout distribution. For binary rewards, the expected within group advantage variance is $p(1{-}p)(g{-}1)/g$; when early GRPO drives $p$ below $p^*(g)$, most groups have identical rewards and provide no group relative signal. We study SFT depth ladders for Qwen2.5-Coder-3B and DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B. We test Qwen2.5-Coder-3B across five depths and three seeds, and DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B across four matched depths and three seeds. On Qwen, pre RL pass@1 rises with SFT depth, but peak GRPO pass@10 falls from $0.806$ to $0.481$ (3 seed mean, $n{=}20$); pre RL entropy is positively associated with the GRPO outcome ($ρ{=}{+}0.69$). On DeepSeek, pass@1 remains far above $p^*(8){=}0.083$, and GRPO outcomes compress rather than invert. A two stage diagnostic, combining pre RL entropy triage with an early GRPO entropy monitor, flags high risk checkpoints and can stop failing runs early. Simple KL to reference regularisation and label smoothing variants do not rescue the collapsed Qwen checkpoint in our setting, suggesting the failure is not a trivial GRPO hyperparameter artefact.

</details>


### [102] [Beyond AHI: An Interpretable Causal-Discovery-Guided Framework for Sleep Recovery in Connected Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18506)
*Saba A. Farahani,Elahe Khatibi,Manoj Vishwanath,Amir M. Rahmani,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果发现的可解释分层框架，用于从多模态多导睡眠图（PSG）数据中推导睡眠恢复评分（SRS）。该框架通过有向无环图（DAG）学习识别呼吸负担、缺氧负担、睡眠碎片化、睡眠架构和自主神经调节五个生理域，并结合生理约束与LLM辅助审计去除混淆变量。在两个大规模队列（MESA n=1540; MrOS n=825）中验证，SRS与患者感知恢复的关联强度是传统AHI的2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PSG客观睡眠评估指标（如AHI）对患者报告结局（如嗜睡、疲劳）的解释力有限，无法充分反映多域生理学对功能恢复的影响。亟需一种可解释、因果驱动的框架来桥接多模态睡眠生理与患者中心结局。

Method: 采用因果发现引导的两阶段筛选流程：首先通过有向无环图（DAG）学习从多模态PSG中识别候选生理驱动因素（呼吸负担、缺氧负担、睡眠碎片化、睡眠架构、自主神经调节）；然后结合生理约束与受限的LLM辅助审计，去除结构混杂变量和构念重叠变量，建立分层睡眠恢复评分（SRS）。

Result: 在两个大型人群队列（MESA n=1540; MrOS n=825）中，五个生理域被确认为与恢复相关的重复性生理域。SRS与患者感知恢复的对齐强度比传统AHI高出2.5倍。

Conclusion: 通过可解释、偏倚感知、领域结构化的框架，将多模态睡眠生理与患者中心结局相链接，为临床睡眠研究和新兴智能健康场景中的恢复建模提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Objective sleep assessment relies on polysomnography (PSG), yet clinical impact is often better reflected in patient-reported outcomes (PROs) such as sleepiness and fatigue. Existing summary indices, including the Apnea-Hypopnea Index (AHI), provide limited insight into the multidomain physiology underlying functional recovery. We propose an interpretable, causal-discovery--guided framework for deriving a hierarchical Sleep Recovery Score (SRS) from multimodal PSG. Using two large population cohorts (MESA: n=1540; MrOS: n=825), we apply directed acyclic graph (DAG) learning to identify candidate physiological drivers spanning respiratory burden, hypoxic burden, sleep fragmentation, sleep architecture, and autonomic regulation. Although derived from clinical PSG, these domains map naturally to sensing streams increasingly available in connected health technologies, including wearable ECG, oximetry, and sleep-stage estimation devices. To preserve mechanistic plausibility, we introduce a two-stage screening process that combines physiology-based constraints with constrained LLM-assisted auditing to identify and remove structural confounders and construct-overlapping variables. Across cohorts, these five domains emerge as recurrent physiological domains associated with recovery, and the resulting SRS shows up to 2.5$\times$ stronger alignment with perceived recovery than AHI. By linking multimodal sleep physiology to patient-centered outcomes through an interpretable, bias-aware, and domain structured framework, this work provides a practical foundation for recovery modeling across both clinical sleep studies and emerging smart and connected health settings.

</details>


### [103] [PSyGenTAB: A Privacy-Preserving Framework for Synthetic Clinical Tabular Data Generation via Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18518)
*Arshia Ilaty,Hossein Shirazi,Manasi Chitale,Kedar Hegde,Dhanalakshmi Ramesh,Rashmi S. Manjunath,Amir Rahmani,Hajar Homayouni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PSyGenTAB是一个隐私保护生成框架，通过将合成医疗数据生成形式化为约束优化问题（使用增广拉格朗日方法求解），在模型训练中嵌入可配置的隐私约束，实现了隐私保护与临床效用的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗AI发展受限于高质量临床数据获取困难，原因在于机构数据孤岛和严格的隐私法规（如HIPAA和GDPR）。现有合成数据方法缺乏明确机制来管理隐私-效用权衡，要么破坏临床有意义的数据模式，要么面临患者重识别风险。

Method: 将合成医疗数据生成形式化为约束优化问题，采用增广拉格朗日方法（Augmented Lagrangian Method）求解，在模型训练中嵌入可配置的隐私约束，强制执行最低隐私阈值同时最大化临床数据效用。

Result: 在多个临床基准测试中，PSyGenTAB保留了特征间临床关系和少数类诊断模式。通过Train-on-Synthetic/Test-on-Real和Train-on-Real/Test-on-Synthetic两种下游评估协议，合成数据训练的模型性能与真实数据相当。隐私审计显示精确记录重现有显著减少，对成员推断攻击具有强韧性。

Conclusion: PSyGenTAB建立了一个原则性的框架来平衡合成医疗数据中的隐私保护和临床效用，支持安全的跨机构AI开发。

Abstract: The development of medical AI is constrained by limited access to high-quality clinical data due to institutional silos and strict privacy regulations such as HIPAA and GDPR. Synthetic data generation offers a potential solution, but existing methods lack principled mechanisms to explicitly manage the privacy-utility trade-off, often degrading clinically meaningful patterns or risking patient re-identification. We present PSyGenTAB, a privacy-preserving generative framework that formulates synthetic healthcare data generation as a constrained optimization problem solved using the Augmented Lagrangian Method. By embedding configurable privacy constraints directly into model training, PSyGenTAB enforces minimum privacy thresholds while maximizing clinical data utility. Across multiple clinically motivated benchmarks, PSyGenTAB preserves inter-feature clinical relationships and minority-class diagnostic patterns essential for reliable health AI. Downstream evaluation using Train-on-Synthetic, Test-on-Real and Train-on-Real, Test-on-Synthetic protocols shows that models trained on synthetic data achieve performance comparable to those trained on real patient records. Privacy auditing further demonstrates reduced exact record reproduction and strong resilience to membership inference attacks. These results establish PSyGenTAB as a principled framework for balancing privacy protection and clinical utility in synthetic healthcare data, supporting secure cross-institutional AI development.

</details>


### [104] [Sparsity Curse: Understanding RLVR Model Parameter Space from Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18521)
*Chenrui Wu,Zexi Li,Jiajun Bu,Jiangchuan Liu,Haishuai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了RLVR模型合并中的“稀疏性诅咒”：尽管RLVR参数更新高度稀疏，但这些更新在参数空间中分散更广，形成近似正交的捷径，导致标准合并方法失效。为此提出SAR-Merging方法，通过Fisher信息敏感度仲裁、幅度感知稀疏化和重缩放来解决冲突，在数学和编程基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索RLVR模型是否因其稀疏的参数更新特性而更易于进行模型合并，以提供一种可扩展、免训练的方式来聚合独立训练的RLVR模型的多样化推理能力。

Method: 提出SAR-Merging方法：(1) 基于Fisher信息的敏感度仲裁解决重叠更新区域中的参数冲突；(2) 幅度感知的稀疏化和重缩放以保护脆弱的推理路径。通过系统性的更新几何实证分析，对比RLVR与SFT的参数空间结构差异。

Result: RLVR模型的参数更新分散更广且近似正交，标准合并方法导致严重退化；SAR-Merging在数学和编程基准上显著优于现有合并方法，支持单任务增强和多能力融合。

Conclusion: RLVR模型存在“稀疏性诅咒”，其稀疏更新在参数空间中的分散性和正交性使得聚合天然脆弱，SAR-Merging为这一独特结构提供了有效的合并方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Reward (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful post-training paradigm that surpasses Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) in eliciting reasoning intelligence and resisting catastrophic forgetting. Recent studies further reveal that RLVR induces highly sparse and off-principal parameter updates compared to SFT. This naturally raises the question: does such sparsity make RLVR models more amenable to model merging? If so, model merging would offer a scalable, training-free path to aggregate diverse reasoning capabilities from independently trained RLVR models. Surprisingly, we find the opposite, uncovering a sparsity curse: the sparse RLVR updates are spread farther apart in parameter space, forming near-orthogonal shortcuts that make aggregation inherently fragile. This is likely rooted in the stochasticity of RL optimization and the diversity of emergent reasoning patterns. Unlike SFT models that converge to shared, flat basins and merge naturally, RLVR models suffer severe degradation under standard merging methods. Through systematic empirical analysis of the update geometry, we characterize the mechanisms behind this failure and propose Sensitivity-aware Resolving Merging (SAR-Merging), a merging recipe tailored for the unique structure of RLVR parameter spaces. SAR-Merging resolves conflicts in overlapping update regions via Fisher Information-based sensitivity arbitration, followed by magnitude-aware sparsification and rescaling to preserve fragile reasoning pathways. Experiments on mathematical and coding benchmarks demonstrate that SAR-Merging substantially outperforms existing merging methods on RLVR models, enabling both single-task enhancement and multi-capability fusion.

</details>


### [105] [On the Residual Scaling of Looped Transformers: Stability and Transferability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18524)
*Shaowen Wang,Bingrui Li,Ge Zhang,Wenhao Huang,Shen Yan,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 循环（权重共享）Transformer通过共享残差块重复N次来增加有效深度，本文发现传统的$1/\sqrt{N}$缩放不足，需要$1/N$缩放，并推导了多层块的分解参数化$\varepsilon = \lambda/(N\sqrt{L})$，使得学习率仅依赖于独特层数L而非循环次数N，实现超参数在N上的直接迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环（权重共享）Transformer中，权重共享导致残差更新在不同迭代间产生相关性，传统的深度缩放分析（如$1/\sqrt{L}$）不足以应对这种相关性，需要更强的缩放规则来保证训练稳定性。

Method: 理论推导表明循环架构需要$\varepsilon = 1/N$缩放；对于多层块（L层独特层循环N次），提出分解参数化$\varepsilon = \lambda/(N\sqrt{L})$，其中$1/N$控制层内循环相关性，$1/\sqrt{L}$控制跨层方差。

Result: 实验验证$1/N$缩放相比$1/\sqrt{N}$缩放能提升可训练性并获得更优的损失值；最优学习率仅依赖于独特层数L，可在不同N之间直接迁移而不需重新调参。

Conclusion: 循环Transformer需要更强的$1/N$缩放规则，分解参数化有效分离了循环深度和网络深度的影响，实现了超参数从少循环到多循环的无缝迁移。

Abstract: Looped (weight-tied) Transformers apply a shared residual block $N$ times ($h \leftarrow h + \varepsilon\,f(h)$, same $f$ at each step), increasing effective depth without adding parameters. Prior depth-scaling analyses prescribe $\varepsilon = 1/\!\sqrt{L}$ for depth-$L$ residual networks. We show that this is insufficient for looped architectures: weight sharing makes residual updates correlated across iterations, requiring the stronger scaling $\varepsilon = 1/N$. For multi-layer blocks ($L$ unique layers looped $N$ times), we derive a factored parameterization $\varepsilon = λ/(N\!\sqrt{L})$ that separates the two sources of growth: $1/N$ controls the within-layer loop correlation, and $1/\!\sqrt{L}$ controls the across-layer variance. A key consequence is that the optimal learning rate depends only on the number of unique layers $L$, not on the loop count $N$, enabling direct hyperparameter transfer from small to large $N$ without retuning. Experiments on looped Transformers confirm that $1/N$ scaling improves trainability and yields better loss than $1/\!\sqrt{N}$ scaling across loop counts.

</details>


### [106] [Hierarchical Attention via Domain Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18525)
*Stephan Köhler,Oliver Rheinbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于两层重叠Schwarz区域分解的层次化注意力机制，用于有限维算子学习。该方法用局部低秩注意力块（重叠子域）和粗尺度注意力块替代全局低秩注意力，在一维扩散问题的数值实验中表现出训练更快、精度更高且参数更少的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受到两层Schwarz区域分解方法结合局部子域修正与粗尺度全局通信的启发，作者旨在设计一种能同时捕捉局部和全局信息的注意力架构，克服全局低秩注意力在参数效率和训练速度方面的局限。

Method: 提出一个两层加性注意力算子：$$M_θ^{-1} = ΦQ_0 K_0^T Φ^T + \sum_{i=1}^{N} R_i^T D_i^{1/2} Q_i K_i^T D_i^{1/2} R_i$$，其中$R_i$为重叠子域限制算子，$D_i$为单位分解权重，$Φ$为粗网格插值矩阵。局部低秩注意力块处理子域信息，粗注意力块传递全局信息。

Result: 针对合成傅里叶右端项的一维扩散问题，数值实验表明：相比全局低秩注意力基线，域分解注意力算子训练更快，近似精度更高，且使用的参数显著减少。

Conclusion: 基于重叠Schwarz区域分解的两层注意力机制在算子学习中是有效的，能够在减少参数的同时提升训练效率与精度，为设计更高效的神经算子架构提供了新思路。

Abstract: We propose a hierarchical attention mechanism based on two-level overlapping Schwarz domain decomposition. The method is motivated by the observation that two-level Schwarz domain decomposition methods combine local subdomain corrections with a coarse level that communicates global, long-range information. We test its usefulness in the context of finite-dimensional operator learning using a simple, one-dimensional diffusion problem with homogeneous Dirichlet boundary conditions. Although elementary, this problem provides a controlled sequence-to-sequence setting in which the exact nonlocal solution operator is known. After discretization, learning the solution operator amounts to approximating the inverse of a symmetric positive definite matrix. As a baseline, we use a global softmax-free low-rank attention operator of the form $QK^T$. The proposed construction replaces this dense global factorization by a two-level additive structure: local low-rank attention blocks on overlapping subdomains are combined with a coarse attention block. The resulting operator has the form $$M_θ^{-1}
  =
  ΦQ_0 K_0^T Φ^T
  +
  \sum_{i=1}^{N}
  R_i^T D_i^{1/2} Q_i K_i^T D_i^{1/2} R_i.$$ Here $R_i$ restricts to an overlapping subdomain, $D_i$ is a partition-of-unity weight, and $Φ$ is a coarse interpolation (or prolongation) matrix. Numerical experiments for synthetic Fourier right-hand sides indicate that the domain-decomposition attention operator is able to train faster and can give more accurate approximations than a global low-rank attention baseline while using significantly fewer parameters.

</details>


### [107] [Do as the Romans Do: Learning Universal Behaviors from Heterogeneous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18537)
*Caleb Chang,Davin Win Kyi,Natasha Jaques,Karen Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRID是一种社会学习方法，能从追求不同目标的异构示范群体中提取通用奖励函数，训练出掌握环境通用能力（如安全性和基本任务熟练度）的通才智能体，并作为下游任务微调的优秀先验，在多个实验中优于标准示范学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类常通过观察他人来学习新技能，但从异构群体中观察到的行为包含冲突信号，难以判断哪些行为值得模仿。现有标准示范学习方法存在模式平均偏差，无法有效从异构示范中提取通用有用行为。

Method: GRID将每个智能体的奖励函数分解为通用奖励（捕捉所有智能体共享的行为）和特定奖励（捕捉个体偏好和目标）。仅基于通用奖励进行训练，实现通才预训练的新范式。

Result: 在合成基函数分解、多智能体Craftax和连续自动驾驶模拟器（Highway-Env）上的实验验证了GRID能在语义上有意义地分离奖励结构，优于标准示范学习基线，并实现更高效和稳定的特化。

Conclusion: GRID成功从异构示范中提取通用行为，避免了模式平均偏差，通才智能体可作为下游任务微调的优秀先验，包括训练中未见过的偏好。

Abstract: Humans often acquire new skills by observing others, since observed behaviors implicitly reveal how to act in an environment. However, observations drawn from a heterogeneous population introduce conflicting behavioral signals, making it difficult to determine which behaviors are worth imitating. We address this challenge with General Reward Inference and Disentanglement (GRID), a social learning method that extracts universally useful behaviors from a heterogeneous population of demonstrators pursuing different goals. GRID decomposes per-agent reward functions into a general reward, capturing behaviors shared across all agents, and specific rewards, capturing individual preferences and objectives. Training exclusively on the general reward provides a new paradigm of generalist pretraining. It yields a generalist agent that internalizes universal environmental competencies, such as safety and basic task proficiency, without the mode-averaging bias that afflicts standard learning from demonstration techniques. This generalist serves as a superior prior for fine-tuning to downstream tasks, including preferences unseen during training. Experiments across a synthetic basis function decomposition, multi-agent Craftax, and a continuous autonomous driving simulator (Highway-Env) confirm that GRID successfully disentangles reward structure in a semantically meaningful way, outperforms standard learning from demonstration baselines, and enables more efficient and stable specialization.

</details>


### [108] [Correcting Sensor-Induced Distribution Drift with Wasserstein Adversarial Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18561)
*Saraa Ali,Vladimir Bocharnikov,Fedor Ratnikov,Mikhail Hushchyn,Artem Ryzhikov,Denis Derkach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Wasserstein-GAN的无监督方法，通过生成器学习可解释的物理变换参数，将有变化的探测器响应分布映射回参考分布，从而校准传感器运动和老化带来的数据退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器运动和老化会降低数据质量及下游数据驱动方法的性能，而直接标注退化参数通常不可得，因此需要一种无监督的校准方法。

Method: 采用Wasserstein-GAN架构，生成器作为可学习的校准变换函数，其可训练权重即为待求的物理变换参数；判别器（批评者）通过Wasserstein目标提供分布距离信号进行对抗训练。

Result: 在跟踪探测器玩具模型（可控层移位）和高粒度Geant4模拟量能器数据（逐单元老化效应）上验证，恢复的老化系数与真实值高度相关，校准后的能量和分布与参考分布一致性提升，且在通道噪声增大时性能合理退化。

Conclusion: 对抗分布匹配可作为无退化参数标签场景下数据驱动校准策略的有效组成部分。

Abstract: The quality of recorded data depends on the stability of the sensor system that acquires it. Sensor motion and aging can degrade the performance and stability of downstream data-driven methods. We present a Wasserstein-GAN-inspired approach for unsupervised inference of physically interpretable transformation parameters that map a changed detector response distribution back to a nominal reference distribution. In contrast to standard generative modeling, the generator is used as a learnable calibration transformation whose trainable weights represent the sought parameters, while the critic provides a distributional distance signal via the Wasserstein objective. We validate the approach on a tracking-detector toy model with controlled layer shifts and demonstrate its application on high-granularity Geant4-simulated calorimeter data with cell-wise aging effects. The method recovers aging coefficients for individual cells with correlation to ground truth and improves agreement between calibrated and reference energy-sum distributions, while exhibiting the expected degradation at increasing channel-to-channel noise levels. These results indicate that adversarial distribution matching can serve as a data-driven component of calibration strategies in settings where direct labels for degradation parameters are unavailable.

</details>


### [109] [Fair Cognitive Impairment Detection Through Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18571)
*William Nguyen,Jiali Cheng,Hadi Amiri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多模态框架，通过跨模态融合和梯度反转的对抗性去学习，在MCI分类中超越SOTA，并显著缩小了不同人口统计子群体（性别、语言）之间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCI检测模型常利用与标签相关的人口统计学线索，导致不同子群体间存在较大性能差距，亟需一种既能保持高分类性能又能消除人口统计偏差的方法。

Method: 结合（1）语音、文本、图像三种模态间的跨模态融合，以及（2）基于梯度反转（gradient reversal）的去学习机制，阻止共享嵌入编码与任务无关的人口统计属性。

Result: 在多语言基准TAUKADIAL和PREPARE上，方法在MCI分类中优于SOTA多语言多模态基线，同时显著缩小了性别和语言子群体间的性能差距；跨数据集迁移分析表明去学习有助于学习更鲁棒的表示。

Conclusion: 人口统计去学习结合多模态融合能有效提升MCI检测的公平性和泛化能力，为可扩展的认知障碍筛查提供了更具鲁棒性的解决方案。

Abstract: Mild Cognitive Impairment (MCI) is a medical condition characterized by a noticeable decline in memory, language, or thinking abilities. MCI detection from spontaneous speech is promising for scalable screening. However, learned models often exploit demographic cues correlated with labels, resulting in a large performance gap across subgroups. We present a multimodal framework that combines (i) cross-model fusion between modalities (speech, text, and image), and (ii) unlearning using gradient reversal that discourages the shared embedding from encoding task-irrelevant demographic attributes. Evaluated on the multilingual benchmarks TAUKADIAL and PREPARE, our method outperforms the state-of-the-art multilingual and multimodal baseline in MCI classification while substantially reducing the performance gap across patient subgroups (sex and language). We further analyze transfer across datasets, showing that demographic unlearning helps learn more robust representations for MCI detection.

</details>


### [110] [Towards Anomaly Detection on Relational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18621)
*Shiyuan Li,Yunfeng Zhao,Yue Tan,Qingfeng Chen,Yixin Liu,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RelAD，一种基于重构的关系数据库异常检测框架，通过条件稀疏门控属性重构和双视图多关系边重构分别捕获属性和关系连接异常，在6个基准数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系数据库异常检测尚未被充分探索，现有表格和图异常检测方法难以处理多表属性高维异构导致的稀疏异常线索被淹没的问题，也无法捕捉跨外键关系的异常连接模式。

Method: RelAD采用基于重构的框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 条件稀疏门控属性重构，抑制冗余多表属性并强调异常语义块；2) 双视图多关系边重构，从内在和行为实体画像检测特定关系的异常连接。最终通过轻量融合模块整合属性和关系信号得到异常分数。

Result: 构建了6个包含系统性异常的基准数据集，实验表明RelAD持续优于其他基线方法，同时具有竞争性的效率。

Conclusion: RelAD有效解决了关系数据库异常检测中的关键挑战，为多表异构数据中的属性和关系异常提供了统一的检测框架。

Abstract: Relational databases are widely used for managing structured data in real-world systems. Detecting anomalies from such relational data is crucial for identifying fraud, risks, and abnormal behaviors, yet remains under-explored. The key challenges lie in the intrinsic complexity of relational data: multi-table attributes are high-dimensional and heterogeneous, making sparse abnormal clues easy to overwhelm by normal or irrelevant information; and anomalies may further manifest as abnormal connection patterns across different foreign-key relations, which existing tabular and graph anomaly detection methods are ill-suited to capture. To address them, we propose RelAD, a reconstruction-based framework that captures anomalies from both attribute and relational edge reconstruction. RelAD contains two core modules: conditional sparse-gated attribute reconstruction, which suppresses redundant multi-table attributes and emphasizes abnormal semantic blocks, and dual-view multi-relational edge reconstruction, which detects relation-specific abnormal connections from both intrinsic and behavioral entity profiles. The resulting attribute and relational signals are integrated through a lightweight fusion module to produce the final anomaly score. We further construct 6 benchmark datasets with systematic anomalies, on which extensive experiments show that RelAD consistently outperforms other baselines while achieving competitive efficiency.

</details>


### [111] [scGTN: Deep Siamese Graph Transformer Network for Single-cell RNA Sequencing Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18672)
*Jinke Wu,Yifan Wang,Siyu Yi,Caiyang Yu,Ziyue Qiao,Nan Yin,Jiancheng Lv,Wei Ju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为scGTN的深度孪生图变换网络框架，通过整合基因表达谱和细胞间结构依赖关系，并采用最优传输策略进行自监督聚类，在多个单细胞RNA-seq基准数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有scRNA-seq数据聚类方法忽略了数据的稀疏性和噪声，以及复杂的细胞间结构信息。

Method: 将scRNA-seq数据构建为图，生成两个增广图视图作为双视图以捕获互补的细胞间信息；采用孪生图变换网络整合最短路径信息和节点距离以捕捉丰富结构关系；利用最优传输策略进行自监督聚类。

Result: 在多个基准scRNA-seq数据集上的实验表明，scGTN持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: scGTN通过显式建模细胞间结构依赖关系和自监督最优传输聚类，有效解决了数据稀疏性和噪声问题，提升了细胞聚类性能。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) serves a pivotal role in characterizing gene expression at the cellular level, enabling the identification of cell types and advancing the understanding of cellular heterogeneity. Despite the significant progress in scRNA-seq data clustering, we argue that current methods always ignore the sparsity and noise, as well as the complex intercellular structural information inherent in scRNA-seq data. Toward this end, in this paper, we propose a novel single-cell RNA-seq clustering framework via deep Siamese Graph Transformer Network (termed scGTN), which explicitly integrates gene expression profile and intercellular structural dependencies for cell clustering. In particular, we formulate scRNA-seq data as a graph and construct two augmented graph views that serve as dual views to capture complementary intercellular information. Then, a Siamese graph transformer network is employed to explicitly incorporate shortest-path information and node-wise distances for capturing richer structural relationships between cells. Finally, we employ an optimal transport strategy to guide the cell clustering in a self-supervised manner. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark scRNA-seq datasets demonstrate that our scGTN consistently outperforms existing methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/W-RMSL/scGTN.

</details>


### [112] [PACT: Preserving Anchored Cores in Task-vectors for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18627)
*Ningyuan Shi,Zhipeng Zhou,Hao Wang,Chunyan Miao,Peilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现预训练模型中存在“承重墙维度”（LBW维度），即任务关键知识仍保留在预训练权重中而非完全转移到任务向量中。现有基于任务向量的模型合并方法忽略LBW维度会导致性能下降。作者提出PACT方法，通过对齐任务向量的正交补与预训练权重子空间来保留LBW维度，并将其从任务向量中移除后再合并，显著提升模型合并性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法遵循任务算术范式，假设任务特定知识完全编码在任务向量中，忽略了预训练模型本身固有的任务偏好。作者发现该假设不成立，预训练权重中存在承重墙维度（LBW维度）包含任务关键知识，被现有方法忽视导致任务冲突无法完全解决和性能退化。

Method: 提出PACT方法，从标量权重和子空间两个角度刻画LBW维度。核心思路是保留任务向量中锚定的任务特定核心（LBW维度），通过将任务向量的正交补与预训练权重子空间对齐，然后将对齐的子空间分量从任务向量中移除，再应用现有模型合并算法。还提出了基于随机SVD的高效变体以提升可扩展性。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的大量实验表明，PACT能够持续增强主流模型合并方法，并建立了新的最先进性能。PACT可以无缝集成到现有方法中。

Conclusion: 模型合并不应仅在任务向量空间中进行，需考虑预训练权重中保留的LBW维度。PACT通过保留和对齐LBW维度有效解决了这一问题，为模型合并提供了新的范式改进方向。

Abstract: Model merging has emerged as a training-free alternative to multi-task learning, aiming to combine multiple task-specific fine-tuned models into a single multi-task model. Most existing model merging approaches follow the Task Arithmetic paradigm, which decomposes fine-tuned weights into pre-trained parameters and task vectors, and performs merging exclusively in the task-vector space. The effectiveness of this paradigm implicitly relies on the assumption that task-specific knowledge is encoded solely within task vectors. We argue that this assumption generally does not hold due to the intrinsic task preferences of pre-trained models. Specifically, we identify \textbf{Load-Bearing Wall (LBW) dimensions}, namely some task-critical knowledge that remains embedded in the pre-trained weights rather than being fully transferred into task vectors. We characterize LBW dimensions from both scalar-weight and subspace perspectives, thereby covering the major paradigms of existing model merging methods. Our analysis reveals that, by ignoring LBW dimensions, task-vector-based approaches fail to fully resolve task conflicts and may inadvertently damage task-specific knowledge encoded in the pre-trained model, leading to degradation. To address this issue, we propose PACT, which preserves the anchored task-specific cores (i.e., LBW dimensions) within task vectors by aligning their orthogonal complements with the subspace of the pre-trained weights. These aligned subspace components are then removed from the task vectors before applying existing model merging algorithms. Furthermore, we develop an efficient variant based on randomized SVD to improve scalability. PACT can be seamlessly integrated with existing methods. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that PACT consistently enhances mainstream model merging approaches and establishes new state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [113] [Bounded Context Management for Tabular Foundation Models on Stream Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18677)
*Jinmo Lee,Doyun Choi,Moongi Choi,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对表格数据流学习问题，提出了一种名为CURE的上下文管理策略，通过熵门控准入和冗余感知驱逐来管理上下文窗口，使表格基础模型无需更新模型参数即可适应流式数据分布变化，在七个数据流上取得了最高27%的相对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流学习方法需要不断更新模型状态以适应分布漂移，而表格基础模型（TFMs）通过上下文学习进行预测，这改变了问题本质：从"如何更新模型"转变为"如何管理上下文"。因此需要一个有效的上下文管理策略来平衡信息保留与冗余消除。

Method: 提出了CURE（Context management via Uncertainty-aware admission and Redundancy aware Eviction）策略，包含两个核心组件：(1) 熵门控准入机制，根据预测不确定性决定是否将新样本加入上下文；(2) 冗余感知驱逐机制，在上下文达到上限时移除冗余样本。三个实践要求：保留最近样本、保留不确定样本、移除冗余样本。

Result: 在七个数据流上，CURE相比传统流学习方法取得了最高27.0%的相对性能提升；在多个不同表格基础模型骨干上表现稳健；与其它策略变体相比排名第一。

Conclusion: 将表格基础模型应用于流学习时，通过合理的上下文管理策略（而非更新模型参数）即可有效应对分布漂移，CURE策略为此提供了一个简单有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Tabular stream learning requires predictions on sequentially arriving examples under distribution shift. While standard methods adapt by updating model states, tabular foundation models (TFMs) make predictions conditioned on a labeled context in an in-context manner, making them a natural alternative for stream learning. This shifts the challenge from how to update the model to how to manage the context. We propose a future information view that yields three practical requirements for context management: preserve recent examples, retain uncertain examples, and remove redundant examples. We instantiate these requirements as CURE (Context management via Uncertainty-aware admission and Redundancy aware Eviction), a context-managing policy with entropy-gated admission and redundancy-aware eviction. Across seven streams, CURE shows up to 27.0% relative improvement over classical stream learners, remains robust across multiple TFM backbones, and ranks first among other policy variants. Code and datasets are available at https://github.com/morcellinus/CURE-ICML-FMSD.

</details>


### [114] [MetaboNet-Bench: A Multi-modal Benchmark for Glucose Forecasting in Type 1 Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18640)
*Nathaniel Jeffries,Miriam Wolff,Sam Royston,Elizabeth Healey,Caleb Mayer,David Klonoff,Michael Snyder,Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaboNet-Bench是一个用于1型糖尿病患者多模态血糖预测的标准化开源评估基准，通过整合血糖、胰岛素和碳水化合物数据，公平比较不同预测模型，并发现数据模态的益处取决于模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前血糖预测算法缺乏标准化评估基准，导致公平比较困难，阻碍创新；且多数算法仅依赖CGM数据，忽略了胰岛素和碳水化合物等多模态信号。

Method: 开发了MetaboNet-Bench开源评估框架，整合血糖、胰岛素和碳水化合物多模态数据，对多种血糖预测模型（包括自定义多模态时序模型）进行标准化基准测试。

Result: 数据模态的加入对模型性能的提升取决于模型的复杂度；纳入更多临床指标有助于识别未来研究中需要填补的空白。

Conclusion: MetaboNet-Bench为1型糖尿病血糖预测模型提供了标准化评估平台，揭示了模型复杂度与多模态数据收益之间的关联，并为未来研究方向提供了指引。

Abstract: Glucose forecasting algorithms are an important aspect of glycemic control management in type 1 diabetes. So far, the research community has developed numerous algorithms and models for forecasting. However, it is well-recognized that the lack of standardized model performance evaluation benchmarks makes fair comparison difficult and hinders further innovation, and thus benchmark standardization is in urgent need.
